These counts verify soundness and compatibility; they do not verify fidelity to the complete Mark^A.  
Comparison with Chapter 3: on the amplifying side (A3) of the trace-leaving pair of Theorem 5, D16 and Mark^PS are both 1 (Clause 0); on the side where “the signal merely stops in-boundary shallowing” (B3), both are empty—when the letter arrives no shallowing occurs, so there is no event to certify; removing the letter to rest would switch shallowing on, which is an inadmissible removal, so this removal difference does not serve as source certification in this paper (the no-opening restriction), in agreement with “r differs between the two sides” in Chapter 3. Lemma 12 (PE) falls on true F runs, where D16 and Mark^PS agree position by position. Formation pair B5: D16 records only the out-of-boundary e+, Mark^PS also records the alphabet-gated g, and r is 1 on both sides.

6\. Declaration of the Bounds of Misjudgment  
For each item we give the source of misjudgment, the reason code or bound used, and the theorem relied on.  
E-g1  Insufficient threshold-crossing coverage: finite settings leave ⊥\_Cov in gate-flip regions; Theorem D1 removes this source within 𝒟₁ (both witnesses and infeasibility certificates can be checked). What remains lies only between 𝒟₁ and 𝒟\_m: ⊥\_Cov when 𝒟\_m is the declared domain and Lemmas C, C′ do not apply. The gating margin \|vt\_j − θ\_j\| serves only as a diagnostic, not as a criterion.  
E-g2  Same-beat cancellation: when potentiation and depression cancel within a beat, a and FD are evaluated event by event, each of them is an action, and HDyn is not misjudged because of the cancellation; only criteria that take the net change as their reading (Dep₀ of Chapter 3, firing counts, final-state differences) misjudge. Under the joint reading, a zero net change with nonzero members gives ⊥\_Joint.  
E-g3  Saturation and individuation of clipping: with only the KO read-out, saturated places give ⊥\_Sat (Lemma 3.1(ii)). The individuation of clipping has two readings, separate and joint; each saturated T position–beat is evaluated under both, the value is taken when they agree, and otherwise ⊥\_Ind; the antecedent IND\_CLIP is needed only when they disagree.  
E-g4  Mixed-state witnesses: a LOC set state need not be reachable by the natural dynamics; setting is a test, and positive determination by Lemma 3.2 further requires a path in the actual event graph, so mixed states produce no misjudgment within model mode; when reading back to a material host, the implementability of set states belongs to the implementation fidelity listed in 5.5.  
E-g5  Insufficient discriminating power of a single activity reading: a difference in firing counts under exclusive LOC ⇒ some YIN or RESET event changes ⇒ κ = 1 if there is an actual path; no difference implies no 0 at all. The 43 groups of failure of organizational participation read off in the original scan of the frozen SNN from “no difference in firing counts after erasure” all have Part = 1 under exact adjudication on 𝒟₁ (Appendix B.2, item 2); the misjudgment rate of the negative determinations of that reading on this family is 43/43. Resetting all weights at once is not an exclusive LOC, and its reading does not serve as a certification atom.  
E-g6  Overdetermination (dynamics side): there is a path but no dependence within the declared domain, so at the criterion level FD = 0 (tier (b)); without a declared B\_FD, ⊥\_Over. Instances O1, O2 and the 15 groups with W0 = 1/2 in the frozen SNN grid belong here.  
E-g7  Dependence through branches that did not occur: ⊥\_Open (end of Lemma 3.2).  
E-g8  Relativity of the declared domain: FD\_𝒟₁ = 0 does not exclude FD\_𝒟\_m = 1; a tier (b) 0 has DOM\[𝒟₁\] as antecedent, and if participation at the object level shows only in coordinated multi-beat variation, that 0 is a misjudgment. Bound: this misjudgment does not occur on events to which Lemmas C, C′ apply.  
E-g9  Budget: ⊥\_Cov; no 0 is issued.  
E-g10  Subthreshold reads: without RD, the read dependence of ineffective processes is invisible under threshold truncation and gives ⊥\_Sub (as E23 of Chapter 3); with RD it can be adjudicated (Theorem C4′, under NS\_act).  
E-g11  Outside the class: multiplicative plasticity, negative entry vectors, lateral inhibition, stochastic gating, λ \< 0 and ρ \> 1 lie outside the working domain; under multiplicative laws the branches of Theorem D1 are no longer affine within a branch, and the monotonicity of Lemma C may fail; no verdict is given.  
E-g12  Trajectory divergence caused by truncation in F encodings: when an F encoding is used for comparison, components with \|c\| ≤ ε are truncated while the host accumulates them, so trajectories, available positions and retention diverge; bound: comparison is made only under NS\_act (5.7).  
E-s1  Source overdetermination: when the gate is overdetermined by in-boundary contributions and NESS fails, ⊥\_OD; positive determinations by M2 carry B\_OD (5.4).  
E-s2  Preventive dependence: when a removal changes a write only by opening a gate that did not actually open (e.g. removing an inhibitory negative current), ⊥\_σOpen; a removal difference obtained only by opening branches that did not actually occur does not serve as source certification in this paper. “No opening” is a restriction of the sufficient certification method of this paper, not an ontological denial concerning all inhibitory mechanisms.  
E-s3  Route termination: for subsequent writes through reading Trace-Responses already left, the source verdicts differ under N′ and Ψ (the separating example of 5.8); a negative determination 0 carries N′, and without its declaration ⊥\_N′; positive determinations do not pass through this route.  
E-s4  Cross-beat certification through auxiliary positions: M1\_aux carries EF1′; if the auxiliary positions bear Trace-Responses, the route is continuation through Trace-Responses, and the positive determination is withdrawn to ⊥\_σOver.  
E-s5  The 0 of D16: when EF-σ fails or has not been verified, Src\_F = 0 is not read back as r = 0 (Theorem D16-SF(5)).  
E-s6  Presence is insufficient: the presence of the teaching current does not by itself give r (7.5(2), comparison C1); the criteria do not rest on presence, temporal correlation or slot reachability.

7\. Adjudication Theorem

### 7.1  Adjudication Theorem

Theorem (adjudication of field concretization for candidate hosts). Let χ be an algorithmic-level, model-mode declared instance of a member of 𝒦\_g, T ⊆ X, with declared intervention domain 𝒟₁.  
(1) Dynamics duties: Part and HDyn are adjudicated by Theorem D2; two-way on Ω\_dec when 𝔅₀ ∪ 𝔅\_b ∪ 𝔅\_c are declared; a tier (b) 0 carries B\_FD\[𝒟₁\], and without that declaration the verdict is ⊥\_Over; with 𝒟\_m as the declared domain, negative determination has only the sufficient negative determinations of Lemmas C, C′, while positive determination can still use single-beat witnesses (5.3).  
(2) Source: r\_R(T) is judged 1 by criterion PS (M1₀ consumes no correspondence hypothesis; M1\_aux carries EF1′; M2 carries B\_OD), judged 0\[N′, EF1′\] by Lemma S⁻, and otherwise ⊥ with a reason code; the direction of judging 1 is valid under the declared source reading (Theorem COR), and the complete source is not exhausted by the criterion.  
(3) Trace-Response set: q is adjudicated from the window-end state and the formation records; TraceSet = q ∧ r ∧ Ident ∧ HistEv.  
(4) Fact of constitution: Q\_R(T) = 1 ⇒ Fld(χ) (Theorem R7(1)); Q\_R(T\_max) = 0 ⇒ ¬Fld(χ) (Theorem R7(2), with antecedents EF1′ and those consumed by the conjuncts that are 0); otherwise ⊥. Q\_R(T) = 0 holds only for the listed T.  
(5) In-boundary self-feedback type: in the open loop it agrees with HDyn event by event (Theorem B2); in the closed loop it follows Theorem B3.  
(6) Relation to upstream: compatible with the verdicts of Chapter 3 on Ω\_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS\_act ∩ NN\_act (Theorem C4′); the relation to D16 of the Mathematical Form is as in Theorem D16-SF.  
Proof: for each item, see the theorems cited in Section 5. ∎

### 7.2  Condition Ledger

Antecedents fall into five classes by status: \[proved\] proved lemmas or theorems; \[structural\] holding by the structure of the model (program host, algorithmic level, model mode; this guarantees only implementation and run identity, and proves no new source, reading or correspondence interpretation); \[declared\] declared in advance and not changed during adjudication, the verdict becoming ⊥ on failure; \[correspondence\] pending correspondence hypotheses, which a verdict depending on them must carry with its conclusion, the verdict changing to the listed reason code on withdrawal; \[reading\] the declared source reading (a declared ontological reading whose content is not proved here). “The program is the host” covers only \[structural\]; it does not exempt \[correspondence\].  
　E1  Class membership (Proposition 1, closure of Definitions 1–3) \[proved\].  
　E2  The R-layer declaration is well formed; K\_t agrees with the program beat by beat (Lemma R1) \[proved\].  
　E3  MOD-R: the events in W acting on T are all listed \[structural\]; on withdrawal, the universal 0 changes to ⊥\_Inv.  
　E4  R2/R3: the read sets are complete, and the slot hypergraph is non-interfering \[proved\] \[structural\]; on withdrawal, the tier (a) 0 changes to ⊥.  
　E5  REC: the actual history is recorded throughout from the declared initial state \[structural\]; on withdrawal, ⊥\_Rec.  
　E6  ENT: the formative history before the record start is registered by entry beat, or every position of T is at most ε at the start \[declared\]; on withdrawal, ⊥\_Rec.  
　E7  POS: the Structural Positions are the synapses and their positions persist, giving Ident \[structural\]; on withdrawal, ⊥\_Id.  
　E8  BEAT-R: the model beat is the L beat; ε is the declared resolution \[declared\]; on withdrawal, ⊥\_Dom.  
　E9  DOM\[𝒟₁\]: the intervention domain is declared in advance as 𝒟₁; a 0 on 𝒟₁ does not imply a 0 on 𝒟\_m \[declared\]; on withdrawal, the tier (b) 0 changes to ⊥\_Over.  
　E10  B\_FD\[𝒟₁\] \[correspondence\] (Lemma 3.4); on withdrawal, the tier (b) 0 changes to ⊥\_Over.  
　E11  IND\_CLIP: individuation of clipping; not needed when the two readings agree \[correspondence\]; on withdrawal, ⊥\_Ind.  
　E12  RD: the event components are readable \[structural\]; on withdrawal, a is read only through KO, and saturated places give ⊥\_Sat.  
　E13  EF1′: the auxiliary positions v, x, y bear no Trace-Responses \[correspondence\]; on withdrawal, the 0s of Fld and T\_max change to ⊥, M1\_aux is unavailable, and Lemma S⁻ is unavailable.  
　E14  IMP-g: implementation fidelity of LOC, removal (including Trace-Response clamping) and the external clamp on the program host \[proved\] \[structural\].  
　E15  Dom4 (algorithmic level): P is the adjudicated host and L is the algorithmic level \[structural\]; read-back to physical objects is declared separately per 5.5.  
　E16–E19  Ω\_F, EF4′, NS\_act, NN\_act (the subdomain of compatibility on the intersection, verified case by case) \[declared\]; when they are not satisfied, Theorem C4′ does not apply.  
　E20  P4R ∧ P4Z: a proved sufficient subdomain (Theorem SUB) \[proved\]; not an antecedent of r = 1.  
　E21  The declared source reading \[reading\] (4.3).  
　E22  Reading N′ \[correspondence\]: used only for the negative determination S⁻, the verdict being written 0\[N′, EF1′\]; it enters no positive determination, nor the formal conclusion on the fidelity of D16 (the extension under N′ appears only in the conditional note after Theorem D16-SF(3), carries E22, and does not amount to “true F runs are automatically completely faithful”); on withdrawal, the 0 of r changes to ⊥\_N′. Reading Ψ is not declared and is mutually exclusive with N′; the choice between them is open (U2 of Section 10); no verdict in this ledger consumes Ψ.  
　E23  Open-loop declaration: the out-of-boundary letters form a declared exogenous sequence \[declared\]; when it is not satisfied, Theorem B2 does not apply and Theorem B3 is used instead.  
　E24  B\_FD^cl: in closed-loop instances, participation via routes lying entirely in B has functional dependence within 𝒟₁ of the clamped instance \[correspondence\]; on withdrawal, the tier (b) 0 changes to ⊥\_Over.  
　E25  Encoding fidelity (R layer): verdicts are evaluated only on R-layer witnesses, and r does not pass through D16 \[proved\].  
　E26  Admissible removal: the removal domain is any subset of components, with the no-opening condition and cross-beat Trace-Response clamping; changes propagate along actual chains (Lemmas CH₀, CH) \[proved\] \[structural\].  
　E27  B\_OD \[correspondence\] (4.3); on withdrawal, M2 is unavailable and the write is recorded as ⊥\_OD.  
　E28  EF-σ(T) \[declared\] (verified case by case): the independent antecedent for position-wise equality of Mark\_F and the complete Mark^A, and also the condition of applicability for reading back a 0 of Src\_F as r = 0; it does not follow from true F runs, from reading only pre-beat Structural Positions, or from the absence of alphabet gating; it is not necessary for every agreement of the two-valued Src; Src\_F = 1 does not need it.  
Verdict certificates and the antecedents they consume:  
　Part = 1, HDyn = 1 (positive determination by Theorem D2): E1–E5, E7, E8, E12, E14; at saturated places also E11 (not needed when the two readings agree); E10 is not consumed.  
　Part = 0, HDyn = 0: the above + E3, E6; at tier (b) also E9, E10.  
　q = 1: E5, E6, E8.  
　r = 1 (M1₀): E1, E2, E4, E5, E8, E14, E26, E21 and Theorem COR; no \[correspondence\] item is consumed. r = 1 (M1\_aux): E13 in addition. r = 1 (M2): E27 in addition.  
　r = 0 (S⁻): E3, E5, E6, E13, E22, E21; the verdict is written 0\[N′, EF1′\]. r = ⊥\_OD: M1₀ and M1\_aux fail, and E27 is undeclared or NESS fails.  
　TraceSet = 1: the union of the antecedents of q = 1 and r = 1 + E7.  
　Fld = 1 (algorithmic level): the union of the positive-determination antecedents of TraceSet, Part and HDyn + E15, E25 (Theorem R7(1)).  
　Fld = 0: the antecedents consumed by the conjuncts that are 0 in Q\_R(T\_max) = 0 + E13; if the 0 comes from tier (a) of HDyn or from a = 0, E10 is not consumed.  
　In-boundary self-feedback type (open loop): as for HDyn + E23; closed loop: Theorem B3 + E24 (at tier (b)).  
　Compatibility on the intersection (Theorem C4′): E16–E19 + E12 + E10 (at tier (b)).  
　D16 read-back: Src\_F = 1 ⇒ r = 1 (8.2(1)(3)); Mark\_F ⊆ Mark^PS (no additional antecedent; equality on true F runs); Mark\_F = the complete Mark^A, and reading back Src\_F = 0 as r = 0, further require E28; for the extension when N′ is declared, see the conditional note after Theorem D16-SF(3), which carries E22 and is not merged into the formal conclusion of this item.

### 7.3  Prohibited Read-Backs

　A criterion-level FD = 0 is not read as HistCond = 0 at the object level unless B\_FD is declared; nor is it read as HDyn = 0 in all overdetermined cases.  
　A 0 on 𝒟₁ is not read as a 0 on 𝒟\_m.  
　An uncertified source is not read as no source; ⊥\_OD and ⊥\_N′ are not read as 0.  
　Q\_R(T) = 0 is not read as Fld = 0; Fld pending T\_max is not read as 0.  
　Algorithmic-level verdicts are not read as verdicts on neuromorphic hardware instances or on biological neural tissue.  
　Reachability on the slot hypergraph is not read as an actual route or as HistCond = 1; the presence of the teaching current is not read as r = 1.  
　The Src\_F = 0 of D16 is not read as r = 0 while EF-σ is unverified; Mark\_F = Mark^PS is not read as Mark\_F = Mark^A.

### 7.4  Breach Modes

　Give a declared instance of a member of 𝒦\_g on which, on Ω\_dec and with 𝔅₀ ∪ 𝔅\_b ∪ 𝔅\_c declared, Theorem D2 returns 1 while HDyn (or Part) fails at the object level, or returns 0 while it holds.  
　Give a case in which the program of Theorem D1 returns 0 while some setting in 𝒟₁ changes the event, or returns 1 while the witness rerun does not change the event.  
　Give a write satisfying criterion PS and M1₀ whose removed signal did not participate in that write through an actual action chain (an instance contradicting Lemma CH₀).  
　Give a write satisfying P4R that is not certified by M1₀ (contradicting Theorem SUB).  
　Give an effective write in the nonnegative-weight subclass for which same-beat M1₀ fails and which lies at none of the three places of Theorem STR.  
　Give an atom in Ω\_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS\_act ∩ NN\_act on which Chapter 3 returns 1 while this paper returns 0 (or the reverse).  
　Give an F encoding satisfying 8.2(1)(3) in which a change recorded by D16 has no signal source (contradicting Theorem D16-SF(1)); or a true F run with Mark\_F ≠ Mark^PS.  
　Give an open-loop 𝒦\_g instance on which the event-by-event atoms of the in-boundary self-feedback type and of HDyn differ.  
　Give a reading listed in Appendix B for frozen SNN Instance P that disagrees with the program.  
7.5  Representative Instances (Frozen SNN; Appendix B.11, B.2 Item 3)  
(1) Instance P: the s1 frozen declared history—formation phase (A + teaching current (1, 0), rest, rest) × 6, then 8 beats of rest, with window W = beats 26–43 given by (A, rest, rest, B, rest, rest) × 3; T = {w00, w10}; ε = 1/100.  
　Entering signal – sustaining medium – firing – structural change – transmission route (beat 0, the pre-beat state being the declared initial state w = b = 1/4, v = x = y = 0): letter A and teaching current I = (1, 0) enter the field; entry units 0 and 1 sustain the stimulus (eligibility traces x0, x1 written to 1), synapses w00 and w10 each transmit 1/4, and the membrane of output 0 sustains teaching current 1; vt0 = 3/2 ≥ θ, so output 0 fires, while vt1 = 1/2 and output 1 does not fire; PLUS00 = A₊x′0 = 1/8, MINUS00 = −1/16; w00 changes from 1/4 to 5/16, z = 1/16 \> ε. Route: stimulus → entry units → through synapses w00, w10, together with the teaching current, into the membrane potential of output 0 → threshold crossing and firing → backpropagation reaches the synapse → multiplied with the eligibility trace and written into w00.  
　q: the window-end z of w00 and w10 is 0.607871 \> ε, the formation beat is 0, and q = 1.  
　r: 36 effective writes act on T (the formation pairings at beats 0, 3, 6, 9, 12, 15 and the in-window beats 26, 32, 38, 4 each), each certified by M1₀: at beats 0–9, removing the teaching current makes PLUS not occur and changes the MINUS component; at beats 12 and 15, removing the teaching current alone does not change PLUS (the Trace-Responses already left make the entry cross the threshold on its own), while joint removal makes it not occur—overdetermination among signals; at beats 26, 32, 38, removing the entry makes it not occur, the already-left Trace-Responses w00, w10 participating through the gate and the eligibility trace x0 through the component. r = 1, consuming none of P4R, P4Z, EF1′, B\_OD. Additional check: all 36 writes can be certified by “removing only the entry while keeping the teaching current”, so in another instance that places the teaching-current apparatus inside B the entry stimulus still gives r = 1 (the other declarations of that instance are not set up separately).  
　Ident = 1, HistEv = 1, TraceSet = 1.  
　Part = 1, HDyn = 1 (Theorem D2; the same with and without a declared B\_FD): all 12 events in W acting on T are positively determined; for example, the witness of PLUS00@26 sets w00 ← 0.267867 and w10 ← 149807/524288 at beat 7, reachable in the actual graph. In-boundary self-feedback type = 1. Q\_R(T) = 1. T\_max = {w00, w10}.  
　Fld = 1 (algorithmic level; Theorem R7(1)). Remaining antecedents: E1–E8, E12, E14, E15, E25, E26, with E21 and Theorem COR; Instance P has no saturated position–beats and does not consume E11; it consumes none of E10, E13, E22, E27, and does not take E20 as an antecedent. This verdict does not imply the qualification of neuromorphic hardware instances or of biological neural tissue.  
(2) Comparisons:  
　Instance N (history (A + teach 1, B + teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A), W = beats 1–5): r = 1 (all 6 writes by M1₀); Part = 1; HDyn = 0 (the PLUS at beat 1 is tier (a): when the gate read w00, w10 at beat 0, they were not yet available; the MINUS at beat 5 depends on T, but its component is −3/512, \|c\| \< ε); T\_max = {w00, w10, w20, w30}, with HDyn = 0 on it and Q\_R(T\_max) = 0; Fld = 0, with antecedents 𝔅₀ and EF1′, not consuming B\_FD.  
　Instances O1, O2: the T events with effective action all fall in tier (b); with B\_FD declared HDyn = 0, without it ⊥\_Over.  
　Instance SAT (W0 = 1/2, A₊ = 1/4, 8 saturated position–beats): HDyn is 1 under both the separate and the joint reading.  
　Comparison C1 (declared initial state v0 = 4/3, x0 = x1 = 1; history (rest + teach (0, 1), rest, rest)): on T = {w00} the only effective write is driven by the preset v0 crossing the threshold on its own (λv0 = 1 ≥ θ); the teaching current acts only on output 1, there is no actual action chain, and r = 0\[N′, EF1′\] (N′ undeclared: ⊥\_N′), q = 1, TraceSet = 0; on T′ = {w01, w11} the chain of PLUS is I1 → INJ1 → γ1 → PLUS, removing I1 makes it not occur, the preset x0, x1 participate in the component, and r = 1.  
　Verdict path of Chapter 3 (Instances P, N): the host is not of class F (3.6), so Y₂ verdicts are not obtained; under Y₁, q, r, Part, HDyn and Fld all return ⊥\_Prof; when a trajectory encoding with “occurrence as actually declared, constant laws” is used to force passage through the entry, the F encoding reads HDyn\_F = 0 while HistCond in the sense of the General Form holds, which is E25 of Chapter 3 (Appendix B.6).  
(3) Frozen SNN parameter grid of 108 groups (W0 ∈ {1/8, 1/4, 3/8, 1/2}, A₊ ∈ {1/16, 1/8, 1/4}, teaching ∈ {1/2, 1, 2}, number of formation pairings n ∈ {1, 3, 6}; T = {w00, w10}):  
　The 70 groups with q = 1: r = 1 (all by M1₀), TraceSet = 1.  
　Among them, the 48 groups with HDyn = 1: Q\_R(T) = 1, Fld = 1 (algorithmic level).  
　The 22 groups with HDyn = 0: Q\_R(T) = 0 holds only for the listed T. In 7 of them (W0 = 1/8; (A₊, teaching, n) = (1/8, 1/2, 3), (1/8, 1, 1), (1/8, 1, 3), (1/8, 2, 1), (1/8, 2, 3), (1/4, 1, 1), (1/4, 2, 1)), T\_max = T, Q\_R(T\_max) = 0 and Fld = 0; the 0 of their HDyn comes from all events in W acting on T being ineffective (6 events per group, all components ≤ ε, a = 0), with antecedents MOD-R, RD, REC, ENT, BEAT-R and EF1′, not consuming B\_FD. In the remaining 15 groups (W0 = 1/2, A₊ ∈ {1/8, 1/4}, excluding the 3 groups with A₊ = 1/4 and n = 6), HDyn = 0 uses tier (b) (⊥\_Over without a declared B\_FD), and T\_max consists of all 8 synapses; their 36 one- and two-element subsets were adjudicated one by one, with no witness of Q\_R = 1; exact adjudication on the 8-position T\_max did not finish within the 60-second budget per group, so Fld is pending T\_max and is not read as 0.  
　The 38 groups with q = 0: TraceSet = 0 (35 groups with r = 1, 3 groups with r = 0\[N′, EF1′\]), only for the listed T; Fld is not adjudicated on this basis.  
(4) Overdetermination examples (Appendix B.11, Section 4):  
　OD-1 (overdetermination among signals): n\_in = 2; entry a at beat 0 (w00 = 1/4, no threshold crossing, leaving x0 = 1), at beat 1 letter b (w10 = 1, crosses the threshold on its own) + I = 1 (crosses on its own), PLUS00@1 = 1/8: removing only I changes nothing, removing only entry 1 changes nothing, and joint removal makes it not occur. M1₀, r = 1.  
　OD-4 (same-beat overdetermination, certifiable across beats): λ = 1, v\_reset = θ = 1; at beat 0, A + I = 1 fires (v resets to 1); at beat 1, none + I = 1/4, PLUS00@1 = 1/8; under same-beat removal of I@1 the gate stays open; after removal at beat 0 (with Trace-Response clamping), the gate at beat 1 closes. M1\_aux, r = 1\[EF1′\].  
　OD-3 (preset membrane potential and signal each sufficient to cross the threshold): λ = 1, preset v = 1, x = 1, letter none + I = 1, PLUS00@0 = 1/8: removing I leaves the write unchanged; {I} crosses the threshold on its own while ∅ does not, so NESS holds. M2, r = 1\[B\_OD\].  
　OD-2 (as above, but with I = 1/4): the only subset that contains I and not λv is {I}, which does not cross the threshold; NESS fails. ⊥\_OD.  
　Overdetermination family (200 members: λ = 1, v\_reset ∈ {0, 1, 3/2}, random preset auxiliary states), with 1311 effective writes: M1₀ 994, M1\_aux 223, M2 24, ⊥\_OD 58, no chain 12; 0 failures in re-checking the M2 witnesses; the removed components of the M1\_aux witnesses all lie among the cross-beat origins; 0 violations of Theorem STR.  
(5) Endpoint examples with preset auxiliary states (Appendix B.11, Section 3): one member, one Structural Position, λ, d\_x, d\_y ∈ {1/2, 1}, A₋ ∈ {0, 1/16}, taking each nonzero stationary auxiliary state (v = 1/2, x = 1, y = 1 and their combinations) and the zero auxiliary state, with letters A + I = 1, none + I = 1, A + I = 0, plus an example with λ = 0, d\_x = 1, x = 1 and letter none + I = 1: 145 cases in all, with 94 effective writes. Of these, 70 satisfy P4R but not P4Z (nonzero stationary auxiliary states arise only when the corresponding coefficient is 1). Adjudicated case by case: every case has an actual action chain issuing from a field-entry component and a same-beat admissible-removal witness, is certified by M1₀, and has r = 1; nonzero auxiliary positions act only as co-participants (e.g. in the example with λ = 0, d\_x = 1, x = 1, the teaching current I0 → INJ0 → γ0 → PLUS00 determines the occurrence, and the preset x0 = 1 determines the component 1/8), and do not constitute a negative determination. The 24 writes on the zero auxiliary state are likewise certified by M1₀. These 70 all satisfy P4R, and Theorem SUB explains why the case-by-case results agree; the basis of adjudication is each case's own chain and witness.

8\. Master Ledger of Candidate Hosts

### 8.1  Explanatory Notes

　Registration rule: each item lists the declared unit, the host class (determined by mechanism, not presupposed), whether the criteria of this paper apply, the current evidence level, A: model verdict, B: conditional inference on the real host, C: empirical certification, gaps, and directions to be proved. A, B and C are separate columns; they are not successive stages, and none passes for another. Host classes fall into four grades: class F (exactly encodable in the F layer of the Mathematical Form, so that Theorems 6 and 7 of Chapter 3 apply directly); gating class (the occurrence of processes is determined by Trace-Response positions through threshold gating; “member” means proved under Definitions 1–3, “extension candidate” means that the mechanism is of the same type but goes beyond Definition 1 and needs a separate class-membership proof); continuous class (continuous-time or continuous-valued smooth nonlinear laws without threshold gating; outside the contract); undetermined (the mechanism is not individuated far enough to be classified). Items to which Chapter 3 applies directly are marked as applications. The lack of existing experiments, the lack of investigation and protocols that cannot yet be carried out are not taken as 0, and candidates are not removed from the ledger on that account. Evidence levels use the V0–V3 grading of *The Master Plan and Results Archive for the Verification of the 17 Hosts*.  
　Two results of this paper have structural significance for most candidates and are registered in column B as conditional inferences: (i) Source: in real hosts, writes of Trace-Responses are mostly executed by processes of the medium itself (receptor modification, tube-wall growth, synaptic plasticity, conductive-filament formation, granular rearrangement). For a given candidate, if it has been proved that an out-of-domain signal participated through an actual route in at least one effective write acting on T, and that write is executed by a mechanism inside B, then an encoding with faithful carriers does not satisfy the event-by-event source-fidelity condition EF-σ, Src\_F = 0 is not read back, and one must turn to R-layer source witnesses or supply a proof of the corresponding read-back (Theorem D16-SF); where writes are executed only by carriers inside B and the participation relation has not yet been certified (including autonomous relaxation and writes driven by preset conditions), EF-σ is registered only as pending verification, and no verdict that it has failed is issued. “Proved” may come from a rigorous model with an explicit conditional derivation; material certification is listed separately (column C); frozen SNN Instance P already has 36 M1₀ witnesses, and the conclusion that EF-σ fails for it is in Theorem D16-SF(4). Under the declared source reading, the direction of certification is “an out-of-domain signal participates in the write through an actual action chain, with a proof of source bearing (such as a removal comparison)”; established Trace-Responses and the state of the medium may participate as well, and no resting starting point is required; criterion PS and Lemmas CH₀, CH are proved only for 𝒦\_g, and transferring them requires a separate proof of lemmas of the same type for each class. (ii) In-boundary self-feedback type: where the environment feeds back through the body or the outside world (closed loop), the in-boundary self-feedback type can be adjudicated only via Theorem B3; in the open-loop case it is adjudicated together with HDyn by Theorem B2.

### 8.2  Item by Item

　01  *Escherichia coli* (biological, non-neural)  
　　Declared unit: a fixed strain and chemotaxis regime; T = receptor modification state (methylation); the signal is a chemoattractant.  
　　Host class: continuous class (MWC cooperativity of ligand binding, activity-dependent continuous rate laws of CheR/CheB, including CheB phosphorylation feedback; no threshold gating or reset).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable (outside the class). Chapter 3: the 4.3 statistics for the first-layer Tr in measurement mode can adjudicate (data must be declared); no verdict yet.  
　　Evidence: Part V1 (Sourjik and Berg 2002, strains with fixed modification states); HDyn branch blockade V1 (Clausznitzer 2010, preprint, rate-dependent model); Shimizu 2010, Meir 2010, Lazova 2011 V1; Alon 1999, Goy 1977 V0.  
　　A: no model verdict in this paper.  
　　B: Source—the attractant (out-of-boundary) acts on the receptors (B) and rewrites the modification state through CheR/CheB (inside B), a participation-type source structure (entry participates in a write executed inside B; declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); HDyn—CheB demethylation depends on activity and activity on the modification state, the route is intracellular, and under an open-loop stimulus regime this is a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type (the structure of Theorem B2). Untested premises: a continuous-class R-layer model and its class-membership proof; direct quantification of the modification state; the single-cell level. Prediction (to be derived): when CheR is restored with tunable expression, the relation between adaptation rate and modification state shifts as the model predicts.  
　　C: ⊥\_Obs (measurement mode returns only 1 or ⊥; the existing V1 evidence has not been declared under 4.3).  
　　Gaps: a contract for the continuous class; direct quantification of methylation; population readings versus a single-cell B; declaration of the background-maintenance period for TraceSet.  
　　Directions to be proved: class definition and corresponding criteria for the continuous class; the conditional derivation of column B; methods of empirical evidence gathering.  
　02a  *Physarum polycephalum* · original boundary (biological, non-neural)  
　　Declared unit: a single multinucleate plasmodium; T = tube-diameter hierarchy.  
　　Host class: continuous class (diameter–flow feedback law, Tero-type continuous dynamics).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable (outside the class). Instance B of Chapter 3: verdict ⊥\_Obs / ⊥\_Prof (7.5 of Chapter 3), an application of Chapter 3.  
　　Evidence: Kramar and Alim 2021, Marbach 2023, Tero 2010, Saigusa 2008 V1; Nakagaki 2000, Boussard 2019 V0; no flow-blocking, thick-tube excision or sham-stimulation interventions.  
　　A: the verdict on Instance B of Chapter 3 (⊥).  
　　B: Source—nutrient stimuli (out-of-boundary) rewrite tube diameters through tube-wall growth (inside B), a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); HDyn—the shear rate determines the change of tube diameter and the route lies within the plasmodium (Marbach 2023), a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type in the open loop. Untested premises: a continuous-class model; material counterparts of MOD, LOC and INV-c (7.5 of Chapter 3).  
　　C: ⊥\_Obs.  
　　Gaps: MOD, LOC exclusivity and INV-c are unobtainable; different specimens cannot be combined into one T; cutting is itself an attractive stimulus (Bäuerle 2017).  
　　Directions to be proved: a continuous-class model with breachable predictions for flow blocking and sham stimulation; the conditions under which erasure experiments are valid (control cuts, read-out after the healing period).  
　02b  *Physarum polycephalum* · extended boundary (including extracellular slime trails)  
　　Declared unit: B contains the plasmodium and the slime trails on the substrate it has passed over (separate from the original boundary); T = the distribution of slime trails.  
　　Host class: undetermined (slime deposition is approximately continuous; avoidance of existing slime is contact-triggered behavioural gating, formally “occurrence determined by Trace-Responses”; class membership to be proved).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable for now; the first layer of Chapter 3 in measurement mode is a possible route of adjudication (data must be declared).  
　　Evidence: Reid 2012 V1 (pre-laid slime lowered the trap-escape success rate from 96% to 33%, 24 per group, with Y-maze and trap-free controls).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—slime is deposited by the plasmodium (inside B), the deposition site follows the movement of the plasmodium, and the movement is driven by environmental stimuli (signals); entry and existing slime jointly determine deposition, which under the declared source reading does not preclude a signal source (declared source reading); an action chain through which the stimulus participates in deposition via an actual route, and a proof of source bearing, must be given (no deposition segment “without existing slime, driven by the stimulus alone” is required); HDyn—slime trails determine avoidance (occurrence determined by Trace-Responses), and avoidance changes the subsequent deposition sites, the route lying within the extended boundary: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type (the structure of Theorem B2 under open-loop stimulation).  
　　C: ⊥\_Obs.  
　　Gaps: declaration of χ, T, ε for the 02b boundary; IMP declaration of masking interventions as LOC (the implementation fidelity listed in 5.5).  
　　Directions to be proved: declaration for the 02b boundary (χ, T, ε); whether the measurement mode of Chapter 3 applies directly.  
　03  *Caenorhabditis elegans* (biological · neural)  
　　Declared unit: ASER→AIB salt-concentration memory, AFD→AIY temperature memory; T = the presynaptic PKC-1-dependent state and the transmission gating point it determines.  
　　Host class: undetermined (*C. elegans* neurons mostly use graded potentials; transmission gating is determined by a set point, and the set point is rewritten over several hours, which is not the spike-threshold gating of 𝒦\_g; class membership to be proved).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable (class membership not proved).  
　　Evidence: Kunitomo 2013 V1, Hawk 2018 V1 (TraceSet, Part; write, blockade and bypass interventions all present); Hiroki 2022, Sato 2021, connectome V0; no in-boundary evidence for HDyn.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—the cultivation salt concentration (out-of-boundary) acts on ASER (B) and rewrites the presynaptic state through the PKC-1 pathway, a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); in-boundary self-feedback type—the circuit envisaged on the card, “T determines turning → the concentration encountered changes → the new concentration rewrites T”, runs through the body and the environment and is a closed loop (Theorem B3): HDyn can hold via an out-of-boundary route (χ\_e type), the in-boundary self-feedback type must be adjudicated separately after the environment is clamped, and the existing evidence does not suffice to adjudicate it.  
　　C: ⊥ (no evidence for HDyn; not judged 0).  
　　Gaps: definition of a graded-gating class; closed-loop declaration and environment clamping (e.g. read-out under fixed-concentration microfluidics); direct electrophysiological measurement of synaptic efficacy.  
　　Directions to be proved: class definition of the graded-gating class; the two declarations, closed-loop and clamped.  
　04  *Drosophila* KC→MBON (biological · neural)  
　　Declared unit: the single compartment α1, or the cross-compartment γ1pedc+α3.  
　　Host class: gating-class extension candidate (synaptic depression when KC firing coincides with DAN signals is third-factor gating; the MBON→DAN return is inter-population feedback; in Definition 1, potentiation and depression are gated only by the unit's own firing, so a separate class-membership proof is needed).  
　　Criteria of this paper: class membership not proved; not applicable for now.  
　　Evidence: Hige 2015, Yamada 2023, Huang 2024, Pavlowsky 2018, Zhao 2018, Jacob and Waddell 2020 V1 (qualified); Felsenberg 2018, connectome synapse counts V0; no physiological demonstration of MBON-α1→PAM-α1 functional connectivity; long-term measurements at the level of the KC→MBON synapse are missing.  
　　A: none (no R-layer model set up).  
　　B: Source—the unconditioned stimulus (sugar, electric shock; out-of-boundary) writes depression through DAN (inside B, if declared as part of B) coinciding with KC activity: a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); in-boundary self-feedback type—under an open-loop stimulus regime, if DAN is declared as part of B, the route “KC→MBON synapse → MBON → DAN → synapse of the same compartment” is of the same type as the SNN route “synapse → output neuron → backpropagation to the same synapse”, and the in-boundary self-feedback type is adjudicated together with HDyn by the structure of Theorem B2; untested premises: a class-membership proof for the third-factor gating extension (of the type of Proposition 1), and the item-by-item mapping of model faithfulness listed in 5.5.  
　　C: ⊥ (gaps as above; not judged 0).  
　　Gaps: definition of the extended class and its class-membership proof; physiological evidence for the return in the α1 compartment; long-term measurements at the synapse level.  
　　Directions to be proved: settling the declared unit between the two formulations; a Definition 1′ for the third-factor gating extension, and whether the monotonicity of Lemma C holds under that extension (multiplicativity and lateral inhibition are the failure points of Lemma C; monotonicity when the third factor is a nonnegative modulation is unproved).  
　05  Human brain (biological · neural; local unit)  
　　Declared unit: the local unit of pair-based STDP in ex vivo human hippocampal slices (using only evidence from human tissue itself, without extrapolating to the whole brain).  
　　Host class: gating class (model-level candidate): the form of pair-based STDP is of the same type as Definition 2(6); real tissue is continuous biophysics, class membership can be proved only at the model level, and the material level requires Dom4.  
　　Criteria of this paper: usable at the model level; for real tissue, the implementation fidelity and model faithfulness listed in 5.5 must be declared.  
　　Evidence: Testa-Silva 2010 V1 (qualified) (TraceSet, Part); Verhoog 2013 V0; no HDyn evidence; durations of 20–30 minutes; tissue from epilepsy patients.  
　　A: the SNN verdicts belong to the program (the limits in 5.5) and are not verdicts on the human brain.  
　　B: Conditional inference—if the local unit satisfies the item-by-item mapping of model faithfulness listed in 5.5 (threshold firing ↔ g, STDP ↔ PLUS/MINUS, bAP ↔ bap, synaptic efficacy ↔ w), then the structure of Theorems D2, B2 and criterion PS transfers as a conditional inference. Breachable predictions (to be refined by derivation): (i) setting the efficacy of a single pathway (the material counterpart of LOC) changes the occurrence of postsynaptic firing and, with it, the subsequent potentiation (an HDyn witness); (ii) blocking dendritic backpropagation should abolish “Trace-Response-dependent potentiation”—in the model this holds by construction, while in tissue it tests the mapping of gating and plasticity, not the theorem itself.  
　　C: ⊥ (no HDyn evidence; not judged 0). The SNN comparison does not prove the qualification of biological neural tissue.  
　　Gaps: setting and backpropagation-blockade experiments in human tissue; a Dom4 declaration.  
　　Directions to be proved: primary experimental evidence on human-tissue STDP with single-pathway setting or backpropagation blockade; a Dom4 declaration.  
　06  *Arabidopsis* (biological, non-neural multicellular)  
　　Declared unit: histone modifications at WRKY promoters in systemic leaves after local pathogen or BTH induction (the chromatin memory of systemic acquired resistance).  
　　Host class: undetermined (the calcium wave is threshold-triggered rapid propagation and is not retained; chromatin modification has continuous dynamics).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: Jaskiewicz 2011 V0; Toyota 2018 V0 (the calcium wave does not fit the retention condition, but glr mutants can serve as a blockade of the transmission route).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—the pathogen (out-of-boundary) rewrites chromatin modification in systemic leaves through signals within the plant (inside B): a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); HDyn—on restimulation the modification state determines the transcriptional response, which belongs to Part; whether the subsequent rewriting of the modification state issues from the modification state itself is unknown.  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: the original evidence of Jaskiewicz 2011 has not been incorporated; the retention duration of the modifications, erasure interventions, alternative explanations by mobile signals such as SA.  
　　Directions to be proved: the evidence on modification retention in the original Jaskiewicz 2011; combined evidence of “local stimulus – transmission blockade – retention of the modification in systemic leaves – restimulation”, and a declaration of χ, T on that basis.  
　07  MLP (computational)  
　　Declared unit: the minimal representative model of Instance A of Chapter 3 (single-weight linear unit, linear optimization law); the designated full network is not declared.  
　　Host class: class F (minimal representative model); the full network (ReLU activation, gradient updates) is a gating-class extension candidate: the ReLU derivative \[z \> 0\] is determined by the weights and decides whether an update reaches a given unit (occurrence determined by Trace-Responses), and the update reads w (a multiplicative law, outside the class).  
　　Criteria of this paper: the minimal representative model is a direct application of Chapter 3; the full network awaits a class-membership proof.  
　　Evidence: program host (algorithmic level).  
　　A (Instance A of Chapter 3, application; 7.5 of Chapter 3): χ\_ext Fld = 1, in-boundary self-feedback type 0 (under INV-c and EF4); χ\_in Fld = 1, in-boundary self-feedback type 1; χ\_frz HDyn = 0, Fld = 0. Note: in χ\_in, the training input drives writes by the optimizer inside B, which under the declared source reading also have a signal source; the r of Instance A is already given by the out-of-boundary initialization pulse and is unaffected.  
　　B: when the full network is deployed frozen, no process acts on the weights within W, so by the structure of χ\_frz HDyn = 0 (an inference from model structure; no instance declared).  
　　C: not applicable (at the algorithmic level the program is the object; hardware is another instance).  
　　Gaps: declaration of the boundaries of the full network and of the optimizer; class definition of a multiplicative gating extension.  
　　Directions to be proved: declaration of the designated network and of the optimizer boundary (with and without the optimizer as two separate instances); the corresponding form of Theorem D1 under the multiplicative extension (no longer affine within a branch; E-g11 of Section 6).  
　08  RNN/LSTM (computational)  
　　Declared unit: the within-episode Hebbian traces of the differentiable plastic recurrent networks of Miconi 2018.  
　　Host class: continuous class (smooth nonlinearity in discrete beats: the Hebbian update is bilinear in activity, and activity passes through tanh; no threshold gating).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable (outside the class).  
　　Evidence: V1 (E4; the run results of the public code have not been incorporated).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—input drives activity, and activity writes the Hebbian trace (inside B): a participation-type source structure (entry and the Hebbian trace already left jointly determine the write, which under the declared source reading does not preclude a signal source; clearing at the start of an episode is only the analogue of a sufficient subdomain); HDyn—subsequent writes depend on the Hebbian trace already left, the route lying within the network: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type under open-loop input.  
　　C: program host; once the run results of the public code are incorporated, V2 is attainable; no material certification is made.  
　　Gaps: class membership; results of the controls with clearing in mid-episode and with α = 0.  
　　Directions to be proved: control results on the public code with clearing in mid-episode and with α = 0.  
　09  Transformer (computational)  
　　Declared unit: weights, context, KV cache, external kNN memory and TTT-layer hidden states, each declared separately.  
　　Host class: undetermined (the TTT layer is of the continuous class: per-token gradient writes, multiplicative; external kNN memory: top-k retrieval is an order-statistic gating determined by the stored keys, and writes follow a fixed schedule).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: Wu 2022, Sun 2024 V1 (E4).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: weights frozen at inference: no process acts within W, so by the structure of χ\_frz HDyn = 0 (if T is taken as the weights; an inference from model structure); TTT hidden state: the amount written depends on the already-left W\_{t−1}, an HDyn candidate; kNN memory: the written content is influenced by retrieval results (strength and direction determined by Trace-Responses), and the occurrence of retrieval gating is determined by Trace-Responses.  
　　C: program host; control results with η = 0 and with reset in mid-run can reach V2.  
　　Gaps: class attribution and declaration of each object; control results.  
　　Directions to be proved: class attribution of the update law of the TTT layer; whether kNN retrieval gating can be written as a gating-class extension.  
　10  SNN (computational · neural; the first case of this paper)  
　　Declared unit: the frozen SNN (Appendix A.1, SHA-256 4e0fd45d…); T = {w00, w10}.  
　　Host class: gating-class member (Proposition 1).  
　　Criteria of this paper: applicable (first case).  
　　Evidence: program host, algorithmic level; programs and run output in Appendices A and B.  
　　A (model mode; 7.5, 5.6 and Theorem D2): Instance P (s1 frozen declared history): q = 1, r = 1 (M1₀, 36/36), TraceSet = 1, Part = 1, HDyn = 1, in-boundary self-feedback type = 1, Fld = 1 (algorithmic level; remaining antecedents in 7.2); Instance N: r = 1, Part = 1, HDyn = 0, in-boundary self-feedback type = 0, Fld = 0 (T\_max, tier (a), not consuming B\_FD); O1, O2: HDyn = in-boundary self-feedback type = 0 (carrying B\_FD) / ⊥\_Over; SAT: HDyn = in-boundary self-feedback type = 1; grid of 108 groups: 48 groups with Fld = 1 (algorithmic level); 22 groups with Q\_R(T) = 0 on the listed T, of which 7 have T\_max = T and Fld = 0 (all events in W acting on T ineffective, a = 0, not consuming B\_FD) and 15 have Fld pending T\_max (not read as 0); 38 groups with TraceSet = 0.  
　　B: running on neuromorphic hardware is another instance, to be declared through the item-by-item mapping of model faithfulness listed in 5.5; as a model of biological tissue it is only a computational simulation, see 05.  
　　C: not applicable (at the algorithmic level the program is the object; no material certification is needed, and none is made).  
　　Gaps: B\_OD (overdetermined cases; Instance P does not involve it); upstream interface G-r (Section 11); evaluation of T\_max for the 15 grid groups (7.5(3)).  
　　Directions to be proved: adjudication of T\_max for the 15 grid groups (its conditions being a larger evaluation budget, or a structural lemma on functional dependence on 𝒟₁); declaration of neuromorphic hardware instances per 5.5.  
　11  Reservoir network (computational / physical device)  
　　Declared unit: the physical reservoir of a single spin-torque nano-oscillator (Torrejon 2017); fixed reservoir dynamics and read-out training declared separately.  
　　Host class: continuous class (nonlinear oscillator relaxation, continuous time).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: Torrejon 2017 V1; Appeltant 2011 V0.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—the input current (out-of-boundary) acts on the device, and the fading state in the reservoir is written by the device's own dynamics: a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; a resting starting point is only a sufficient subdomain, not a necessary condition); if B is taken as the device, the read-out weights lie outside the boundary (they are trained on a computer), which does not fit TraceSet or HDyn; the reading of the fading state as a formation record is registered (formation registered by the effective changes of D16, retention adjudicated within the window).  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: memory-capacity or delay-task measurements that separate history dependence from instantaneous nonlinearity; erasure controls with longer intervals.  
　　Directions to be proved: theoretical predictions for memory capacity and the interval controls; primary experimental evidence from physical reservoirs with swept feedback strength.  
　12  Fluvial depositional system (material)  
　　Declared unit: the Caltech backwater-controlled experimental delta (Ganti 2016).  
　　Host class: undetermined (continuous sediment transport and aggradation, plus threshold avulsion events: avulsion occurs when aggradation reaches about 0.3 times the channel depth—the occurrence of avulsion is determined by Trace-Responses crossing a threshold, isomorphic to gating in the gating class, but the system is a continuous-time hybrid, outside the contract).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable (outside the contract); its threshold-avulsion gating structure is isomorphic to that of the gating class (see the host-class field), making it the first non-biological candidate for a hybrid-class extension (8.3).  
　　Evidence: Ganti 2016, Chadwick 2022, Ganti 2014 V1; Reitz 2010, Reitz and Jerolmack 2012 V0; Martin and Edmonds 2022 V1 (E4, erasure predictions); no flume experiments that flatten the aggradational deposit or pre-cut channels.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—water and sediment supply (out-of-boundary) leave traces through the bed's own aggradation (the material action of the medium): a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); HDyn—the aggraded deposit crossing a threshold determines the occurrence of avulsion (occurrence determined by Trace-Responses, an R-layer representation, Mathematical Form D18), the route lying within the bed: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type under open-loop inflow (the energy comes from outside the boundary, the route lies inside it).  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: a contract for the continuous-time hybrid class; the budget is not closed; Trace-Response erasure or implantation experiments.  
　　Directions to be proved: definition of a hybrid class (continuous transport + threshold events) taking the gating structure of this paper as a template.  
　13  Reaction–diffusion system (material)  
　　Declared unit: photo-imprinted Turing patterns in a photosensitive BZ-AOT microemulsion (Kaminaga 2006).  
　　Host class: continuous class (partial differential equations, dissipative bistability).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: Kaminaga 2006 V0; Kuhnert 1989 V0.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: maintenance depends on continuous illumination and reactant supply, registered separately under Tr^rest and Tr^cut of Chapter 3; Source—illumination (out-of-boundary) selects the branch of the medium, a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; a uniform initial state is only a sufficient subdomain).  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: primary original evidence; a systematic comparison of different illumination histories; re-excitation tests after supply cut-off.  
　　Directions to be proved: primary original evidence; Liesegang precipitation bands as a candidate instance with a clearer retention structure (extending the object domain requires a separate declaration).  
　14  Memristive material networks (material)  
　　Declared unit: Ag-PVP nanowire networks; single devices and networks listed separately.  
　　Host class: gating-class extension candidate (atomic switches form when the junction voltage crosses a threshold, the activation threshold falls with cycling, and rewriting within the retention period starts from the connected structure already left; but the junction voltage is given by solving the Kirchhoff network and is a nonlinear function of the conductances, beyond the form in Definition 1 in which the gating argument is affine in w).  
　　Criteria of this paper: class membership not proved; a candidate for the second, non-neural case.  
　　Evidence: Manning 2018, Diaz-Alvarez 2019, Milano 2020, Milano 2023, Diaz-Alvarez 2020, Loeffler 2023 V1; Ohno 2011 V0; the three duties are spread over different samples; the spatial dimension (distinguishing Trace-Response guidance from topological guidance) is missing.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—the applied voltage (out-of-boundary) forms conductive filaments through ion migration within the junctions (inside B): a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; a reset starting point is only a sufficient subdomain); HDyn—the pathways already left determine the subsequent voltage distribution and thereby which junctions cross the threshold (occurrence determined by Trace-Responses), the route lying within the network: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type under open-loop excitation (the structure of Theorem B2).  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: class definition of network gating (the gating argument is a nonlinear function of the conductances, so the affine branching of Theorem D1 does not apply directly); write–removal–read–rewrite controls on the same network; the premise that a reset network is equivalent to the original network.  
　　Directions to be proved: the conditions for serving as the second, non-neural case of 𝒦\_g—an R-layer declaration of a small network, and whether the monotonicity of Lemma C holds on it (the conditions under which increasing conductance makes junction voltages monotone).  
　15  Granular matter (material)  
　　Declared unit: a vertically tapped, compacting column of glass beads (Josserand 2000); the contact network as the T candidate.  
　　Host class: undetermined (granular rearrangement is triggered by local forces crossing thresholds; the contact network is not directly observed, and the laws are not individuated).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: Josserand 2000 V1 (comparison of subsequent compaction for equal density and different histories, supporting Part and behaviour-level HDyn); Vanel 1999 V1 (the construction history determines the basal stress; no HDyn evidence).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—tapping (out-of-boundary) rearranges the grains through the material action of the contacts between them: a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; transfer requires a separate proof); HDyn—the direction and rate of subsequent rearrangement issue from the configuration already left, the route lying within the column: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type under open-loop tapping.  
　　C: ⊥.  
　　Gaps: direct observation of the configuration variables (photoelastic or X-ray tomography); erasure-type interventions; independent replication.  
　　Directions to be proved: experimental evidence on cyclic-loading memory with imaging of the contact network; a declaration of the contact network as T, with theoretical predictions.  
　16  Hysteretic magnetic materials (material)  
　　Declared unit: exchange bias (Meiklejohn and Bean 1956); return-point memory (Pierce 2005) listed separately.  
　　Host class: gating-class extension candidate (Preisach type: hysterons flip when the external field crosses a threshold; interfacial pinning determines the loop shift, and the training effect is a negative rewriting of the pinning by subsequent sweeps).  
　　Criteria of this paper: class membership not proved.  
　　Evidence: Meiklejohn and Bean 1956 V0; Pierce 2005 V1 (its return-point memory issues from quenched disorder at preparation, with no formation process within W, which does not fit the formation-record condition).  
　　A: none.  
　　B: Source—the cooling field (out-of-boundary) pins the antiferromagnetic spins through interfacial exchange coupling: a participation-type source structure (declared source reading; a zero-field-cooled starting point is only a sufficient subdomain); HDyn—loop sweeps read in the pinning and weaken it through the training effect, the route being the core–shell interface: a candidate for the in-boundary self-feedback type.  
　　C: ⊥ (key original sources V0).  
　　Gaps: primary original evidence; original experiments on the training effect and on erasure by heating; class definition of hysteron gating.  
　　Directions to be proved: original experimental evidence on the training effect of exchange bias (including interfacial spin probing and heating-erasure controls); an R-layer declaration of Preisach gating.  
　17  G17 gravitational-environment–electromagnetic-pulse continuous Trace-Response and geometric-history system (physical field)  
　　Declared unit: light propagation, historical retention, subsequent read-in, field qualification and new dynamics kept separate.  
　　Host class: undetermined (continuous field theory).  
　　Criteria of this paper: not applicable.  
　　Evidence: the literature baseline is the deflection, VLBI and Cassini constraints together with a list of equivalent formulations; according to general relativity, light leaves no persistent local gravitational imprint, gravitational-wave memory has not been detected, and the bending of light paths is not taken as evidence of sedimentation.  
　　A: none.  
　　B: under existing theory there is no candidate position at which “a signal leaves a Trace-Response in a medium” (the q of TraceSet has no bearer); whether a scheme that generates the trajectories of motion by an explicit dynamical mechanism can stand, whether it is an equivalent reformulation, and whether it yields new predictions are separate questions.  
　　C: no verdict.  
　　Gaps: the declaration of candidate Trace-Response positions itself.  
　　Directions to be proved: declaration of candidate Trace-Response positions; adjudication of qualification.

### 8.3  Overview

　　Host classes: gating-class member 1 (10); gating-class extension candidates 4 (04, 05, 14, 16); class F 1 (07, the minimal representative model); continuous class 5 (01, 02a, 08, 11, 13); undetermined 7 (02b, 03, 06, 09, 12, 15, 17). 18 items in all.  
　　Criteria of this paper apply directly: 10 (A-column verdicts complete). Direct applications of Chapter 3: 07 (Instance A), 02a (Instance B).  
　　With A-column verdicts: 10 (this paper; the Fld = 1 of Instance P is an algorithmic-level verdict and carries no source correspondence antecedent), 07 (Chapter 3), 02a (Chapter 3, ⊥). With conditional inferences in column B: all except 17 (the source structure, and the open-loop or closed-loop attribution of the in-boundary self-feedback type). Column C: no item has obtained an empirical verdict of 1; measurement mode gives no 0.  
　　Highest evidence level V1 (mostly V1 qualified); no V2 or V3.  
　　Verdicts of 0: none (the Fld = 0 of Instance N under 10, the Fld = 0 of the 7 grid groups and the Q = 0 on the listed T in the grid are model verdicts on declared instances under the listed antecedents, not verdicts on candidates; the 15 grid groups with W0 = 1/2 have Fld pending T\_max).  
　　Extension candidates closest to the definition of the gating class: 04 (third-factor gating) and 14 (network gating, candidate for the second, non-neural case); covered by the second contract (continuous class): 01, 02a, 08, 11, 13; first candidate for the hybrid class: 12 (threshold avulsion).

9\. Declaration of Strength  
　Proved (with proofs): the closure of Definitions 1–3 and Proposition 1; Lemmas R1–R3, R3σ; Proposition N1, Lemma Q, Propositions N2, N2′ (under the listed assumptions, E1–E4 among them); Theorem L1, Lemmas L2, L3; Lemmas 3.1, 3.2, 3.3, and the criterion-level part of Lemma 3.4; Theorems D1, D2 (relative to 𝒟₁); Lemmas C, C′, D; Theorem TM; Lemmas CH₀, CH; Theorem COR (sufficient direction; M1₀ unconditionally, M1\_aux under EF1′, M2 under B\_OD); Proposition M-r; Proposition Z, Lemma Rest; Theorem SUB; Theorem STR (nonnegative-weight subclass); Lemma S⁻ (under reading N′); Lemma IMP-g; Theorem R7; Theorems B1, B2, B3; Lemma NS, Theorem C4′; Theorem D16-SF(1), (2) and (3) (compatibility with Mark^PS).  
　Instance verdicts (model mode, algorithmic level): for frozen SNN Instance P, Part = HDyn = 1, r = 1, TraceSet = 1 and Fld = 1, with the remaining antecedents listed in 7.2; Fld = 0 for Instance N; comparison C1; Fld = 1 for 48 grid groups and Fld = 0 for 7; the verdicts on the endpoint examples with preset auxiliary states and on overdetermination examples OD-1, OD-4.  
　Conditional conclusions: r = 1 for example OD-3 (under B\_OD); positive determinations by M1\_aux (under EF1′); the 0 of r (under N′, EF1′); the tier (b) 0 (under B\_FD); complete fidelity on true F runs in the conditional note of 5.8 (under N′).  
　Finite program verification: the readings listed in Appendix B; they cover their generating domains, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs of Section 5.  
　Not issued:  
　　exact adjudication on 𝒟\_m;  
　　the ontological content of the declared source reading; the holding of B\_FD, EF1′, B\_OD, N′;  
　　exhaustive adjudication of the complete participation-type source Mark^A, and unconditional equality of D16 with the complete Mark^A;  
　　the Fld of the 15 grid groups;  
　　the qualification of any candidate on a real host, and extrapolation of the frozen SNN verdicts to neuromorphic hardware or biological neural tissue;  
　　criteria for classes outside 𝒦\_g (gating extensions, continuous class, hybrid class);  
　　“relying on internal continuation alone, a field sooner or later becomes a dead field” (open item U4, not a premise of any verdict).  
　Evidence levels: mathematical proof and finite verification are kept apart; the three columns A, B, C for candidate hosts are separate, and model verdicts, conditional inferences on real hosts and empirical certification do not substitute for one another.

10\. Open Items  
　U1  The certification criterion for overdetermined cases: whether to adopt B\_OD (the NESS reading); whether to adopt, for ⊥\_OD cases, the weaker reading “an actual nonzero contribution counts as participation”. A supplement to the ontological source reading, not yet declared.  
　U2  Whether the route of action of a source ends at the Structural Position: whether the source of an earlier signal for a subsequent change can be certified along Trace-Responses already left (N′: it ends at the Structural Position, and the subsequent action is continuation; Ψ: it can continue, certified by removal comparison). The illustrating example is the separating example of 5.8 (r({y}) = 0\[N′, EF1′\] under N′, r({y}) = 1\[Ψ\] under Ψ). A supplement to the ontological source reading, not yet declared; while the choice is open, N′ serves only as a pending antecedent of negative determination. The positive determination for frozen SNN Instance P does not involve this item.  
　U3  The correspondence of B\_FD\[𝒟₁\] and of EF1′: the former is the object-level correspondence of the tier (b) 0, the latter says that the auxiliary positions bear no Trace-Responses; neither is proved upstream.  
　U4  “Relying on internal continuation alone, a field sooner or later becomes a dead field”: a proposition awaiting argument, not a premise of any adjudication.  
　U5  Exact adjudication on 𝒟\_m (combinatorial enumeration plus polyhedra, or conditions under which 𝒟\_m and 𝒟₁ give the same functional dependence on 𝒦\_g); closing the interpretation of the ⊥\_Open region.  
　U6  The individuation of clipping (separate or joint).  
　U7  Exact adjudication of the 15 grid groups with W0 = 1/2 on the 8-position T\_max.  
　U8  Class definitions of the gating-class extensions, and whether the monotonicity of Lemma C holds under them: third-factor gating, network gating (the gating argument a nonlinear function of the conductances), the Preisach type, multiplicative laws.  
　U9  Criteria for the continuous class and the hybrid class (continuous transport plus threshold events); transfer of PS-type certification beyond 𝒦\_g.  
　U10  The mode of declaration of closed-loop hosts under Theorem B3, and the material implementation of environment clamping.  
　U11  Section 8 of the General Form leaves unanswered “whether all Trace-Responses left by in-field continuation can be traced back to signals; the relation between initial conditions and endogenous pulses”; this remains open.

11\. Interfaces  
　*The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* (registration of upstream interfaces):  
　　G-r1  The evaluation rule for r at the R layer: the R-layer Trace is an evidence value (D7), and there is no evaluation rule for r. Registered: at the R layer, Src\_R(T) = \[∃ k ∈ T, Mark\_R(k) ≠ ∅\], with Mark\_R evaluated by participation-type source, certification requiring an actual action chain and a proof of source bearing; criterion PS, with Lemmas CH₀, CH and Theorem COR, can serve as the instance for the gating class.  
　　G-r2  D16 and 8.2: D16 itself is unchanged. Registered: EF-σ(T) is the condition of applicability for reading back a 0 of Src\_F as r = 0; its relation to 8.2(6) of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* (encoding fidelity) is this: 8.2(6) explicitly lists, as evaluated on R-layer witnesses, the cases “wherever the Trace-Responses determine the occurrence, timing or position of an action”; every case in which a signal participates through an actual route of action in a write by a carrier inside B (EF-σ then fails, see 8.1(i) of this paper) is a case of r under the same encoding-fidelity clause, not explicitly listed there, and is evaluated on R-layer witnesses; EF-σ must be verified case by case; under 8.2(1)(3), Src\_F = 1 is always sound.  
　　G-r3  Registration of readings: the “two kinds of elements” of Section 3 of the General Form are not mutually exclusive under the declared source reading; the choice between N′ and Ψ (U2); B\_OD (U1); EF1′ (U3); the item left unanswered in Section 8 of the General Form (U11).  
　*The General Form of Field Concretization*: the declared source reading serves as the source reading of (r); the in-boundary self-feedback type of Section 7 is adjudicated on 𝒦\_g by Theorems B1–B3.  
　*The Adjudication Form of the Sedimentation-Field*: on 𝒦\_g this paper supplies negative determination outside class F (tier (a), and tier (b) carrying B\_FD), and is compatible with the verdicts of that paper on Ω\_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS\_act ∩ NN\_act; EF4 is replaced by “R3 + DOM\[𝒟₁\] + B\_FD\[𝒟₁\]”; the T\_max reduction of Lemma 9 holds on 𝒦\_g.  
　When the verdicts and certificates of the frozen SNN (7.2, 7.5) are cited as the first SNN case, they carry the antecedents listed in 7.2 and the algorithmic-level limits.  
　*The Master Plan and Results Archive for the Verification of the 17 Hosts* (the evidence records of each candidate): Section 8 lists the verdicts and evidence levels of each candidate in columns A, B, C; the material verdicts are ⊥, and there is no verdict of 0.

## Appendix A  Program Source

The following 18 programs run in the same directory; they use only the Python standard library, with no third-party dependencies, and all numerical values are computed exactly with fractions.Fraction. A.1 is the frozen SNN program and is only loaded and checked; A.4, A.9, A.13 and A.17 are only imported; the rest are entry points to be run. SNN\_DIR in A.2 points to the directory where the programs reside, so A.1 must be in the same directory. The programs give finite verification and explicit witnesses; random generation and grids cover only their generating domains, and universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text. In the program output, the “strict subdomain” column is the classification of A.13 (S*₀: same-beat chain, removal witness and P4R ∧ P4Z; S*₁: cross-beat chain from a resting history start; ⊥\_aux: P4R holds but P4Z does not; S: the rest with a chain and a removal witness; “1\[strict\]” is the positive-determination mark of this classification), and the “PS” column is the determination by criterion PS of the main text; the main text adopts only the latter, and the former serves as a comparison for Theorem SUB.  
A.1  snn\_core.py  Frozen SNN program (only loaded and checked, its SHA-256 checked before loading; the object checked in Proposition 1)

~~~python
"""Frozen SNN instance s1: a replayable SNN instance (program host). Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library, deterministic, replayable event by event.
Host instance χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W):
  P this program; L the algorithmic level (discrete time steps); B = {4 input neurons, 2 output LIF neurons, 8 synapses}; W the declared window.
  Input-neuron firing is fixed by an external stimulus sequence (out-of-boundary signal); the teaching current teach is injected into output 0 only during the formation phase (out-of-boundary signal).
  Output neurons: LIF, v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext; when v ≥ θ the neuron fires and resets to 0.
  Plasticity: pair-based STDP (trace form): presynaptic traces x_i and postsynaptic traces y_j are multiplied by DEC each step and incremented by 1 on firing;
    when postsynaptic neuron j fires (via the backpropagated signal reaching the synapse), w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i; when presynaptic neuron i fires, w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j;
    weights are clipped to [0, W_MAX]. Optional relaxation toward baseline (RELAX, default 0).
  Intervention switches: plastic (plasticity on/off); bap (whether postsynaptic firing propagates back to the synapse; when off, STDP does not see postsynaptic firing, i.e. the route is cut).
Trace-Response: above-baseline weight z_ij = w_ij − W0.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """One step: returns (new state, event record). The event record lists presynaptic firing, postsynaptic firing and the plasticity components of each synapse (positive, negative, relaxation)."""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist: [(input name, teaching current)]. Returns (final state, event log)."""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """Fingerprint of the frozen log: SHA-256 of the events serialized in a fixed order."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.2  gate\_class.py  Definitions 1–3 of 𝒦\_g and beat-by-beat verification of Proposition 1 (3.1)

~~~python
"""Independent implementation of the formal definition of the event-gating class 𝒦_g (main text 3.1, Definitions 1–3), and beat-by-beat finite verification that “the frozen SNN is a member of 𝒦_g” (main text, Proposition 1).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 gate_class.py
The frozen snn_core.py (Appendix A.1) is loaded only as the object under check; its SHA-256 is verified before loading.
Parameters of a K_g member G (Definition 1):
  n_in, n_out          number of entry units, number of gating units; Structural Positions X = {(i, j)} (fully connected)
  pat: Σ_in -> {0,1}^n_in  entry letter to entry pulse vector (including rest 'none' -> zero vector)
  lam[j], theta[j], vreset[j]   leak, threshold, reset value
  dx[i], dy[j]         eligibility-trace decay
  Ap, Am, rho          potentiation amplitude, depression amplitude, relaxation rate
  b[i][j], lo[i][j], hi[i][j]   baseline, valid interval
Protocol switches (declared items, not part of the state): plastic, bap
Letter σ = (entry name, I vector); I is the out-of-boundary injection (teaching current).
"""
import sys, hashlib, random, itertools, importlib.util
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
SNN_DIR = __import__('os').path.dirname(__import__('os').path.abspath(__file__))   # the frozen snn_core.py sits in the same directory as this program (Appendix A.1)
FROZEN_SHA = {'snn_core.py': '4e0fd45d2442890ecf3a7687ea285f89ed161e6fb4ab1083ca0fb79821960e49'}

class Gate:
    """A member of K_g (Definition 1). State o = (w, v, x, y); w[i][j] are the Structural Positions, v, x, y the auxiliary positions."""
    def __init__(self, n_in, n_out, pat, lam, theta, vreset, dx, dy, Ap, Am, rho, b, lo, hi):
        self.n_in, self.n_out, self.pat = n_in, n_out, dict(pat)
        self.lam, self.theta, self.vreset = list(lam), list(theta), list(vreset)
        self.dx, self.dy = list(dx), list(dy)
        self.Ap, self.Am, self.rho = Ap, Am, rho
        self.b, self.lo, self.hi = b, lo, hi
        self.check_domain()
    def check_domain(self):
        """Parameter constraints of Definition 1; anything that violates them is not a K_g member."""
        assert 'none' in self.pat and all(v == 0 for v in self.pat['none'])
        assert all(len(p) == self.n_in and all(c in (0, 1) for c in p) for p in self.pat.values())
        assert all(0 <= l <= 1 for l in self.lam) and all(0 <= d <= 1 for d in self.dx + self.dy)
        assert 0 <= self.rho <= 1 and self.Ap >= 0 and self.Am >= 0
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                assert self.lo[i][j] <= self.b[i][j] <= self.hi[i][j]
        return True
    # ---------- Definition 2: per-beat execution order (eight stages, reading the pre-beat state) ----------
    def step(self, o, sigma, plastic=True, bap=True):
        """Returns (o', rec). rec lists the gate arguments vt, g, and for each synapse (P+, P-, Pr, pre-clip sum, post-clip value)."""
        w, v, x, y = o
        name, I = sigma
        s = self.pat[name]                                                     # (1) read input
        vt = [self.lam[j] * v[j] + sum(w[i][j] * s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)) + I[j]
              for j in range(self.n_out)]                                      # (2) integrate (pre-beat w)
        g = [1 if vt[j] >= self.theta[j] else 0 for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (3) gate, comparison >=
        v2 = [self.vreset[j] if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]    # (4) reset
        x2 = [self.dx[i] * x[i] + s[i] for i in range(self.n_in)]              # (5) eligibility traces
        y2 = [self.dy[j] * y[j] + g[j] for j in range(self.n_out)]
        w2 = [[None] * self.n_out for _ in range(self.n_in)]
        comp = {}
        for i in range(self.n_in):
            for j in range(self.n_out):
                pp = self.Ap * x2[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else F(0)   # (6) potentiation: only if g_j = 1
                pm = -self.Am * y2[j] if (plastic and bap and s[i]) else F(0)  #     depression: only if s_i = 1
                pr = -self.rho * (w[i][j] - self.b[i][j]) if (plastic and self.rho != 0) else F(0)
                S = w[i][j] + pp + pm + pr
                nw = min(self.hi[i][j], max(self.lo[i][j], S))                 # (7) clipping
                w2[i][j] = nw
                comp[(i, j)] = (pp, pm, pr, S, nw)
        rec = dict(s=s, I=tuple(I), vt=vt, g=tuple(g), comp=comp)              # (8) read out g
        return (w2, v2, x2, y2), rec
    def init(self, w=None):
        return ([[self.b[i][j] if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(self.n_out)] for i in range(self.n_in)],
                [F(0)] * self.n_out, [F(0)] * self.n_in, [F(0)] * self.n_out)
    def run(self, hist, o=None, **kw):
        o = o or self.init()
        traj, recs = [o], []
        for sig in hist:
            o, r = self.step(o, sig, **kw)
            traj.append(o); recs.append(r)
        return traj, recs

def snn_params(S):
    """Reads the K_g parameters from the current global constants of the snn_core module (correspondence between Definition 1 and lines 15–20 of snn_core; main text, Proposition 1)."""
    n_in, n_out = S.N_IN, S.N_OUT
    full = lambda val: [[val] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    return dict(n_in=n_in, n_out=n_out, pat=S.PAT, lam=[S.LEAK] * n_out, theta=[S.THETA] * n_out,
                vreset=[F(0)] * n_out, dx=[S.DEC] * n_in, dy=[S.DEC] * n_out, Ap=S.A_PLUS, Am=S.A_MINUS,
                b=full(S.W0), lo=full(F(0)), hi=full(S.W_MAX))

def load_snn_core():
    p = SNN_DIR + '/snn_core.py'
    h = hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
~~~
