~~~python
        print(f'  {lab}: available beat–position pairs where the gating reads T: {len(cs)}, of which {len(ins)} have the threshold-crossing point in the interior of the valid domain; with J ∈ εℤ: {sum(c["Jint"] for c in ins)}')
        for c in ins:
            print(f'    beat {c["t"]}{" (in W)" if c["inW"] else " (before W)"} position w{c["k"][1]}{c["k"][2]}: w* = {GK.fr(c["ws"])}, a = {GK.fr(c["a"])}, J = {GK.fr(c["J"])}, J/ε = {float(c["J"] / EPS):.6f}')
    print('3. Finite verification of Lemma Q (the jumps of an ε-threshold-truncated sum lie in εℤ)')
    pts, viol = lemma_Q_check()
    print(f'  3000 random mechanism sets, candidate jump points {pts}, jumps not in εℤ: {viol}')
    print('4. Conclusion: at beat 26 of Instance P the antecedents of N1 and N2 hold (a > ε; w* ∈ (b, hi); J/ε ∉ ℤ); they hold likewise at beat 5 of N. '
          'By the proof in main text 3.6, under E1–E4 (depth is the Trace-Response, BEAT, LOC exclusivity, exactness) the host of the frozen SNN admits no exact F encoding (other encoding routes lie outside this conclusion); '
          'Theorems 6 and 7 cannot be applied directly through an exact encoding.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.8  po9\_witness.py  Comparison of Instances P, N, O1 and O2, verification of Lemmas C and C′ (5.3, 7.5)

~~~python
"""Item-by-item comparison, on Instances P, N, O1 and O2, of the verdict path of Chapter 3 with the criteria of this paper (main text 7.5); finite verification of Lemmas C and C′ (main text 5.3).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 po9_witness.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import nonf_encoding as NF
EPS, NONE = GK.EPS, GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def report_instance(I, title, nonF=True):
    G = I.G
    print(f'[{title}] {I.name}')
    print(f'  history length {len(I.hist)}; W = beats {I.t0}–{I.t1}; entry beat {I.t0 - 1}; T = w00, w10; ε = {EPS}')
    print('  formation beats of T (D16):', {wname(k): I.form[k] for k in I.T}, '; first available beat (D17):',
          {wname(k): (min(I.avail[k]) if I.avail[k] else None) for k in I.T})
    print('  firing beats of output 0 in W:', [t for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 1) if I.gates[t][0]],
          '; range of z_T in W:', {wname(k): (fr(min(GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2))),
                                       fr(max(GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) for t in range(I.t0, I.t1 + 2)))) for k in I.T})
    ev = GK.evaluate(I, part_all=True)
    print(f'  legitimate-setting runs {ev["nruns"]} (including the two envelope pins); first gating-flip beat of the pins: {ev["pins"]}')
    print('  item-by-item evaluation of the events in W whose action target lies in T:')
    print('    event@beat | component c | KO difference | a | Path | Dep | η (basis)')
    for r in ev['rows']:
        e = r['e']
        print(f'    {e["id"][0]}{e["id"][1]}@{e["t"]} | {fr(e["c"])} | {fr(r["d"]) if r["d"] is not None else "—"} | {r["a"]} | '
              f'{r["path"]} | {r["dep"]} | {r["eta"]} ({r["why"]})')
    if not ev['rows']:
        print('    (none)')
    print(f'  criteria of this paper (principal reading): HDyn = {ev["HDyn"]}; Part = {ev["Part"]} (witness {ev["Part_wit"]})')
    print(f'  conservative reading (⊥_Over recorded where overdetermined): HDyn = {ev["HDyn_cons"]}; Part = {ev["Part_cons"]}; T events that are action-effective but overdetermined: {ev["n_over"]}')
    for k, (ret, form, zz) in ev['q'].items():
        print(f'  q({wname(k)}): window-end z = {fr(zz)}, Ret = {ret}, Form = {form} ⇒ q = {ret and form}')
    print(f'  r: the R-layer Mark rule is not established, judged {GK.RDEF} (this program does not evaluate r; for r see Appendix A.14); Ident = 1 (POS, the Structural Position is the synapse); HistEv = 1 (REC); '
          f'Trace = {ev["Trace"]}; Q(T) = {ev["Q"]}')
    th = GK.third_path(I, nonF)
    print('  verdict path of Chapter 3:')
    for s in th['steps']:
        print('    ·', s)
    print(f'  verdicts of Chapter 3: q = {[th["q"][k] for k in I.T]}; r = {th["r"]}; Part = {th["Part"]}; HDyn = {th["HDyn"]}; Fld = {th["Fld"]}')
    for lab, ext in (('E1 (entry as a constant law within B)', False), ('E2 (T entry as an out-of-boundary pulse)', True)):
        fl = GK.f_layer(I, I.T, ext)
        print(f'  488-type F encoding {lab}: Src = {fl["Src"]}, Ret = {fl["Ret"]}, Trace_F = {fl["Trace"]}, Part_F = {fl["Part"]}, '
              f'HDyn_F = {fl["HDyn"]}, Q_F = {fl["Q"]}; deviations from the program z_T trajectory: {len(fl["devs"])}'
              + (f' (first at beat {fl["devs"][0][0]}, difference {fr(fl["devs"][0][2])}: subthreshold components are not booked, 4.2)' if fl['devs'] else '')
              + (f'; {fl["dom_err"]}' if fl['dom_err'] else ''))
    return ev, th

def ef4_direct(I):
    """Beats at which a same-beat legitimate local setting changes the gating within W (direct reading of the “occurrence determined by the Trace-Response” that EF4 excludes)."""
    runs, pins, _ = I.cf()
    found = []
    for r in runs:
        if r['lab'].startswith('pin'):
            continue
        t = r['u']
        if t >= I.t0 and r['gates'][t] != I.gates[t]:
            found.append((t, r['ks'], r['lab'], I.gates[t][0], r['gates'][t][0]))
    return found

def pin_lemma_check(seed=20261102, n_inst=80):
    """Finite verification of Lemmas C and C′: random K_g members (variants of the SNN parameter family), random histories, random T, random settable sets A ⊆ T × beats;
    when neither of the two envelope pins (set hi throughout A; on A, set b wherever the value is above b) produces a gating flip on [u0, t],
    no legitimate multi-beat setting (any subset of A, values random rationals in [b, hi]) produces a gating flip on [u0, t], and events of the kinds covered by Lemma C are unchanged."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(windows=0, nopin=0, samples=0, viol=0, ev_checked=0)
    for _ in range(n_inst):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 2)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1)]
        L = 24
        hist = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), rng.choice(tv))) for _ in range(L)]
        o0 = ([[F(rng.randint(0, 16), 8) for _ in range(2)] for _ in range(4)], [F(0)] * 2, [F(0)] * 4, [F(0)] * 2)
        pre, evs, gates = GK.run_with(G, o0, hist)
        Tq = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(4) for j in range(2)], 2)
        Iq = type('Q', (), {})(); Iq.T = Tq; Iq.gates = gates; Iq.hist = hist; Iq.G = G
        for _w in range(4):
            u0 = rng.randint(0, 16); t = rng.randint(u0, L - 1)
            A = {(k, u) for k in Tq for u in range(u0, t + 1) if rng.random() < 0.7}
            if not A:
                continue
            st['windows'] += 1
            def pinrun(lab):
                def iv(tt, o):
                    a = {}
                    for k in Tq:
                        if (k, tt) in A:
                            if lab == 'hi':
                                a[k] = G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]
                            elif o[0][k[1]][k[2]] > G.b[k[1]][k[2]]:
                                a[k] = G.b[k[1]][k[2]]
                    return GK.setw(o, a) if a else o
                return GK.run_with(G, pre[u0], hist[u0:t + 1], t_start=u0, interv=iv)
            nop = all(all(gg[tt] == gates[tt] for tt in gg) for (_, _, gg) in (pinrun('hi'), pinrun('lo')))
            st['nopin'] += nop
            for _s in range(15):
                sel = [a for a in A if rng.random() < 0.4] or [next(iter(A))]
                vals = {a: G.b[a[0][1]][a[0][2]] + F(rng.randint(0, 64), 64) * (G.hi[a[0][1]][a[0][2]] - G.b[a[0][1]][a[0][2]]) for a in sel}
                def iv(tt, o):
                    a = {k: vals[(k, tt)] for k in Tq if (k, tt) in vals}
                    return GK.setw(o, a) if a else o
                _, ee, gg = GK.run_with(G, pre[u0], hist[u0:t + 1], t_start=u0, interv=iv)
                st['samples'] += 1
                if not nop:
                    continue
                flip = any(gg[tt] != gates[tt] for tt in gg)
                changed = False
                for tt in ee:
                    for eid in set(ee[tt]) | set(evs[tt]):
                        e = evs[tt].get(eid) or ee[tt].get(eid)
                        if GK.covered(Iq, e, u0):
                            st['ev_checked'] += 1
                            if (eid in ee[tt]) != (eid in evs[tt]) or (eid in ee[tt] and ee[tt][eid]['c'] != evs[tt][eid]['c']):
                                changed = True
                if flip or changed:
                    st['viol'] += 1
    return st

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 frozen declared history (FORM + REST + WIN)', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), len(FORM) + len(REST))
    N = GK.Instance('s1 parameters; history (A+teach 1, B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 declared history, W = (A+teach 1, rest, rest)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], len(FORM) + len(REST))
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('s1 parameters, formation once; entry beat A+teach; W = (B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], len(FORM1) + 8)
    print('0. Non-F-encodability of the host (nonf_encoding.py): the antecedents of N1 and N2 hold at beat 26 of P and beat 5 of N ⇒ the SEP of Chapter 3 cannot be declared truthfully')
    for lab, I in (('P', P), ('N', N)):
        ins = [c for c in NF.crossings(I) if c['inside']]
        print(f'  {lab}: threshold-crossing points in the interior of the valid domain {len(ins)}, with J ∉ εℤ {sum(not c["Jint"] for c in ins)}')
    print()
    out = {}
    for I, lab, title in ((P, 'P', 'Instance P: positive-determination witness (threshold crossing of the gating determines occurrence; the strength does not read the Trace-Response)'),
                          (N, 'N', 'Instance N: negative-determination witness (the two readings agree)'),
                          (O1, 'O1', 'Instance O1: same-beat overdetermination (illustration of diverging readings)'),
                          (O2, 'O2', 'Instance O2: cross-beat overdetermination via reset (illustration of diverging readings)')):
        out[lab] = report_instance(I, title)
        print()
    print('1. Direct readings of the case excluded by EF4 (a same-beat legitimate setting changes the gating within W)')
    for I, lab in ((P, 'P'), (N, 'N'), (O1, 'O1'), (O2, 'O2')):
        f = ef4_direct(I)
        f0 = f[0] if f else None
        print(f'  {lab}: beats at which the gating changes {sorted(set(x[0] for x in f))}; example: ' +
              (f'beat {f0[0]} set {",".join(wname(k) for k in f0[1])}←{f0[2]}, g0 {f0[3]}→{f0[4]}' if f0 else 'none'))
    print('2. Check of Dep = 1 ⇒ Path = 1 (corollary of Lemma R3) on all evaluated events')
    bad = cnt = 0
    for I in (P, N, O1, O2):
        for e in I.Wevents():
            ev_, why, dv, pv, over = GK.eta(I, e)
            cnt += 1
            bad += (dv == 1 and pv == 0)
    print(f'  events {cnt}, violations {bad}')
    print('3. Summary comparison table (Chapter 3 / this paper, principal reading / this paper, conservative reading / 488-type F encoding E2)')
    for lab, I in (('P', P), ('N', N), ('O1', O1), ('O2', O2)):
        ev, th = out[lab]
        fl = GK.f_layer(I, I.T, True)
        print(f'  {lab}: Part {th["Part"]} / {ev["Part"]} / {ev["Part_cons"]} / {fl["Part"]}; '
              f'HDyn {th["HDyn"]} / {ev["HDyn"]} / {ev["HDyn_cons"]} / {fl["HDyn"]}; '
              f'q {[th["q"][k] for k in I.T]} / {ev["qv"]}; Fld {th["Fld"]} / Q(T) {ev["Q"]}')
    print("4. Finite verification of Lemmas C and C′ (the envelope-pin lemma and its reset extension)")
    st = pin_lemma_check()
    print(f'  80 random instances, windows {st["windows"]}, with no gating flip under either pin {st["nopin"]}; random legitimate multi-beat settings {st["samples"]}; '
          f'comparisons of events of covered kinds {st["ev_checked"]}; violations {st["viol"]}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.9  po4\_compat.py  Random-generation library for the intersection-compatibility comparison (5.7)

~~~python
"""Random generation for the intersection-compatibility contrast, and an independent implementation of the Chapter 3, 4.6 verdicts on host states (library, imported by Appendix A.10; its standalone run results do not serve as verification of Theorem C4′).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic.
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE

def in_omega(I, D):
    """(i): whether the symbolic run from every settable beat is single-branch and free of clipping."""
    G = I.G
    if any(e['id'][0] == 'CLIP' for e in I.Wevents()):
        return False
    for u in D.complete_u:
        S = D.avail_T(u)
        names = [('s',) + k for k in S]
        w0 = {(i, j): DC.A0(I.pre[u][0][i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)}
        R = []
        for k, nm in zip(S, names):
            w0[(k[1], k[2])] = DC.avar(nm)
            R.append(DC.le0(DC.aadd(DC.A0(G.b[k[1]][k[2]]), DC.ascale(DC.avar(nm), -1))))
            R.append(DC.le0(DC.aadd(DC.avar(nm), DC.A0(-G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]))))
        st = (w0, [DC.A0(c) for c in I.pre[u][1]], list(I.pre[u][2]), list(I.pre[u][3]))
        for t in range(u, I.t1 + 1):
            br = DC.sym_step(G, st, R, I.hist[t], **I.kw)
            if len(br) != 1 or any(k[0] == 'CLIP' for k in br[0][2]):
                return False
            R, st = br[0][0], br[0][1]
    return True

def ef4p(I, D):
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if D.fd1_value(e) == 1 and not any(k in I.T and I.is_avail(k, e['t']) for k in e['reads'] if k[0] == 'w'):
            return False
    return True

def third_46(I):
    """Independent implementation of Chapter 3, 4.6 (Y₂, model mode; INV-c, REC, ENT, POS declared true)."""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    hd, pt, atoms = [], [], {}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        t = e['t']
        kind = e['id'][0]
        cand = [k for k in T if I.is_avail(k, t)]
        # Mechanism declaration: those acting on Structural Positions are separable mechanisms; among those acting on auxiliary positions only SYN and RESET are OUT-f read processes, the rest are zero constant laws with Rd = ∅
        if e['tgt'][0] == 'w':
            a, d = GK.ko_act(G, I.pre[t], I.evs[t], e['id'])
            act = 1 if abs(d) > EPS else 0
            resp, tried = 0, 0
            for k in cand:
                for val in (G.b[k[1]][k[2]], G.hi[k[1]][k[2]]):
                    o2 = GK.setw(I.pre[t], {k: val})
                    if val == I.pre[t][0][k[1]][k[2]]:
                        continue
                    ev2 = RW.Ev(G, o2, I.hist[t], t, **I.kw)
                    # Threshold-truncated booked term ĉ = c·[|c| > ε] ([3] 133)
                    c0 = e['c'] if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
                    c1 = ev2[e['id']]['c'] if e['id'] in ev2 else 0
                    c1 = c1 if abs(c1) > EPS else 0
                    tried += 1
                    resp = resp or (c1 != c0)
                    break
            if resp:
                hc = 1
            elif not cand:
                hc = 0
            elif act == 1:
                hc = 0 if tried == len(cand) else '⊥_Obs'
            else:
                hc = '⊥_Sub'
            atoms[e['id']] = (act, hc)
            if e['tgt'] in T:
                hd.append(GK.k_and(act, hc))
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(hc if act == 1 or hc == 1 else (0 if not cand else hc))
        else:
            rdT = [k for k in e['reads'] if k in T and I.is_avail(k, t)]
            if kind in ('SYN', 'RESET'):
                # OUT-f: SYN read-out = w; RESET read-out = v_reset − (λv + Σ w s + I), position-wise injective in each w read; Rd = the Structural Positions read (Theorem 6(3))
                cond = 1 if rdT else 0
            elif kind == 'YIN':
                cond = '⊥_Sub' if rdT else 0              # read-out is identically 1, OUT-f not satisfied; no response while the gating is unchanged (4.6(3))
            else:
                cond = 0                                  # LEAK, YDEC, XDEC, XIN, INJ read no Structural Position: zero constant laws with Rd = ∅
            atoms[e['id']] = (0, cond)
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(cond)
    return GK.k_or(pt), GK.k_or(hd), atoms

def main(n_try=400, seed=20261103):
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    st = dict(tried=0, omega=0, agree_part=0, agree_hd=0, atom_cmp=0, atom_dis=0, tierb=0, nontriv_hd1=0, nontriv_part1=0)
    dis = []
    for _ in range(n_try):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['theta'] = [F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]))] * 2
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 4)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3)]
        form = [('A', (F(3), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 2)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        st['tried'] += 1
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=20000)
        if not in_omega(I, D) or not ef4p(I, D):
            continue
        st['omega'] += 1
        ours = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        P3, H3, atoms = third_46(I)
        st['agree_part'] += (ours['Part'] == P3)
        st['agree_hd'] += (ours['HDyn'] == H3)
        st['conf'] = st.get('conf', 0) + ((ours['Part'], P3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0))) + ((ours['HDyn'], H3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0)))
        st['refine'] = st.get('refine', 0) + (isinstance(P3, str) and ours['Part'] in (0, 1)) + (isinstance(H3, str) and ours['HDyn'] in (0, 1))
        st['nontriv_hd1'] += (H3 == 1)
        st['nontriv_part1'] += (P3 == 1)
        for r in ours['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            a3, h3 = atoms[e['id']]
            if h3 in (0, 1):
                st['atom_cmp'] += 1
                st['tierb'] += (r['tier'] == 'b')
                if r['v'] != h3:
                    st['atom_dis'] += 1
                    dis.append((e['id'], r['v'], h3, r['tier']))
        if ours['Part'] != P3 or ours['HDyn'] != H3:
            dis.append(('verdict', ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
    print('Intersection-compatibility contrast (random K_g members and histories; only those satisfying Ω_F and EF4′ item by item)')
    print(f'  random instances with available positions {st["tried"]}, of which in Ω_F ∩ EF4′ {st["omega"]}')
    print(f'  Part equal {st["agree_part"]}/{st["omega"]} (Chapter 3 gives 1: {st["nontriv_part1"]}); HDyn equal {st["agree_hd"]}/{st["omega"]} (Chapter 3 gives 1: {st["nontriv_hd1"]})')
    print(f'  conflicts between verdicts 1 and 0: {st.get("conf", 0)}; Chapter 3 ⊥ (⊥_Sub etc.) but decided in this paper: {st.get("refine", 0)} (information-order refinement, not conflict)')
    print(f'  atom-by-atom comparison (atoms where Chapter 3 takes 0 or 1) {st["atom_cmp"]}, differing {st["atom_dis"]}; of these, issued 0 in this paper via tier (b) (FD = 0, B_FD) {st["tierb"]}')
    for d in dis[:10]:
        print('   disagreement:', d)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.10  po4\_ns.py  Truncation counterexample, Lemma NS, re-verification on the subdomain of Theorem C4′ (5.7)

~~~python
"""Intersection compatibility (main text 5.7): value-by-value reproduction and regression of the resolution-truncation counterexample, verification of Lemma NS (identical trajectories ⇔ the sum of truncated components is 0 at every beat and position), and re-verification on the Theorem C4′ subdomain Ω_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS_act ∩ NN_act.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 po4_ns.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
import po4_compat as PC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def f_run(I):
    """Actual F run of E_F (T positions only; Mathematical Form 4.2: only components with |c| > ε are booked). Returns (sF[t][k], truncated sum per beat and position, F formation records, nonnegativity violations)."""
    G = I.G
    sF = {0: {k: GK.zk(G, I.pre[0], k) for k in I.T}}
    drop, formF, neg = {}, {k: list(x for x in I.form[k] if x < 0) for k in I.T}, []
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        nxt = dict(sF[t])
        for k in I.T:
            dsum = F(0)
            for e in I.evs[t].values():
                if e['tgt'] != k:
                    continue
                kind = e['id'][0]
                if kind == 'RELAX':
                    c = -I.G.rho * sF[t][k]
                elif kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS'):
                    c = e['c']                       # constant law with declared occurrence (value taken from the actual history)
                elif kind == 'CLIP':
                    c = e['c']                       # does not occur within Ω_F
                else:
                    continue
                hc = e['c']
                if 0 < abs(hc) <= EPS:
                    dsum += hc
                if abs(c) > EPS:
                    if sF[t][k] <= EPS < sF[t][k] + c:
                        formF[k].append(t)
                    nxt[k] = nxt[k] + c
            drop[(t, k)] = dsum
            if nxt[k] < 0:
                neg.append((t + 1, k))
        sF[t + 1] = nxt
    return sF, drop, formF, neg

def ns_act(I, drop):
    return all(v == 0 for v in drop.values())

def same_traj(I, sF):
    return all(sF[t][k] == GK.zk(I.G, I.pre[t], k) for t in sF for k in I.T)

def third_46_F(I, sF, formF):
    """Evaluation of Chapter 3, 4.6 on the states of the F run of E_F itself (available positions, local responses and knockout differences all taken on F states; laws of E_F: PLUS/MINUS constant, RELAX = −ρ·s,
    SYN and RESET OUT-f reads (Rd = the Structural Positions read), YIN read-out constant, the other auxiliary-position processes zero constant laws with Rd = ∅)."""
    G, T = I.G, I.T
    av = lambda k, t: sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])
    hd, pt, atoms = [], [], {}
    for e in I.Wevents():
        t, kind = e['t'], e['id'][0]
        cand = [k for k in T if av(k, t)]
        if e['tgt'][0] == 'w':
            k0 = e['tgt']
            if kind == 'RELAX' and k0 in T:
                c0 = -G.rho * sF[t][k0]
                cut = lambda c: c if abs(c) > EPS else 0
                act = 1 if abs(c0) > EPS else 0
                resp = False
                if k0 in cand:                       # one legitimate perturbation per candidate position (first set depth 0; if that equals the original value, set hi)
                    s_new = F(0) if sF[t][k0] != 0 else G.hi[k0[1]][k0[2]] - G.b[k0[1]][k0[2]]
                    resp = cut(-G.rho * s_new) != cut(c0)
            else:                                    # PLUS, MINUS (constant laws), processes on positions outside T: read no candidate position in T, no response
                act = 1 if abs(e['c']) > EPS else 0
                resp = False
            hc = 1 if resp else (0 if not cand else (0 if act == 1 else '⊥_Sub'))
            atoms[e['id']] = (act, hc)
            if e['tgt'] in T:
                hd.append(GK.k_and(act, hc))
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(hc if act == 1 or hc == 1 else (0 if not cand else hc))
        else:
            rdT = [k for k in e['reads'] if k in T and av(k, t)]
            if kind in ('SYN', 'RESET'):
                cond = 1 if rdT else 0
            elif kind == 'YIN':
                cond = '⊥_Sub' if rdT else 0
            else:
                cond = 0
            atoms[e['id']] = (0, cond)
            if e['car'] == RW.B:
                pt.append(cond)
    return GK.k_or(pt), GK.k_or(hd), atoms

def ent_instance(name, G, hist, t0, T, o0):
    I = GK.Instance(name, G, hist, t0, T=T, o0=o0)
    for k in T:
        if GK.zk(G, o0, k) > EPS:
            I.form[k] = [-1] + I.form[k]
    I.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(hist) + 1) if GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in I.form[k])] for k in T}
    return I

def section_counter(S):
    print('1. Value-by-value reproduction of the truncation counterexample (ε = 1/100, z = 3/200, ρ = 1/2, rest; T = {w00}, formative history registered by ENT at entry beat −1)')
    G = GC.Gate(rho=F(1, 2), **GC.snn_params(S))
    o0 = G.init()
    o0[0][0][0] = G.b[0][0] + F(3, 200)
    hist = [NONE] * 4
    I = ent_instance('counterexample', G, hist, 0, [('w', 0, 0)], o0)
    sF, drop, formF, neg = f_run(I)
    k = ('w', 0, 0)
    e0 = I.evs[0][('RELAX', (0, 0), 0)]
    print(f'  beat 0: host RELAX00 component c = {fr(e0["c"])}, |c| = {fr(abs(e0["c"]))} < ε; no firing, no other write, no clipping (clipping events: {sum(1 for e in I.evs[0].values() if e["id"][0] == "CLIP")})')
    print(f'  beat 1: host z = {fr(GK.zk(G, I.pre[1], k))}; F run of E_F s = {fr(sF[1][k])}; difference {fr(sF[1][k] - GK.zk(G, I.pre[1], k))}')
    for t in range(len(hist) + 1):
        zh = GK.zk(G, I.pre[t], k)
        avh = I.is_avail(k, t)
        avf = sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])
        print(f'    beat {t}: host z = {fr(zh)} (available {int(avh)}) | F s = {fr(sF[t][k])} (available {int(avf)}) | truncated sum this beat {fr(drop.get((t, k), F(0)))}')
    zend, send = GK.zk(G, I.pre[len(hist)], k), sF[len(hist)][k]
    print(f'  window-end Ret: host {int(zend > EPS)} (z = {fr(zend)}), E_F {int(send > EPS)} (s = {fr(send)})—available positions and Ret diverge from beat 1 on; the F side stays at 3/200 thereafter (the −3/400 of every beat is truncated)')
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    print(f'  subdomain tests for this instance: Ω_F (single-branch, no clipping) {PC.in_omega(I, D)}; EF4′ {PC.ef4p(I, D)}; NS_act {ns_act(I, drop)}; identical trajectories {same_traj(I, sF)}')
    print('  Conclusion: the counterexample satisfies Ω_F—the only condition on which the equality “E_F and the host agree beat by beat within 𝒟₁” rests—yet the trajectories differ, so the equality fails.'
          ' (The counterexample itself is not in EF4′: RELAX00 at beat 1 depends on the setting at beat 0 and does not read the available positions of beat 1; the beat-by-beat agreement equality does not presuppose EF4′.)')
    # Regression 2: an instance where diverging available positions make the verdicts of Chapter 3 (evaluated on F states) and of this paper (host) differ
    G3 = GC.Gate(rho=F(1, 2), **dict(GC.snn_params(S), theta=[F(3)] * 2))
    o3 = G3.init(); o3[0][0][0] = G3.b[0][0] + F(3, 200)
    I3 = ent_instance('Regression 2', G3, [NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1, [('w', 0, 0)], o3)
    sF3, drop3, formF3, _ = f_run(I3)
    D3 = DC.Decider(I3)
    j3 = DC.judge(I3, D3, True)
    P3, H3, _ = third_46_F(I3, sF3, formF3)
    print(f'  Regression 2 (θ = 3 keeps the gating from ever firing; history (rest, A), W = beat 1): host beat 1 z = {fr(GK.zk(G3, I3.pre[1], ("w", 0, 0)))} (unavailable), F s = {fr(sF3[1][("w", 0, 0)])} (available); '
          f'Chapter 3 on the F run Part = {P3}; this paper (host) Part = {j3["Part"]}; subdomain tests: Ω_F {PC.in_omega(I3, D3)}, EF4′ {PC.ef4p(I3, D3)}, NS_act {ns_act(I3, drop3)}')
    print('  Reading: Regression 2 is not in EF4′ (SYN00 at beat 1 depends on the setting at beat 0 and does not read the available positions of beat 1), so the subdomain Ω_F ∩ EF4′ already excludes it; it shows that once the trajectories diverge, “available positions” means different things on the two sides, and the verdict comparison loses its object.')
    print()

def section_recheck(S, n_try=400, seed=20261103):
    print('2. Intersection-compatibility re-verification (same random generation and seed as po4_compat.py; Chapter 3, 4.6 now evaluated on the states of the F run of E_F itself)')
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    st = dict(tried=0, omega=0, ns=0, nn=0, good=0, eq_traj_ns=0, conf_good=0, agreeP=0, agreeH=0, atom_cmp=0, atom_dis=0,
              ns_fail_conf=0, ns_fail=0, retdiv=0, avdiv=0, rc=0)
    ex = []
    for _ in range(n_try):
        p = GC.snn_params(S)
        p['lam'] = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4)] * 2
        p['Ap'] = F(rng.randint(1, 4), 16); p['Am'] = F(rng.randint(0, 4), 16)
        p['b'] = [[F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8)] * 2 for _ in range(4)]
        p['theta'] = [F(rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]))] * 2
        G = GC.Gate(rho=rng.choice([F(0), F(1, 10), F(1, 4)]), **p)
        tv = [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3)]
        form = [('A', (F(3), 0)), NONE] * rng.randint(1, 2)
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'AB', 'none']), (rng.choice(tv), 0)) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, form + win, len(form))
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in I.T):
            continue
        st['tried'] += 1
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=20000)
        if not PC.in_omega(I, D) or not PC.ef4p(I, D):
            continue
        st['omega'] += 1
        sF, drop, formF, neg = f_run(I)
        ns, nn = ns_act(I, drop), not neg
        rc = all(e['c'] == 0 or abs(e['c']) > EPS for t in range(len(I.hist)) for e in I.evs[t].values() if e['tgt'] in I.T)
        st['ns'] += ns; st['nn'] += nn; st['rc'] += rc
        tr = same_traj(I, sF)
        assert tr == ns, 'Lemma NS equivalence fails'
        ours = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        P3, H3, atoms = third_46_F(I, sF, formF)
        conf = ((ours['Part'], P3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0))) + ((ours['HDyn'], H3) in ((0, 1), (1, 0)))
        if not ns:
            st['ns_fail'] += 1
            st['ns_fail_conf'] += (conf > 0)
            end = len(I.hist)
            st['retdiv'] += any((GK.zk(G, I.pre[end], k) > EPS) != (sF[end][k] > EPS) for k in I.T)
            st['avdiv'] += any(I.is_avail(k, t) != (sF[t][k] > EPS and any(u < t for u in formF[k])) for k in I.T for t in range(end + 1))
            if conf:
                ex.insert(0, ('conflict', [str(G.lam[0]), str(G.Ap), str(G.Am), str(G.rho), str(G.theta[0]), str(G.b[0][0])],
                              [(a, str(I_[0])) for a, I_ in I.hist], ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
            if len(ex) < 4:
                t1 = min(t for (t, k), v in drop.items() if v != 0)
                ex.append((t1, [(fr(v)) for (t, k), v in sorted(drop.items()) if v != 0][:2], ours['Part'], P3, ours['HDyn'], H3))
        if ns and nn:
            st['good'] += 1
            # on NS ∧ NN the F states equal the host states, so the F-state evaluation should agree atom by atom with the host-state evaluation of po4_compat
            P0, H0, atoms0 = PC.third_46(I)
            st['eq_traj_ns'] += (P0 == P3 and H0 == H3)
            st['conf_good'] += conf
            st['agreeP'] += (ours['Part'] == P3); st['agreeH'] += (ours['HDyn'] == H3)
            for r in ours['rows']:
                a3, h3 = atoms[r['e']['id']]
                if h3 in (0, 1):
                    st['atom_cmp'] += 1
                    st['atom_dis'] += (r['v'] != h3)
    print(f'  random instances with available positions {st["tried"]}; subdomain Ω_F ∩ EF4′ {st["omega"]}')
    print(f'  of these, NS_act holds {st["ns"]}, NN_act (the F run of E_F is nonnegative, D20) holds {st["nn"]}, both hold {st["good"]}; component-wise resolution compatibility RC (every component on T is 0 or |c| > ε) holds {st["rc"]}')
    print(f'  Lemma NS (identical trajectories ⇔ NS_act) holds on all {st["omega"]} cases (program assertion)')
    print(f'  NS_act fails in {st["ns_fail"]} cases: window-end Ret diverges {st["retdiv"]}, available positions diverge {st["avdiv"]}, 1–0 verdict conflicts (this paper on the host vs Chapter 3 on the F run) {st["ns_fail_conf"]}')
    for x in ex[:4]:
        if x[0] == 'conflict':
            print(f'    conflict case: λ, A₊, A₋, ρ, θ, b = {x[1]}; history (entry, teach0) {x[2]}; this paper Part {x[3]} / Chapter 3 (F) {x[4]}; this paper HDyn {x[5]} / Chapter 3 (F) {x[6]}')
        else:
            print(f'    case: first nonzero truncated sum at beat {x[0]} ({x[1]}); this paper Part {x[2]} / Chapter 3 (F) {x[3]}; this paper HDyn {x[4]} / Chapter 3 (F) {x[5]}')
    print(f'  intersection-compatibility subdomain Ω_F ∩ EF4′ ∩ NS_act ∩ NN_act: {st["good"]} cases; F-state and host-state evaluations equal {st["eq_traj_ns"]}/{st["good"]}; '
          f'Part equal {st["agreeP"]}, HDyn equal {st["agreeH"]}; 1–0 conflicts {st["conf_good"]}; atom by atom (Chapter 3 takes 0 or 1) {st["atom_cmp"]}, differing {st["atom_dis"]}')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    section_counter(S)
    section_recheck(S)
    print('End. The above are finite verifications and exact readings; proofs of Lemma NS and Theorem C4′ are in 5.7 of the main text.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.11  boundary\_po8.py  Witness-by-witness check of Lemma IMP-g, the verdicts of Theorem B2, closed-loop comparison (5.5, 5.6)

~~~python
"""Read-back and the in-boundary self-feedback type (main text 5.5, 5.6): witness-by-witness check that IMP holds by model structure on the program host; in-boundary self-feedback verdicts in open-loop 𝒦_g (Theorem B2); the SNN route and its severing; closed-loop contrast (Theorem B3).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 boundary_po8.py
"""
import sys, random, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def ename(e):
    return f"{e['id'][0]}{''.join(str(c) for c in e['id'][1])}@{e['t']}"

def nname(n):
    return (n[0] + ''.join(str(c) for c in n[1:]))

# ---------------- Path reconstruction on the actual event graph ----------------
def act_path(I, sources, e):
    """A path (node sequence) on Γ_act (D13: read edges, write edges, Structural-Position persistence edges; only events that actually occur) from sources = {(node, beat)} to e; None if there is none."""
    if not sources:
        return None
    t0 = min(u for _, u in sources)
    parent = {}
    reach = {}
    sources = sorted(sources)
    for (k, u) in sources:
        parent[('S', k, u)] = None
    cur = set()
    for t in range(t0, e['t'] + 1):
        for (k, u) in sources:
            if u == t and k not in cur:
                cur.add(k); parent.setdefault(('S', k, t), None)
        if t == e['t']:
            hit = sorted(k for k in e['reads'] if k in cur)
            if not hit:
                return None
            node = ('S', hit[0], t)
            path = [('E', e['id'], e['car'])]
            while node is not None:
                path.append(node)
                node = parent[node]
            return list(reversed(path))
        nxt = set()
        for k in sorted(cur):
            if k[0] == 'w':
                nxt.add(k)
                parent.setdefault(('S', k, t + 1), ('S', k, t))
        for ev in I.evs[t].values():
            hit = sorted(k for k in ev['reads'] if k in cur)
            if hit:
                pe = ('E', ev['id'], ev['car'])
                parent.setdefault(pe, ('S', hit[0], t))
                nxt.add(ev['tgt'])
                parent.setdefault(('S', ev['tgt'], t + 1), pe)
        cur = nxt
    return None

def path_str(p):
    out = []
    for n in p:
        if n[0] == 'S':
            out.append(f'({nname(n[1])},{n[2]})')
        else:
            out.append(f"{n[1][0]}{''.join(map(str, n[1][1]))}@{n[1][2]}[{n[2]}]")
    return ' → '.join(out)

def path_in_B(p):
    """The path lies entirely in B: every state node belongs to B (Definition 4: w, v, x, y are all nodes of B) and every event node has carrier B."""
    return all(n[0] == 'S' or n[2] == RW.B for n in p)

# ---------------- 1. Read-back ----------------
def imp_check(I, D):
    rows = []
    for eid, (u, asg) in D.fd1.items():
        t = eid[2]
        e = I.evs[t][eid]
        Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
        set_state = GK.setw(I.pre[u], asg)
        # IMP-2 exclusivity
        diff = [(k) for k in RW.nodes(I.G) if RW.node_val(set_state, k) != RW.node_val(I.pre[u], k)]
        imp2 = set(diff) <= Q and all(k[0] == 'w' for k in diff)
        # IMP-1 actual read (the first hop in the actual event graph is a read edge)
        p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
        imp1 = p is not None
        # IMP-3 same transition: the rerun uses the same G.step and the same declared letter sequence
        pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, set_state, I.hist[u:], t_start=u, **I.kw)
        imp3 = True
        # IMP-4 no bypass: every changed event lies in the forward-reachable set of the slot hypergraph (R3)
        R = RW.reach_forward(I.G, I.hist, {(k, u) for k in Q}, **I.kw)
        bad = 0
        for tt in range(u, len(I.hist)):
            for e0 in I.evs[tt].values():
                if not GK.same_event(evs2, e0) and not (e0['reads'] & R[tt]):
                    bad += 1
        changed = not GK.same_event(evs2, e)
        rows.append(dict(e=e, u=u, Q=Q, imp1=imp1, imp2=imp2, imp3=imp3, imp4=(bad == 0), changed=changed, path=p))
    return rows

def section_po7(insts):
    print('1. Read-back: IMP holds by model structure on the program host (witness-by-witness check)')
    tot = dict(n=0, i1=0, i2=0, i3=0, i4=0, ch=0)
    for nm, I in insts:
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        rows = imp_check(I, D)
        for r in rows:
            tot['n'] += 1
            tot['i1'] += r['imp1']; tot['i2'] += r['imp2']; tot['i3'] += r['imp3']; tot['i4'] += r['imp4']; tot['ch'] += r['changed']
        ex = [r for r in rows if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T][:1]
        print(f'  {nm}: 𝒟₁ positive-determination witnesses {len(rows)}; IMP-1 {sum(r["imp1"] for r in rows)}, IMP-2 {sum(r["imp2"] for r in rows)}, '
              f'IMP-3 {sum(r["imp3"] for r in rows)}, IMP-4 {sum(r["imp4"] for r in rows)}; witness rerun changes the event {sum(r["changed"] for r in rows)}')
        for r in ex:
            print(f'    e.g. {ename(r["e"])}, beat {r["u"]}, set {",".join(wname(k) for k in sorted(r["Q"]))}; actual path {path_str(r["path"])[:260]}')
    print(f'  total witnesses {tot["n"]}: all four items hold {min(tot["i1"], tot["i2"], tot["i3"], tot["i4"])}; witness effective {tot["ch"]}')
    print('  Reading: all four items are given by the program structure (the setting directly rewrites the state variables the process reads; K unchanged; letters unchanged; differences propagate only along the slot hypergraph); '
          'this is the witness-by-witness check, on the gating class, of “in model mode IMP holds by model structure” ([3] line 200), and it does not extend to any material realization (Dom4).')
    print()

# ---------------- 2. In-boundary self-feedback type ----------------
def inb_verdict(I, D, bfd=True):
    """Theorem B2: in open-loop K_g, the in-boundary self-feedback verdict = the HDyn verdict; a positive determination also gives the actual path and checks that it lies entirely in B."""
    res = DC.judge(I, D, bfd)
    wit = None
    for r in res['rows']:
        e = r['e']
        if e['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1 and r['v'] == 1 and e['car'] == RW.B:
            u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
            Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
            p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
            wit = (e, u, Q, p)
            break
    return res, wit

def section_po8(G, insts):
    print('2. In-boundary self-feedback type (open-loop K_g, Theorem B2)')
    for nm, I in insts:
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        res, wit = inb_verdict(I, D, True)
        resn, _ = inb_verdict(I, D, False)
        extT = [e for e in I.Wevents() if e['tgt'] in I.T and e['car'] != RW.B]
        line = f'  {nm}: HDyn = {res["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: {resn["HDyn"]}); out-of-boundary-carrier events acting on T {len(extT)}; in-boundary self-feedback type = {res["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: {resn["HDyn"]})'
        print(line)
        if wit:
            e, u, Q, p = wit
            print(f'    witness {ename(e)} (beat {u}, set {",".join(wname(k) for k in sorted(Q))}); actual path entirely in B: {path_in_B(p)}; path length {len(p)}')
        if res['HDyn'] == 0:
            print('    antecedents of the 0 verdict: ' + '; '.join(DC.premises(I, D, res, True)) + '; open-loop declaration (the letters are the declared exogenous sequence, no out-of-boundary pathway)')
    # SNN route “synapse → output neuron → backpropagation to the same synapse”: same-beat witness at beat 26 of Instance P
    P = insts[0][1]
    t = 26
    e = P.evs[t][('PLUS', (0, 0), t)]
    asg = {('w', 0, 0): G.b[0][0]}
    pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, GK.setw(P.pre[t], asg), P.hist[t:t + 1], t_start=t)
    p = act_path(P, {(('w', 0, 0), t)}, e)
    rec = G.step(P.pre[t], P.hist[t])[1]
    rec2 = G.step(GK.setw(P.pre[t], asg), P.hist[t])[1]
    print(f'  SNN route (Instance P, beat 26, legitimate same-beat setting w00 ← b): actual vt0 = {fr(rec["vt"][0])}, g0 = {rec["g"][0]}; after the setting vt0 = {fr(rec2["vt"][0])}, g0 = {rec2["g"][0]}; '
          f'PLUS00@26 after the setting {"does not occur" if ("PLUS", (0, 0), t) not in evs2.get(t, {}) else "still occurs"}')
    print(f'    actual path: {path_str(p)}; entirely in B: {path_in_B(p)}')
    print('    Refined reading: (w00,26) passes through SYN00@26 (synaptic transmission, reads w00) into the integral vt0 of output 0 → gating g0 (output-neuron firing) → backpropagation reaches the synapse (bap) → PLUS00@26 writes back to the same synapse w00; '
          'the carriers of all links are units within B (synapse, output neuron), and no out-of-boundary node of the declaration lies on this path.')
    # Route severing: by construction
    o_r = P.pre[P.t0]                                   # state after formation (plasticity and backpropagation both on) and rest
    for flag, kw in (('bap = 0', dict(bap=False)), ('plastic = 0', dict(plastic=False))):
        # sever only within W (as in the SNN-v2 contrast): W recorded from beat 0, formative history registered by ENT at entry beat −1
        I2 = GK.Instance(P.name + f' ({flag} within W)', G, P.hist[P.t0:], 0, o0=o_r, **kw)
        for k in I2.T:
            I2.form[k] = [-1] + I2.form[k]
        I2.avail = {k: [t for t in range(len(I2.hist) + 1)
                        if GK.zk(G, I2.pre[t], k) > EPS and any(u < t for u in I2.form[k])] for k in I2.T}
        nT = sum(1 for e2 in I2.Wevents() if e2['tgt'] in I2.T)
        D2 = DC.Decider(I2)
        j2 = DC.judge(I2, D2, True)
        print(f'  severed within W, {flag} (formative history registered by ENT): events in W acting on T {nT}; HDyn = {j2["HDyn"]}, in-boundary self-feedback type = {j2["HDyn"]}, Part = {j2["Part"]} (by the construction of Definition 2(6): potentiation and depression occur only under plastic ∧ bap, ρ = 0; not an independent finding)')
    print()

# ---------------- 3. Finite verification of Theorem B2 ----------------
def random_gate(rng):
    n_in, n_out = 3, 2
    pat = {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'a': (1, 0, 0), 'b': (0, 1, 1), 'ab': (1, 1, 1)}
    lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(n_out)]
    b = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, [F(1)] * n_out, [F(0)] * n_out, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out,
                   F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8), F(rng.randint(0, 2), 16), F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]), 10), b,
                   [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)], [[F(rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)])

def section_b2():
    print('3. Finite verification of Theorem B2 (random open-loop K_g members)')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_inst = n_wit = n_inB = n_inj_mid = n_extT = 0
    n_eq = 0
    while n_inst < 40:
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(5, 8)
        form = [(rng.choice(['a', 'b', 'ab']), (F(1), F(rng.choice([0, 1])))) for _ in range(2)]
        win = [(rng.choice(list(G.pat)), tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2]), 4) for _ in range(2))) for _ in range(L - 2)]
        h = form + win
        T = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(3) for j in range(2)], 2)
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 2, T=T)
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in T):
            continue
        D = DC.Decider(I, budget=30000)
        res = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        n_inst += 1
        n_extT += sum(1 for e in I.Wevents() if e['tgt'] in T and e['car'] != RW.B)
        for r in res['rows']:
            e = r['e']
            if e['tgt'] in T and r['v'] == 1 and e['id'] in D.fd1:
                u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
                Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
                p = act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
                n_wit += 1
                if p is not None and path_in_B(p):
                    n_inB += 1
                if p is not None and any(n[0] == 'E' and n[1][0] == 'INJ' for n in p[:-1]):
                    n_inj_mid += 1
        n_eq += 1
    print(f'  instances {n_inst}: out-of-boundary-carrier events acting on T {n_extT}; positive-determination witnesses {n_wit}, of which with actual path entirely in B {n_inB}; INJ as intermediate node {n_inj_mid} times')
    print('  Reading: the read set of INJ is empty, so INJ can have no incoming edge and cannot be an intermediate node of a path; the only events acting on T are PLUS, MINUS, RELAX, CLIP, all with carriers in B (Definition 5).')
    print()

# ---------------- 4. Closed-loop contrast ----------------
def run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=None, clamp=None, I_first=(0, 0)):
    """Closed loop: the environment (out-of-boundary carrier E) reads the firing g0(t) of output 0 at beat t and at beat t+1 injects I0 = 1 (if g0 = 1) or 0 into output 0.
    When clamp is given, the letters are clamped to the clamp sequence (external clamp: out-of-boundary components take their actual values, independent of the setting). Returns (pre-beat state per beat, events per beat, realized letter sequence)."""
    pre, evs, letters = {}, {}, []
    Inext = I_first
    for n, a in enumerate(inputs):
        t = t0 + n
        if interv is not None:
            o = interv(t, o)
        sig = clamp[n] if clamp is not None else (a, Inext)
        e = RW.Ev(G, o, sig, t)
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig)
        pre[t], evs[t] = o, e
        letters.append(sig)
        Inext = (F(1) if rec['g'][0] else F(0), F(0))
        o = o2
    return pre, evs, letters

def section_closed(G):
    print('4. Closed-loop contrast (scope of Theorem B3)')
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    o = G.init()
    for sig in FORM + REST:
        o, _ = G.step(o, sig)
    t0 = 26
    inputs = ['A', 'none']
    pre, evs, letters = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0)
    e = evs[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27))
    print(f'  Declaration: formation and rest as in s1 (beats 0–25); letter A at beat 26, rest at beat 27; closed-loop injection by the environment: I0 at beat 27 = {fr(letters[1][1][0])} (because output 0 fires at beat 26); W = beat 27; T = {{w00, w10}}')
    print(f'  actual: PLUS00@27 {"occurs, c = " + fr(e["c"]) if e else "does not occur"}')
    asg = {('w', 0, 0): G.b[0][0], ('w', 1, 0): G.b[1][0]}
    iv = lambda t, oo: GK.setw(oo, asg) if t == 26 else oo
    preL, evsL, lettersL = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=iv)
    preC, evsC, _ = run_closed(G, o, inputs, t0, interv=iv, clamp=letters)
    eL = evsL[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27)); eC = evsC[27].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 27))
    print(f'  beat 26, set w00, w10 ← b: with the environment active, beat 26 g0 = {int(("RESET", (0,), 26) in evsL[26])}, beat 27 I0 = {fr(lettersL[1][1][0])}, PLUS00@27 {"occurs" if eL else "does not occur"} (response: present)')
    print(f'                             with external clamp (letters clamped to actual values), PLUS00@27 {"occurs, c = " + fr(eC["c"]) if eC else "does not occur"} (response: {"none" if (eC and eC["c"] == e["c"]) else "present"})')
    print('  Actual path: (w00,26) → gating g0(26) → environment E reads the firing and injects at beat 27 (out-of-boundary carrier) → INJ0@27 → gating g0(27) → PLUS00@27: via out-of-boundary, χ_e type; HDyn (closed-loop instance) = 1.')
    # HDyn tier on the clamped instance (letters = actually realized sequence, open-loop declaration) = in-boundary self-feedback type of the closed-loop instance (Theorem B3)
    Icl = GK.Instance('clamped instance', G, FORM + REST + letters, 27)
    D = DC.Decider(Icl)
    j = DC.judge(Icl, D, True); jn = DC.judge(Icl, D, False)
    rows = [r for r in j['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in Icl.T]
    print('  events in W acting on T on the clamped instance (open-loop declaration, letters = actually realized sequence): ' + '; '.join(f'{ename(r["e"])} a={r["a"]} η={r["v"]} (tier {r["tier"]})' for r in rows))
    print(f'  ⇒ in-boundary self-feedback type of the closed-loop instance: {j["HDyn"]} when B_FD^cl is declared, {jn["HDyn"]} when it is not; HDyn of the closed-loop instance = 1 (via an out-of-boundary route).')
    print('  Reading: no response under the external clamp only shows that the functional dependence of the clamped instance is 0 (tier (b)); by Lemma 13 of Chapter 3 and the three negative tiers, the verdict stays ⊥ when the corresponding antecedent is not declared, and “no response under the external clamp” does not yield 0. '
          'Placing the environment inside B, or switching to an open-loop exogenous declaration, gives a different instance ([3] 68).')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 frozen declared history', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+teach 1, B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    O1 = GK.Instance('s1 declared history, W = (A+teach 1, rest, rest)', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)
    FORM1 = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE]
    O2 = GK.Instance('formation once; W = (B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                     FORM1 + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] + [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4); S.W0 = F(1, 2)
    Gs = GC.snn_gate(S)
    SAT = GK.Instance('W0 = 1/2, A₊ = 1/4 (clipping in effect)', Gs, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    S.A_PLUS = F(1, 8); S.W0 = F(1, 4)
    insts = [('Instance P', P), ('Instance N', N), ('Instance O1', O1), ('Instance O2', O2), ('Instance SAT', SAT)]
    t0 = time.time()
    section_po7(insts[:2])
    section_po8(G, insts)
    section_b2()
    section_closed(G)
    print(f'End. The above are finite verifications and exact readings; proofs of the general propositions are in 5.5 and 5.6 of the main text.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.12  b2\_codes.py  Event-by-event agreement check for Theorem B2 (5.6)

~~~python
"""Event-by-event equal-value verification of Theorem B2: in open-loop 𝒦_g the in-boundary self-feedback type and HDyn agree item by item in candidate domains, external-clamp reruns and reason codes (main text 5.6).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 b2_codes.py
"""
import sys, random
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import r_layer_witness as RW
import decision as DC
import boundary_po8 as BP
NONE = GK.NONE

def compare(I, bfd):
    D = DC.Decider(I, budget=30000)
    res = DC.judge(I, D, bfd)
    hd = {r['e']['id']: (r['a'], r['v']) for r in res['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T}
    dom_h = set(hd)
    inb, dom_b = {}, set()
    for e in I.Wevents():
        if e['tgt'] in I.T and e['car'] == RW.B:
            dom_b.add(e['id'])
            ex = DC.eta_exact(I, D, e, bfd)
            v = ex['v']
            if v == 1:
                u, asg = D.fd1[e['id']]
                Q = {k for k, val in asg.items() if val != I.pre[u][0][k[1]][k[2]]}
                p = BP.act_path(I, {(k, u) for k in Q}, e)
                v = 1 if (p is not None and BP.path_in_B(p)) else '⊥_ext'
            inb[e['id']] = (1 if abs(e['c']) > GK.EPS else 0, v)
    same = sum(1 for k in dom_h if inb.get(k) == hd[k])
    codes = sorted({str(v[1]) for v in hd.values()})
    J = lambda d: GK.k_or(GK.k_and(a, v) for a, v in d.values())
    return dom_h == dom_b, same, len(dom_h), J(hd), J(inb), codes, res['nsat']

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    insts = [('Instance P', GK.Instance('', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)),
             ('Instance N', GK.Instance('', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)),
             ('Instance O1', GK.Instance('', G, FORM + REST + [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE], 26)),
             ('Instance O2', GK.Instance('', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] + [NONE] * 7 + [('A', (F(1), 0))] +
                                    [('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 11))]
    print('1. Four SNN instances (separate reading; two columns, B_FD declared and not declared)')
    for nm, I in insts:
        for bfd in (True, False):
            d, same, n, H, B, codes, nsat = compare(I, bfd)
            print(f'  {nm} (B_FD {"declared" if bfd else "not declared"}): candidate domains equal {d}; event-by-event (a, η) equal (η including reason codes) {same}/{n}; HDyn = {H}, in-boundary self-feedback type = {B}; η values occurring {codes}')
    print('2. Random open-loop K_g members (same generation as section 3 of boundary_po8, seed 20261102)')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_inst = tot = same_t = dom_ok = cod = 0
    allcodes = set()
    while n_inst < 40:
        G2 = BP.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(5, 8)
        form = [(rng.choice(['a', 'b', 'ab']), (F(1), F(rng.choice([0, 1])))) for _ in range(2)]
        win = [(rng.choice(list(G2.pat)), tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2]), 4) for _ in range(2))) for _ in range(L - 2)]
        T = rng.sample([('w', i, j) for i in range(3) for j in range(2)], 2)
        I = GK.Instance('', G2, form + win, 2, T=T)
        if not any(I.avail[k] for k in T):
            continue
        n_inst += 1
        for bfd in (True, False):
            d, same, n, H, B, codes, nsat = compare(I, bfd)
            dom_ok += d; tot += n; same_t += same; cod += (H == B)
            allcodes |= set(codes)
    print(f'  40 instances × two columns: candidate domains equal {dom_ok}/80; event-by-event (a, η) equal {same_t}/{tot}; verdicts equal {cod}/80; η values occurring {sorted(allcodes)}')
    print('  Note: under the joint reading (D9) of clipping-saturated position–beats, both sets of verdicts take the same set of joint events; this program compares only separate-reading atoms.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.13  source\_r3.py  Library for the strict-subdomain classification of sources, the removal domain, the resting conditions and the F-model comparison (5.4)

~~~python
"""Strict-subdomain classification library for sources (the classification compared by Theorem SUB in main text 5.4: S*₀, S*₁, ⊥_aux, S, etc.), removal domains, the resting conditions P4R and P4Z, and the F-model comparator (imported by Appendices A.14, A.15).
“⊥_aux” denotes a write for which P4R holds but P4Z does not (outside the strict subdomain); the main text adjudicates such writes case by case by criterion PS (main text 7.5(5)).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic.
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr

def wname(k):
    return f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}'

def ename(e):
    return f"{e['id'][0]}{''.join(str(c) for c in e['id'][1])}@{e['t']}"

# ---------------- Definition S-1: entry components (the nonzero components of the declared out-of-boundary letter σ = (a, I)) ----------------
def entry_comps(G, sigma):
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    return [('in', i) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]] + [('I', j) for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != 0]

def removals(G, sigma):
    """Removal domain 𝒟_σ(t): set some nonzero components of the actual letter to zero (for the entry, take the letters of the declared alphabet whose pattern is bitwise no greater than the actual pattern; for the teaching current, take each component's original value or 0).
    Removal acts only on the declared out-of-boundary signal; it changes neither the state nor the process law (Dom3: a test, not an intrusion)."""
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    subs = [a for a, p in G.pat.items() if all(p[i] <= s[i] for i in range(G.n_in))]
    Is = list(itertools.product(*[sorted({I[j], 0}, key=str) for j in range(G.n_out)]))
    out = []
    for a in subs:
        for Iv in Is:
            sig = (a, tuple(Iv))
            if sig != (name, tuple(I)):
                out.append(sig)
    return out

# ---------------- Definition S-2: actual within-beat source chain (entry component → actual event → firing node → write), not passing through Structural-Position nodes ----------------
def tick_chain(G, o, sigma, evs, carried, t):
    """Return (R, gate, carried_next, why, gwhy).
    R: the events (kind, idx) of this beat reachable from entry components along actual edges → their sets of chain origins (entry components (type, index, beat)); every edge carries a nonzero contribution:
      letter edges: ('in', i) → XIN_i, SYN_ij (occurrence fixed by s_i), MINUS_ij (occurrence fixed by s_i), PLUS_ij (component via x′_i = d·x_i + s_i); ('I', j) → INJ_j;
      inflow into firing node γ_j (exists only when g_j = 1): from SYN_ij (c ≠ 0), INJ_j, LEAK_j (v_j carries field-entry influence and c ≠ 0);
      γ_j → RESET_j, YIN_j, PLUS_ij (occurrence), MINUS_ij (component via y′_j = d·y_j + g_j);
      auxiliary positions across beats: v, x, y in carried (node → set of origins) pass to the next beat via LEAK, XDEC, YDEC and this beat's new inputs (XIN, YIN, RESET).
    The chain does not pass through nodes of Structural Positions w (SYN reading w is an inflow edge, not a transmission edge of the chain; RELAX only reads w and is never on the chain)."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    ev = {(e['id'][0], e['id'][1]): e for e in evs.values()}
    R, why = {}, {}
    def add(key, reason, orig):
        if key in ev and orig:
            if key not in R:
                why[key] = reason
            R.setdefault(key, set()).update(orig)
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if s[i]:
            add(('XIN', (i,)), f'entry {i}', {('in', i, t)})
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                add(('SYN', (i, j)), f'entry {i} transmitted via synapse w{i}{j}', {('in', i, t)})
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if I[j] != 0:
            add(('INJ', (j,)), f'teaching current I{j}', {('I', j, t)})
        if ('v', j) in carried and ('LEAK', (j,)) in ev and ev[('LEAK', (j,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('LEAK', (j,)), f'v{j} carries earlier field-entry influence', carried[('v', j)])
        if ('y', j) in carried and ('YDEC', (j,)) in ev and ev[('YDEC', (j,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('YDEC', (j,)), f'y{j} carries earlier field-entry influence', carried[('y', j)])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if ('x', i) in carried and ev[('XDEC', (i,))]['c'] != 0:
            add(('XDEC', (i,)), f'x{i} carries earlier field-entry influence', carried[('x', i)])
    gate, gwhy = {}, {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:                                  # g_j = 1
            src = [k for k in R if ev[k]['c'] != 0 and
                   ((k[0] == 'SYN' and k[1][1] == j) or (k[0] in ('INJ', 'LEAK') and k[1][0] == j))]
            if src:
                gate[j] = set().union(*[R[k] for k in src]); gwhy[j] = src
    for j in gate:
        add(('RESET', (j,)), f'firing γ{j}', gate[j]); add(('YIN', (j,)), f'firing γ{j}', gate[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0 else set())
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            key = ('PLUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                orig = (gate.get(j, set())) | xo
                rs = []
                if j in gate: rs.append(f'firing γ{j} reaches the synapse by backpropagation (occurrence)')
                if xo: rs.append(f'x′{i} carried into field (component)')
                add(key, '; '.join(rs), orig)
            key = ('MINUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                yo = gate.get(j, set()) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0 else set())
                add(key, f'entry {i} (occurrence)' + (f'; y′{j} carried into field (component)' if yo else ''), {('in', i, t)} | yo)
            key = ('CLIP', (i, j))
            mem = [m for m in (('PLUS', (i, j)), ('MINUS', (i, j))) if m in R]
            if key in ev and mem:
                add(key, 'clipping member on chain', set().union(*[R[m] for m in mem]))
    C2 = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:
            if j in gate:
                C2[('v', j)] = set(gate[j])
        else:
            cs = [k for k in [('LEAK', (j,)), ('INJ', (j,))] + [('SYN', (i, j)) for i in range(G.n_in)] if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0]
            if cs:
                C2[('v', j)] = set().union(*[R[k] for k in cs])
        yo = set(gate.get(j, set())) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0 else set())
        if yo:
            C2[('y', j)] = yo
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0 else set())
        if xo:
            C2[('x', i)] = xo
    return R, gate, C2, why, gwhy

def chains(I):
    """For the actual history of instance I, compute beat by beat Ch_0 (using only this beat's field entry) and Ch_aux (across beats via auxiliary positions), each a map event key → set of chain origins."""
    ch0, chaux, info = {}, {}, {}
    carried = {}
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        R0, g0, _, why0, gw0 = tick_chain(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], {}, t)
        Ra, ga, carried, whya, gwa = tick_chain(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], carried, t)
        ch0[t], chaux[t], info[t] = R0, Ra, (why0, gw0, whya, gwa)
    return ch0, chaux, info

def changed_comps(G, sig, sig2, t):
    """Entry components whose value at beat t is changed by the removal sig → sig2."""
    return {('in', i, t) for i in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[sig[0]][i] != G.pat[sig2[0]][i]} | \
           {('I', j, t) for j in range(G.n_out) if sig[1][j] != sig2[1][j]}

# ---------------- Definition S-3: field-entry dependence SigDep (removing components of the declared signal changes the occurrence or the component of an event) ----------------
def sigdep0(I, e, orig=None):
    """Same-beat removal witnesses; when orig is given, keep only those whose “removed components intersect the chain origins of e” (dependence and chain arise from the same set of entry components)."""
    t = e['t']
    wits = []
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        if orig is not None and not (changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[t], sig, t) & orig):
            continue
        evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
        if e['id'] not in evs2:
            wits.append((sig, 'does not occur'))
        elif evs2[e['id']]['c'] != e['c']:
            wits.append((sig, f"component {fr(e['c'])}→{fr(evs2[e['id']]['c'])}"))
    return wits

def sigdep_m(I, e, lookback=6, orig=None):
    """Remove signal components at an earlier beat u (t − lookback ≤ u < t), rerun from u to t(e), and the event changes (cross-beat dependence of field entry via auxiliary positions)."""
    t = e['t']
    for u in range(max(0, t - lookback), t):
        for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[u]):
            if orig is not None and not (changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u) & orig):
                continue
            pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, I.pre[u], [sig] + I.hist[u + 1:t + 1], t_start=u, **I.kw)
            if not GK.same_event(evs, e):
                return (u, sig)
    return None

def null_letter(G):
    return ('none', tuple(0 for _ in range(G.n_out)))

def p5_null(I, t, k):
    """Antecedent P5: when this beat's letter is replaced by rest (all field entry removed), Structural Position k carries no effective event — the declared initial organization and the hosting rule alone leave no effective change on k.
    (This concerns only the action position k of e, not the other positions of T, so Mark_R(k) does not depend on T and r_R is ∃-type monotone in T.)"""
    evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], null_letter(I.G), t, **I.kw)
    return not any(e2['tgt'] == k and abs(e2['c']) > EPS for e2 in evs2.values())

def trace_free(I, t):
    """Antecedent P4′ of S*₁: in the pre-beat states of beats 0–t, no Structural Position of X carries a Trace-Response already left (z ≤ ε) — all earlier activity arises only from field entry and the declared initial organization."""
    G = I.G
    X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    return all(GK.zk(G, I.pre[u], k) <= EPS for u in range(t + 1) for k in X)

def p5_all(I, t, k, o_init):
    """Antecedent P5′ of S*₁: starting from the declared initial state, with beats 0–t all replaced by the rest letter, k carries no effective event at beat t."""
    pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(I.G, o_init, [null_letter(I.G)] * (t + 1), **I.kw)
    return not any(e2['tgt'] == k and abs(e2['c']) > EPS for e2 in evs[t].values())

def rest_state(I, o):
    """P4R: o is a fixed point under the rest letter (K(o, rest) = o, and rest produces no nonzero component), and X carries no Trace-Response already left.
    This function checks only one-step fixedness and that the components under rest are 0; whether the auxiliary state takes the memoryless baseline is verified item by item separately by zero_aux (P4Z)."""
    G = I.G
    o2, rec = G.step(o, null_letter(G), **I.kw)
    evs = RW.Ev(G, o, null_letter(G), 0, **I.kw)
    X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
    return same_state(o2, o) and all(e['c'] == 0 for e in evs.values()) and all(GK.zk(G, o, k) <= EPS for k in X)

def zero_aux(I, o):
    """P4Z: the load-bearing auxiliary states each take the memoryless baseline 0 (v_j = 0, x_i = 0, y_j = 0), i.e. the initial values of Definition 1 (the initial state of snn_core.py in Appendix A.1).
    x and y are eligibility traces (x = Σ d^k s_{t−k}, likewise y), and v is the integrated field entry; the value 0 means “no earlier field entry or firing has left a value on the auxiliary positions”. Verified item by item, not inferred from fixedness under rest."""
    return all(c == 0 for c in o[1]) and all(c == 0 for c in o[2]) and all(c == 0 for c in o[3])

def same_state(o1, o2):
    return o1[0] == o2[0] and list(o1[1]) == list(o2[1]) and list(o1[2]) == list(o2[2]) and list(o1[3]) == list(o2[3])

def classify(I, ch0, chaux, o_init, lookback=6, tmax=None):
    """For every event in Hist(T) (t ≤ t₁) that acts on T and is effective, compute its source class.
    S*₀: Ch_0 ∧ same-beat removal witness (removed components among the chain origins) ∧ P4R ∧ P4Z (the pre-beat state is a resting state and the auxiliary state is 0 item by item) ∧ P5 (no effective event on k under rest);
    S: Ch_aux ∧ removal witness (same beat or earlier beat, removed components among the chain origins); ⊥_σOpen: removal dependence present but no actual chain issuing from the removed components;
    ⊥_σOver: actual chain present but no dependence within the declared removal domain; no chain: Ch_aux unreachable."""
    rows = []
    tmax = I.t1 if tmax is None else tmax
    for t in range(0, tmax + 1):
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in I.T or abs(e['c']) <= EPS:
                continue
            key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
            o0 = ch0[t].get(key, set()); oa = chaux[t].get(key, set())
            c0, ca = bool(o0), bool(oa)
            sd0 = sigdep0(I, e, o0) if c0 else []
            sdA = sigdep0(I, e, oa) if ca else []
            sdm = None if (sd0 or sdA or not ca) else sigdep_m(I, e, lookback, oa)
            p4r = rest_state(I, I.pre[t])
            p4 = p4r and zero_aux(I, I.pre[t])
            p5 = p5_null(I, t, e['tgt'])
            if c0 and sd0 and p4 and p5:
                cls = 'S*₀'
            elif ca and (sdA or sdm) and rest_state(I, I.pre[0]) and zero_aux(I, I.pre[0]) and trace_free(I, t) and p5_all(I, t, e['tgt'], I.pre[0]):
                cls = 'S*₁'
            elif c0 and sd0 and p4r and p5 and not zero_aux(I, I.pre[t]):
                cls = '⊥_aux'                    # fixed under rest but auxiliary state nonzero: outside the source subdomain (outside the strict subdomain); not judged 0
            elif ca and (sdA or sdm):
                cls = 'S'
            elif (w0 := sigdep0(I, e)) or (wm := sigdep_m(I, e, lookback)):
                wit = (t, w0[0][0]) if w0 else wm
                cls = '⊥_σOpen' if wfree_reach(I, e, wit) else 'continuation'
            elif ca:
                cls = '⊥_σOver'
            else:
                cls = 'no chain'
            rows.append(dict(e=e, c0=c0, ca=ca, sd0=sd0 or sdA, sdm=sdm, p4=p4, p5=p5, cls=cls, orig=oa))
    return rows

def wfree_reach(I, e, wit):
    """Whether the removal dependence can propagate without passing through Structural Positions: on the slot hypergraph with the Structural-Position nodes deleted, can e be reached from the removed components?
    If not, then by the same argument as for R3σ the dependence can propagate only through Structural Positions (Trace-Responses already left), and e is an in-field continuation (e.g. relaxation reading the Trace-Response at its own position), not a field-entry dependence."""
    u, sig = wit
    comps = {(k, i) for (k, i, _u) in changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u)}
    aff, R = entry_slot_reach(I.G, I.hist, u, comps, noW=True, **I.kw)
    return aff(e) if e['t'] == u else bool(e['reads'] & R.get(e['t'], frozenset()))

def r_verdict(rows):
    """r_R(T): S* present → 1 (theorem, Lemma S*); no S* but S present → recorded as “1 under reading RS”; every effective T event “no chain” → 0 (Lemma S⁻, antecedents REC, ENT, R1, EF1′ and reading N);
    otherwise ⊥."""
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S*₀' for r in rows):
        return '1[strict]', 'S*₀'
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S*₁' for r in rows):
        return '1[strict, EF1′]', 'S*₁'
    if any(r['cls'] == '⊥_aux' for r in rows):
        return '⊥_aux', 'outside the source subdomain (nonzero stationary auxiliary state)'
    if any(r['cls'] == 'S' for r in rows):
        return '1 (RS reading) / ⊥ (RS not declared)', 'S'
    if all(r['cls'] in ('no chain', 'continuation') for r in rows):
        return 0, 'S⁻'
    return '⊥', '⊥'

def show_chain(I, info, e, which='0'):
    t = e['t']
    why0, gw0, whya, gwa = info[t]
    why, gw = (why0, gw0) if which == '0' else (whya, gwa)
    key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
    parts = [why.get(key, '—')]
    j = e['id'][1][1] if e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') else None
    if j is not None and j in gw:
        parts.append('field-entry inflow into γ%d: ' % j + ', '.join(f"{k[0]}{''.join(map(str, k[1]))} ({fr(I.evs[t][(k[0], k[1], t)]['c'])})" for k in sorted(gw[j])))
    return '; '.join(parts)

# ---------------- 1, 2: SNN instances ----------------
def section_snn(G):
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    REST = [NONE] * 8
    P = GK.Instance('s1 frozen declared history', G, FORM + REST + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+teach 1, B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    o_init = G.init()
    out = {}
    print('1. SNN field-entry signal — sustaining medium — firing formed — structural change — transmission path (Instance P, beat 0, exact readings item by item)')
    t = 0
    sig = P.hist[t]
    o = P.pre[t]
    rec = G.step(o, sig)[1]
    print(f'  Declared: B = 4 entry units, 2 output units, 8 synapses; out-of-boundary signal = entry stimulus sequence and teaching current ([core] lines 3–4); T = {{w00, w10}}; ε = {EPS}')
    print(f'  Pre-beat state at beat 0 = declared initial state (w = b = {G.b[0][0]}, v = x = y = 0): {same_state(o, o_init)}; resting state (P4R): {rest_state(P, o)}; auxiliary state is the memoryless baseline 0 item by item (P4Z): {zero_aux(P, o)}')
    print(f'  (1) Field-entry signal: letter {sig[0]} (entry pattern {G.pat[sig[0]]}) + teaching current I = ({fr(F(sig[1][0]))}, {fr(F(sig[1][1]))}); entry components {entry_comps(G, sig)}')
    ev = P.evs[t]
    def c(kind, idx):
        k = (kind, idx, t)
        return fr(ev[k]['c']) if k in ev else 'none'
    print(f'  (2) Sustaining medium: entry units 0, 1 receive the stimulus (XIN0 = {c("XIN", (0,))}, XIN1 = {c("XIN", (1,))} written into eligibility traces x0, x1); '
          f'synapses w00, w10 transmit (SYN00 = {c("SYN", (0, 0))}, SYN10 = {c("SYN", (1, 0))}; the transmitted amount is the baseline b, not a Trace-Response); '
          f'the membrane of output unit 0 sustains the teaching current (INJ0 = {c("INJ", (0,))}, carrier outside the boundary)')
    print(f'  (3) Firing formed: output 0 integrates vt0 = λ·0 + {fr(G.b[0][0])} + {fr(G.b[1][0])} + 1 = {fr(rec["vt"][0])} ≥ θ = {G.theta[0]}, g0 = {rec["g"][0]}; '
          f'output 1 integrates vt1 = {fr(rec["vt"][1])} < θ, g1 = {rec["g"][1]}')
    print(f'  (4) Structural change: PLUS00 = A₊·x′0 = {c("PLUS", (0, 0))}, MINUS00 = −A₋·y′0 = {c("MINUS", (0, 0))}; PLUS10 = {c("PLUS", (1, 0))}, MINUS10 = {c("MINUS", (1, 0))}; '
          f'w00: {fr(P.pre[0][0][0][0])} → {fr(P.pre[1][0][0][0])}, Trace-Response z = {fr(P.pre[1][0][0][0] - G.b[0][0])} > ε, formation-record beat {P.form[("w", 0, 0)][:1]}')
    ch0, chaux, info = chains(P)
    for key in [('PLUS', (0, 0)), ('MINUS', (0, 0))]:
        e = ev[(key[0], key[1], t)]
        print(f'  (5) Transmission path of {ename(e)}: {show_chain(P, info, e)}; chain origins {sorted(ch0[t][key])}')
    print('      That is: stimulus → entry units (XIN) → transmission via synapses w00, w10 (SYN) and the teaching current (INJ) converge on the membrane potential of output 0 → firing γ0 on crossing the threshold → backpropagation to the synapse → '
          'multiplication by the presynaptic eligibility trace x′0, written into w00; the whole chain completes within beat 0 and passes through no Trace-Response already left.')
    for r in sigdep0(P, ev[('PLUS', (0, 0), t)], ch0[t][('PLUS', (0, 0))]):
        print(f'      Removal to {r[0][0]}+I=({fr(F(r[0][1][0]))},{fr(F(r[0][1][1]))}): PLUS00@0 {r[1]}')
    print(f'      Effective events on w00, w10 under the rest letter (all field entry removed): {"none" if (p5_null(P, 0, ("w", 0, 0)) and p5_null(P, 0, ("w", 1, 0))) else "present"} (P5)')
    print()
    print('2. Source certification (Lemmas S*, S, S⁻)')
    for I, nm in ((P, 'Instance P'), (N, 'Instance N')):
        ch0, chaux, info = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, o_init)
        rv = r_verdict(rows)
        cnt = {}
        for r in rows:
            cnt[r['cls']] = cnt.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
        print(f'  [{nm}] {I.name}; Hist(T) = beats 0–{I.t1}, {len(rows)} effective events acting on T, classes: ' +
              '; '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(cnt.items())))
        for r in rows[:6]:
            e = r['e']
            print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | Ch_0 {int(r['c0'])} Ch_aux {int(r['ca'])} | SigDep_0 {len(r['sd0'])} removal witnesses | "
                  f"P4 {int(r['p4'])} P5 {int(r['p5'])} | {r['cls']}")
        sform = sorted({r['e']['t'] for r in rows if r['cls'] == 'S'})
        if sform:
            print(f'    Beats with class S (weak reading RS: jointly determined by field entry and Trace-Responses already left): {sform[:12]}{" …" if len(sform) > 12 else ""}')
        print(f'    r_R(T) = {rv[0]} (basis {rv[1]})')
        out[nm] = (I, rows, rv)
    # Contrast C1: the Trace-Response is formed only from the declared initial organization via in-boundary continuation; the teaching current is present (acting on output 1) but does not reach T along an actual chain
    o1 = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(4, 3), F(0)], [F(1), F(1), F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)])
    C1 = GK.Instance('C1: declared initial state v0 = 4/3, x0 = x1 = 1 (initial organization, not Trace-Responses); history (rest+teach(0,1), rest, rest)', G,
                     [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o1)
    ch0, chaux, info = chains(C1)
    rows = classify(C1, ch0, chaux, o1)
    print(f'  [Contrast C1] {C1.name}; T = {{w00}}')
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']
        print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | Ch_0 {int(r['c0'])} Ch_aux {int(r['ca'])} | SigDep_0 {len(r['sd0'])} | {r['cls']}")
    zend = C1.pre[len(C1.hist)][0][0][0] - G.b[0][0]
    rv = r_verdict(rows)
    print(f'    Window-end z(w00) = {fr(zend)} > ε: q = 1; r_R({{w00}}) = {rv[0]} ({rv[1]}: the only effective event acting on w00 has no field-entry chain); TraceSet = 0')
    C1b = GK.Instance('', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 1), ('w', 1, 1)], o0=o1)
    ch0b, chauxb, _ = chains(C1b)
    rowsb = classify(C1b, ch0b, chauxb, o1)
    print(f'    Same history, T′ = {{w01, w11}} (the teaching current drives output 1 to fire, which writes): classes {[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rowsb]}; '
          f'r_R(T′) = {r_verdict(rowsb)[0]} (the declared initial state v0 = 4/3, x0 = x1 = 1 is not a resting state, P4R fails, so not S*₀; class S of the weak reading)')
    print('    Reading: the presence of the teaching current does not by itself give r; a conditional positive determination of r requires that “entry components on a resting medium write into T along an actual chain, and removal changes that write” (this program is the strict-subdomain classification; the current criterion is in Appendix A.14).')
    print()
    return P, N, out

# ---------------- 3: TraceSet and the Fld verdict path ----------------
def section_fld(G, P, N, out):
    import decision as DC
    print('3. TraceSet and the Fld verdict path (model mode, column A)')
    res = {}
    for nm in ('Instance P', 'Instance N'):
        I, rows, rv = out[nm]
        end = I.pre[len(I.hist)]
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
~~~
