~~~python
        # T_max ([3] 288): Structural Positions with window-end z > ε and a formation record; formation is recomputed on all of X by the component-wise threshold crossing of D16
        Iall = GK.Instance('', G, I.hist, I.t0, T=X)
        Tmax = [k for k in X if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Iall.form[k]]
        q = {k: (GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and bool(I.form[k])) for k in I.T}
        t0 = time.time()
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        j = DC.judge(I, D, True)
        jn = DC.judge(I, D, False)
        trace = '1[strict]' if (all(q.values()) and str(rv[0]).startswith('1[')) else (0 if not all(q.values()) or rv[0] == 0 else '⊥')
        Q = GK.k_and(1 if trace == '1[strict]' else trace, j['Part'], j['HDyn'])
        Q = '1[strict]' if Q == 1 else Q
        print(f'  [{nm}] T = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in I.T)}}}: q = ' + ', '.join(f'{wname(k)} {int(v)} (window-end z = {fr(GK.zk(G, end, k))}, formation beat {I.form[k][:1]})' for k, v in q.items()))
        print(f'    r = {rv[0]} (Lemma {rv[1]}; ⊥_src when the strict-subdomain correspondence antecedents are not declared); Ident = 1 (POS: the Structural Position is the synapse, its position persists); HistEv = 1 (REC: full record from the declared initial state); TraceSet = {trace}')
        print(f'    Part = {j["Part"]}, HDyn = {j["HDyn"]} (Theorem D2, B_FD declared; B_FD not declared: Part = {jn["Part"]}, HDyn = {jn["HDyn"]}); Q_R(T) = {Q}')
        print(f'    T_max = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in Tmax)}}}')
        if set(Tmax) == set(I.T):
            QT = Q; jT = j
        else:
            IT = GK.Instance(I.name, G, I.hist, I.t0, T=Tmax)
            ch0, chaux, _ = chains(IT)
            rowsT = classify(IT, ch0, chaux, G.init())
            rT = r_verdict(rowsT)[0]
            qT = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and IT.form[k] for k in Tmax)
            DT = DC.Decider(IT)
            jT = DC.judge(IT, DT, True)
            jTn = DC.judge(IT, DT, False)
            trT = '1[strict]' if (qT and str(rT).startswith('1[')) else '⊥'
            QT = GK.k_and(1 if trT == '1[strict]' else trT, jT['Part'], jT['HDyn'])
            print(f'    On T_max: r = {rT}, TraceSet = {trT}, Part = {jT["Part"]}, HDyn = {jT["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: HDyn = {jTn["HDyn"]}); Q_R(T_max) = {QT}')
            if jT['HDyn'] == 0:
                print('    Antecedents of HDyn(T_max) = 0: ' + '; '.join(DC.premises(IT, DT, jT, True)))
        fld = '1[strict]' if (QT == 1 or Q == '1[strict]') else (0 if QT == 0 else '⊥')
        prem = 'conditional 1: when the strict-subdomain correspondence antecedents hold, the witness T has Q_R = 1 ([3] 4.7 “the existence of a witness suffices”); when they are not declared, r = ⊥_src and TraceSet, Q_R, Fld = ⊥' if fld == '1[strict]' else \
            'judged 0: Q_R(T_max) = 0; also requires EF1′ (auxiliary positions bear no Trace-Response), that what lies outside T_max is borne by EF1′, and the antecedent list for HDyn judged 0 (Theorem D2); r does not participate (TraceSet is already 1)'
        print(f'    Fld = {fld} ({prem})')
        res[nm] = (q, rv, trace, j, Q, Tmax, QT, fld)
    print()
    return res

# ---------------- 4: independent rerun verification of R3σ and the S* antecedents ----------------
def entry_slot_reach(G, hist, u, comps, plastic=True, bap=True, noW=False):
    """Forward reachability on the slot hypergraph from the set comps of entry components at beat u: returns (a predicate deciding, by event kind, whether an event of this beat is affected; the node set R[t])."""
    name, I = hist[u]
    s = G.pat[name]
    ins = {i for (k, i) in comps if k == 'in'}
    js = {j for (k, j) in comps if k == 'I'}
    gates = set(js) | ({j for j in range(G.n_out)} if ins else set())   # an entry affects the gating of every output via the SYN slots
    nxt = set()
    for j in gates:
        nxt |= {('v', j), ('y', j)} | {('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in)}
    for i in ins:
        nxt |= {('x', i)} | {('w', i, j) for j in range(G.n_out)} | {('v', j) for j in range(G.n_out)}
    def affected_now(e):
        kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
        if kind in ('XIN',):
            return idx[0] in ins
        if kind == 'SYN':
            return idx[0] in ins
        if kind == 'INJ':
            return idx[0] in js
        if kind in ('RESET', 'YIN'):
            return idx[0] in gates
        if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP'):
            return idx[1] in gates or idx[0] in ins
        return False
    if noW:
        nxt = {n for n in nxt if n[0] != 'w'}
    R = {u + 1: frozenset(nxt)}
    cur = set(nxt)
    for t in range(u + 1, len(hist)):
        dep = RW.slot_deps(G, hist[t], plastic, bap)
        cur = {n for n, d in dep.items() if d & cur}
        if noW:
            cur = {n for n in cur if n[0] != 'w'}
        R[t + 1] = frozenset(cur)
    return affected_now, R

def random_gate(rng):
    n_in, n_out = 3, 2
    pat = {'none': (0, 0, 0), 'a': (1, 0, 0), 'b': (0, 1, 1), 'ab': (1, 1, 1)}
    lam = [F(rng.randint(0, 4), 4) for _ in range(n_out)]
    th = [F(1)] * n_out
    b = [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    lo = [[F(0)] * n_out for _ in range(n_in)]
    hi = [[F(rng.choice([1, 2])) for _ in range(n_out)] for _ in range(n_in)]
    return GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, lam, th, [F(0)] * n_out, [F(1, 2)] * n_in, [F(1, 2)] * n_out,
                   F(rng.randint(1, 3), 8), F(rng.randint(0, 2), 16), F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1]), 10), b, lo, hi)

def random_hist(rng, G, L):
    letters = list(G.pat)
    h = []
    for _ in range(L):
        a = rng.choice(letters)
        I = tuple(F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, -1, 3]), 4) for _ in range(G.n_out))
        h.append((a, I))
    return h

def section_checks():
    print('4. Finite verification')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_cmp = n_viol = 0
    n_inst = 0
    # R3σ: events unreachable from the removed components on the slot hypergraph are unchanged after removal
    while n_inst < 150:
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 8)
        h = random_hist(rng, G, L)
        o0 = G.init()
        pre, evs, _ = GK.run_with(G, o0, h)
        for u in range(L):
            for sig in removals(G, h[u]):
                gone = set(entry_comps(G, h[u])) - set(entry_comps(G, sig))
                # the removal changes the value of some component (including removal of a negative teaching current): take the set of components “whose value changed”
                name, I = h[u]
                changed = {('I', j) for j in range(G.n_out) if I[j] != sig[1][j]} | \
                          {('in', i) for i in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[name][i] != G.pat[sig[0]][i]}
                aff, R = entry_slot_reach(G, h, u, changed)
                pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, pre[u], [sig] + h[u + 1:], t_start=u)
                for t in range(u, L):
                    for e in evs[t].values():
                        reach = aff(e) if t == u else bool(e['reads'] & R[t])
                        if not reach:
                            n_cmp += 1
                            if not GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                                n_viol += 1
        n_inst += 1
    print(f'  R3σ (field-entry non-interference): 150 random K_g members (including negative teaching current and relaxation), {n_cmp} comparisons of slot-unreachable events, {n_viol} changed after removal')
    # Independent verification of the S* antecedents: on random histories, the removal witnesses of S*-class events are rechecked by a full rerun; both ends of the Ch_0 chain of an S*-class event are recomputed independently from the event's read set and the letter
    rng = random.Random(7)
    tot = {'S*₀': 0, 'S*₁': 0, '⊥_aux': 0, 'S': 0, '⊥_σOpen': 0, '⊥_σOver': 0, 'continuation': 0, 'no chain': 0}
    rerun_fail = 0
    open_neg = 0
    for _ in range(120):
        G = random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = random_hist(rng, G, L)
        T = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=T)
        ch0, chaux, _ = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, G.init(), lookback=3, tmax=L - 1)
        for r in rows:
            tot[r['cls']] += 1
            if r['cls'] == 'S*₀':
                sig, _w = r['sd0'][0]
                t = r['e']['t']
                pre2, evs2, _ = GK.run_with(G, I.pre[t], [sig], t_start=t)
                if GK.same_event(evs2, r['e']):
                    rerun_fail += 1
            if r['cls'] == '⊥_σOpen':
                if any(F(x) < 0 for x in I.hist[r['e']['t']][1]):
                    open_neg += 1
    print(f'  Source classes (120 random members, T = all synapses, effective T events): ' + '; '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in tot.items()) +
          f'; S*₀ removal witnesses failing the full rerun: {rerun_fail}; ⊥_σOpen with negative teaching current at that beat: {open_neg}')
    print('  Reading: “continuation” = removing an earlier field entry changes the event, but the event is unreachable on the slot hypergraph with Structural Positions deleted — the dependence propagates only through Trace-Responses already left (all are relaxations reading their own position); this is in-field continuation, not field-entry dependence;')
    print('        ⊥_σOpen = the removal dependence can propagate through non-structural positions but no actual chain exists (the dependence passes only through a firing that did not occur, e.g. when an inhibitory negative current is removed); by negative tier (c) it stays ⊥, and no 1 is issued.')
    print()

# ---------------- 5: comparison with D16 (F-layer Src/Mark) ----------------
class FM:
    """F-class mechanism model (minimal subset of the MTM of Chapter 3, A.4): Structural Positions, out-of-boundary pulses (by letter), mechanisms (constant/linear law, carrier B/out-of-boundary, optionally gated by letter)."""
    def __init__(self, sites, s0, T, eps, pulses, mechs):
        self.sites, self.s0, self.T, self.eps = sites, dict(zip(sites, s0)), T, eps
        self.pulses, self.mechs = pulses, mechs          # pulses: {letter: [(position, amount)]}; mech: dict(name,tgt,car,law,reads,k,b,off,beats)
    def run(self, hist):
        s = dict(self.s0)
        ev_all = []
        for t, u in enumerate(hist):
            evs = []
            for (tg, a) in self.pulses.get(u, []):
                if a > self.eps:
                    evs.append(dict(kind='pulse', name=f'p{tg}', tgt=tg, c=F(a), car='out-of-boundary', letter=True, reads=()))
            for m in self.mechs:
                if m.get('beats') is not None and t not in m['beats']:
                    continue
                if u in m.get('off', ()):
                    continue
                c = m['b'] + (m['k'] * sum(s[r] for r in m['reads']) if m['law'] == 'lin' else 0)
                gated = bool(m.get('off')) and 'rest' in m.get('off', ())     # does not run under rest ⇒ occurrence fixed by the field-entry letter
                evs.append(dict(kind='mech', name=m['name'], tgt=m['tgt'], c=c, car=m['car'], letter=gated, reads=m['reads']))
            ev_all.append((t, u, dict(s), evs))
            for e in evs:
                if abs(e['c']) > self.eps:
                    s[e['tgt']] += e['c']
        return ev_all, s

def d16_mark(M, hist):
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] in M.T and abs(e['c']) > M.eps and e['car'] == 'out-of-boundary':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name']))
    return mark

def lemmaS_mark(M, hist):
    """Lemma S reading on the F model: clause 0 (an effective change by an out-of-boundary carrier or an out-of-boundary pulse, i.e. the action of the signal itself);
    clause 1 (S*): a write within B driven by the letter (its mechanism does not run under rest), with empty read set and the declared initial state as pre-beat state (the form of P4 in the F-model comparison; F positions are all Structural Positions, with no auxiliary state); removing the field entry (replacing the letter by rest) makes it not occur, and T carries no effective event under rest.
    F positions are all Structural Positions, so chains cannot cross beats, and clause 1 can hold only via alphabet-gated edges."""
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] not in M.T or abs(e['c']) <= M.eps:
                continue
            if e['car'] == 'out-of-boundary':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], 'clause 0'))
            elif e['letter'] and not e['reads'] and u != 'rest' and s == M.s0 and t == 0:
                # P5: after replacing this beat by rest, T carries no effective event
                h2 = list(hist); h2[t] = 'rest'
                ev2, _ = M.run(h2[:t + 1])
                if not any(x['tgt'] in M.T and abs(x['c']) > M.eps for x in ev2[t][3]):
                    mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], 'clause 1 (S*)'))
    return mark

def section_d16():
    print('5. Comparison with D16 (F-layer Src/Mark)')
    H = F(1, 2)
    seed = dict(name='seed', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(0,))
    reset_g = dict(name='reset', tgt='g', car='B', law='lin', reads=('g',), k=F(-1), b=F(0))
    A3 = FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
            [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0)),
             dict(name='amp', tgt='x', car='out-of-boundary', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=H, b=F(0), off=('rest',)), reset_g])
    B3 = FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
            [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0), off=('a',)), reset_g])
    h3 = ['rest', 'a', 'rest']
    B5 = FM(('x',), (1,), ('x',), F(0), {},
            [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',)),
             dict(name='e+', tgt='x', car='out-of-boundary', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(1,)),
             dict(name='i-', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(-1), beats=(1,))])
    B5p = FM(('x',), (0,), ('x',), F(0), {},
             [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',))])
    h5 = ['w', 'rest']
    for nm, M, h, claim in (('A3 (out-of-boundary carrier amplification +x/2 cancels in-boundary shallowing)', A3, h3, 'Chapter 3: r = 1'),
                            ('B3 (signal stops only; in-boundary shallowing)', B3, h3, 'Chapter 3: r = 0'),
                            ('B5 (alphabet-gated in-boundary constant law + out-/in-boundary cancelling pair)', B5, h5, 'Chapter 3: r holds on both sides (ensured by the cancelling pair)'),
                            ('B5′ (B5 without the cancelling pair, initial value 0)', B5p, h5, 'Chapter 3: undetermined')):
        m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        s1 = int(any(m1[i] for i in M.T)); s2 = int(any(m2[i] for i in M.T))
        print(f'  {nm}: D16 Src_F = {s1} (Mark {m1["x"]}); r of Lemma S = {s2} ({m2["x"]}); {claim}')
    # F^σ-free subclass: random F models (mechanisms inside B without alphabet gating; out-of-boundary mechanisms and out-of-boundary pulses arbitrary), Mark sets compared position by position
    rng = random.Random(31)
    same = diff = 0
    for _ in range(600):
        sites = ('x', 'y', 'z')
        T = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, 3)))
        pulses = {a: [(rng.choice(sites), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 2))] for a in ('a', 'b') if rng.random() < 0.7}
        mechs = []
        for n in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
            car = rng.choice(['B', 'B', 'out-of-boundary'])
            law = rng.choice(['const', 'lin'])
            m = dict(name=f'm{n}', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car=car, law=law,
                     reads=(rng.choice(sites),) if law == 'lin' else (), k=F(rng.randint(-1, 2), 4), b=F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4))
            if car == 'out-of-boundary' and rng.random() < 0.5:
                m['off'] = ('rest',)
            mechs.append(m)
        M = FM(sites, (0, 0, 0), T, F(1, 100), pulses, mechs)
        h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
        try:
            m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        except Exception:
            continue
        a = {i: sorted((t, n) for t, n in m1[i]) for i in T}
        b = {i: sorted((t, n) for t, n, _c in m2[i]) for i in T}
        same += (a == b); diff += (a != b)
    print(f'  F^σ-free subclass, 600 random cases: Mark sets of Lemma S identical to D16 position by position in {same} cases, different in {diff} cases')
    rng = random.Random(32)
    dv = tot = 0
    kinds = set()
    for _ in range(600):
        sites = ('x', 'y')
        T = ('x',)
        mechs = [dict(name='g', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(rng.randint(1, 3), 4), off=('rest',))]
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            mechs.append(dict(name='m', tgt='x', car=rng.choice(['B', 'out-of-boundary']), law='lin', reads=('y',), k=F(rng.randint(-2, 2), 4), b=F(0)))
        M = FM(sites, (0, 0), T, F(1, 100), {}, mechs)
        h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
        m1, m2 = d16_mark(M, h), lemmaS_mark(M, h)
        tot += 1
        if bool(m1['x']) != bool(m2['x']):
            dv += 1
            kinds |= {c for (_t, _n, c) in m2['x'] if (_t, _n) not in m1['x']}
    print(f'  600 random cases with alphabet-gated writes inside B: Src values diverge in {dv} cases; all divergences come from {sorted(kinds)} (i.e. “writes driven by the letter with carrier inside B”, the location of interface Gap G-r2)')
    print()

def section_grid(S):
    """q, r and TraceSet evaluated group by group on the 108 groups of the frozen SNN parameter grid (the declared histories of snn_scan.py); Part and HDyn take the grouped results of the lp03_exact.py run record (Appendix B.2, second item)."""
    print('6. q, r and TraceSet on the 108 groups of the frozen SNN parameter grid (T = {w00, w10}; declared history: formation (A+teach, rest, rest)×n, rest 8, W is (A,rest,rest,B,rest,rest)×3)')
    W0s, APs, Ts, Ns = [F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]
    tab = {}
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product(W0s, APs, Ts, Ns):
        S.W0, S.A_PLUS = W0, AP
        G = GC.snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
        I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, len(form) + 8)
        ch0, chaux, _ = chains(I)
        rows = classify(I, ch0, chaux, G.init())
        rv = r_verdict(rows)[0]
        end = I.pre[len(hist)]
        q = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and I.form[k] for k in I.T)
        tr = '1[strict]' if (q and str(rv).startswith('1[')) else (0 if (not q or rv == 0) else '⊥')
        key = (int(q), str(rv) + '/' + r_verdict(rows)[1], str(tr))
        tab.setdefault(key, []).append((W0, AP, teach, n))
    S.W0, S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
    for (q, rv, tr), lst in sorted(tab.items()):
        ex = lst[0]
        print(f'  q = {q}, r = {rv}, TraceSet = {tr}: {len(lst)} groups (e.g. W0 = {ex[0]}, A₊ = {ex[1]}, teach {ex[2]}, n = {ex[3]})')
    nq0r = len([1 for (q, rv, tr), lst in tab.items() for _ in lst if q == 0])
    print(f'  Reading: groups with q = 0 have TraceSet = 0, regardless of r; in the 70 groups with q = 1, r is “1 under the strict-subdomain correspondence antecedents” throughout: most by S*₀ (formation beat 0, pre-beat state a resting state), the rest by S*₁ (rest at the start of the history, first firing at the second or third pairing, chain across beats via the membrane potential, additionally requiring EF1′). When the strict-subdomain correspondence antecedents are not declared, r = ⊥_src for these 70 groups.')
    print('  Comparison with the grouping of the core task run_lp03_exact.txt: in the 70 groups with q = 1, Part = 1; HDyn = 1 in 48 groups (originally “holds” 27, “FP” 12, “P” 3, “PH” 3 with W0 = 3/8, “FPH” 3 with W0 = 1/2 and A₊ = 1/4, n = 6) — '
          'these 48 groups have Q_R(T) = 1 and Fld = 1 under the strict-subdomain correspondence antecedents (⊥ when these are not declared); the remaining 22 groups with q = 1 obtain only Q_R(T) = 0 on the listed T (HDyn = 0: 7 groups by tier (a), 15 groups by tier (b), which requires B_FD; ⊥_Over when B_FD is not declared); Fld awaits T_max and is not read as 0.')
    print()

def section_aux(S):
    print('7. Regression on preset auxiliary states: a rest fixed point does not exclude nonzero auxiliary memory; endpoints λ, d_x, d_y set to 1')
    H = F(1, 2)
    def gate1(lam, dx, dy):
        return GC.Gate(1, 1, {'none': (0,), 'A': (1,)}, [lam], [F(1)], [F(0)], [dx], [dy], F(1, 8), F(0), F(0),
                       [[F(1, 4)]], [[F(0)]], [[F(2)]])
    G = gate1(F(0), F(1), H)
    o = ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])
    I = GK.Instance('preset eligibility-trace case', G, [('none', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o)
    o0 = ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(0)])
    I0 = GK.Instance('', G, [('none', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o0)
    c1 = I.evs[0].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 0)); c0 = I0.evs[0].get(('PLUS', (0, 0), 0))
    rows = classify(I, *chains(I)[:2], o)
    print(f'  Preset eligibility-trace case (n_in = n_out = 1, λ = 0, d_x = 1, d_y = 1/2, A₊ = 1/8, A₋ = 0, ρ = 0, b = 1/4; state w = b, v = 0, x = 1, y = 0):')
    print(f'    rest_state (P4R) = {rest_state(I, o)}; P4Z = {zero_aux(I, o)}; letter none + I = 1: PLUS = {fr(c1["c"]) if c1 else 0} when x = 1, PLUS = {fr(c0["c"]) if c0 else 0} when x = 0')
    print(f'    Strict-subdomain classification: {[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rows]}; r_R = {r_verdict(rows)[0]} (not judged 0)')
    rows0 = classify(I0, *chains(I0)[:2], o0)
    print(f'    Same parameters, auxiliary state 0: classes {[(ename(r["e"]), r["cls"]) for r in rows0]} (the PLUS component is 0, no effective write; without an entry stimulus the teaching current alone leaves no Trace-Response)')
    print('  Endpoint table (each row: λ, d_x, d_y; whether a nonzero stationary auxiliary state exists; P4R, P4Z on that state; class of the SF positive case on the zero auxiliary state):')
    for lam, dx, dy in [(F(1), H, H), (H, F(1), H), (H, H, F(1)), (F(1), F(1), F(1)), (H, H, H)]:
        Gx = gate1(lam, dx, dy)
        cand = []
        if lam == 1: cand.append(('v', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(1, 2)], [F(0)], [F(0)])))
        if dx == 1: cand.append(('x', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])))
        if dy == 1: cand.append(('y', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(1)])))
        res = []
        for nm, oo in cand:
            Ix = GK.Instance('', Gx, [('A', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oo)
            rx = classify(Ix, *chains(Ix)[:2], oo)
            res.append(f'{nm}≠0: P4R {rest_state(Ix, oo)}, P4Z {zero_aux(Ix, oo)}, classes {sorted({r["cls"] for r in rx})}')
        oz = Gx.init()
        Iz = GK.Instance('', Gx, [('A', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oz)
        rz = classify(Iz, *chains(Iz)[:2], oz)
        print(f'    λ = {lam}, d_x = {dx}, d_y = {dy}: ' + ('; '.join(res) if res else 'no nonzero stationary auxiliary state (for λ, d < 1 the auxiliary positions of a rest fixed point are necessarily 0)') +
              f'; zero auxiliary state, letter A + I = 1: classes {sorted({r["cls"] for r in rz})}')
    print('  Reading: nonzero stationary auxiliary states appear only when the corresponding coefficient is 1; on such a state P4R holds and P4Z fails, and the event is classed ⊥_aux (outside the source subdomain), not S*₀; on the zero auxiliary state S*₀ holds as usual.')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = GK.Instance('', G, FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    print(f'  Frozen SNN: λ = {G.lam[0]}, d_x = {G.dx[0]}, d_y = {G.dy[0]} (all < 1); at beat 0 the pre-beat state has P4R = {rest_state(P, P.pre[0])}, P4Z = {zero_aux(P, P.pre[0])} — within the strict subdomain, the S*₀ witness of Instance P is unchanged (Section 2).')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    P, N, out = section_snn(G)
    section_fld(G, P, N, out)
    section_checks()
    section_d16()
    section_grid(S)
    section_aux(S)
    print('End. The above are finite verifications and exact readings; proofs of the general propositions are in Section 5 of the main text.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.14  source\_r4.py  Criterion PS, Lemmas CH₀ and CH, Theorems SUB and STR, overdetermination, endpoint examples, D16 comparison, grid sources (4.3, 5.4, 5.8, 7.5)

~~~python
"""Participation-type source-certification criterion PS (main text 4.3, 5.4): per-write determination for Instances P, N and C1; TraceSet and Fld; case-by-case determination of the endpoint examples with preset auxiliary states; overdetermination cases; finite verification of Lemmas CH₀ and CH, Theorems SUB and STR, and the overdetermination family; comparison of D16 with the F model; q, r and TraceSet on the grid.
Criterion: chain Ch^D15 (actual events, functional support); admissible removal (any subset of components of the removal domain, no-opening condition g′ ≤ g, plus Trace-Response clamping across beats); M1₀ (same-beat), M1_aux (cross-beat, requires EF1′), M2 (NESS test for gate overdetermination, requires B_OD); reason codes ⊥_OD, ⊥_σOpen, ⊥_σOver; no chain, continuation.
In the output, the “strict subdomain” column is the classification of Appendix A.13, and the “PS” column is the verdict of this program.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 source_r4.py
"""
import sys, random, itertools, time
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import r_layer_witness as RW
import gate_criteria as GK
import source_r3 as S3
EPS = GK.EPS
NONE = GK.NONE
fr = GK.fr
ename, wname = S3.ename, S3.wname
removals, changed_comps, entry_comps = S3.removals, S3.changed_comps, S3.entry_comps
rest_state, zero_aux, null_letter = S3.rest_state, S3.zero_aux, S3.null_letter

def cname(c):
    return f"{'entry ' if c[0] == 'in' else 'teaching current I'}{c[1]}@{c[2]}"

# ---------------- Definition S-2′: actual action chain Ch^D15 ----------------
def tick_chain4(G, o, sigma, evs, carried, t):
    """Return (R, gate, carried_next, why). R: event key (kind, idx) → chain origin set (field-entry components (type, index, beat)).
    Edges: ('in', i) → XIN_i, SYN_ij (feeds into the gate only when c = w_ij ≠ 0), MINUS_ij (occurrence), PLUS_ij (component via x′_i); ('I', j) → INJ_j → γ_j;
        a carried v_j feeds into γ_j when λ_j ≠ 0 (D15: the partial derivative of vt with respect to v is λ_j); γ_j (present only when g_j = 1) → RESET_j, YIN_j, PLUS_ij (occurrence), MINUS_ij (via y′_j);
        a carried x_i enters the component of PLUS_ij when d_x,i ≠ 0, and a carried y_j enters the component of MINUS_ij when d_y,j ≠ 0; CLIP via its members on the chain.
    Across beats: when v_j resets it takes the origin set of γ_j; otherwise it takes the union of this beat's inputs (SYN, INJ), merged with the previous carried set when λ_j ≠ 0; likewise for x_i, y_j (the previous carried set is kept when d ≠ 0).
    The chain does not pass through nodes of the Structural Positions w."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sigma
    s = G.pat[name]
    ev = {(e['id'][0], e['id'][1]): e for e in evs.values()}
    R, why = {}, {}
    def add(key, reason, orig):
        if key in ev and orig:
            if key not in R:
                why[key] = reason
            R.setdefault(key, set()).update(orig)
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        if s[i]:
            add(('XIN', (i,)), f'entry {i}', {('in', i, t)})
            for j in range(G.n_out):
                add(('SYN', (i, j)), f'entry {i} transmitted via synapse w{i}{j}', {('in', i, t)})
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if I[j] != 0:
            add(('INJ', (j,)), f'teaching current I{j}', {('I', j, t)})
    gate, gsrc = {}, {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:                                   # g_j = 1
            orig, src = set(), []
            for i in range(G.n_in):
                k = ('SYN', (i, j))
                if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0:
                    orig |= R[k]; src.append(f'SYN{i}{j} ({fr(ev[k]["c"])})')
            if ('INJ', (j,)) in R:
                orig |= R[('INJ', (j,))]; src.append(f'INJ{j} ({fr(ev[("INJ", (j,))]["c"])})')
            if G.lam[j] != 0 and carried.get(('v', j)):
                orig |= carried[('v', j)]; src.append(f'carried v{j} (λ·v = {fr(G.lam[j] * v[j])})')
            if orig:
                gate[j] = orig; gsrc[j] = src
    for j in gate:
        add(('RESET', (j,)), f'firing γ{j}', gate[j]); add(('YIN', (j,)), f'firing γ{j}', gate[j])
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] != 0 else set())
        for j in range(G.n_out):
            key = ('PLUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                rs = []
                if j in gate: rs.append(f'firing γ{j} reaches the synapse by backpropagation (occurrence; inputs to γ{j}: ' + ', '.join(gsrc[j]) + ')')
                if xo: rs.append(f'x′{i} carried into field (component)')
                add(key, '; '.join(rs), gate.get(j, set()) | xo)
            key = ('MINUS', (i, j))
            if key in ev:
                yo = gate.get(j, set()) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] != 0 else set())
                add(key, f'entry {i} (occurrence)' + (f'; y′{j} carried into field (component)' if yo else ''), {('in', i, t)} | yo)
            key = ('CLIP', (i, j))
            mem = [m for m in (('PLUS', (i, j)), ('MINUS', (i, j))) if m in R]
            if key in ev and mem:
                add(key, 'clipping member on chain', set().union(*[R[m] for m in mem]))
    C2 = {}
    for j in range(G.n_out):
        if ('RESET', (j,)) in ev:
            if j in gate:
                C2[('v', j)] = set(gate[j])
        else:
            cs = set()
            for k in [('INJ', (j,))] + [('SYN', (i, j)) for i in range(G.n_in)]:
                if k in R and ev[k]['c'] != 0:
                    cs |= R[k]
            if G.lam[j] != 0 and carried.get(('v', j)):
                cs |= carried[('v', j)]
            if cs:
                C2[('v', j)] = cs
        yo = set(gate.get(j, set())) | (carried.get(('y', j), set()) if G.dy[j] != 0 else set())
        if yo:
            C2[('y', j)] = yo
    for i in range(G.n_in):
        xo = ({('in', i, t)} if s[i] else set()) | (carried.get(('x', i), set()) if G.dx[i] != 0 else set())
        if xo:
            C2[('x', i)] = xo
    return R, gate, C2, why

def chains4(I):
    ch0, chaux, why = {}, {}, {}
    carried = {}
    for t in range(len(I.hist)):
        R0, _, _, w0 = tick_chain4(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], {}, t)
        Ra, _, carried, wa = tick_chain4(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], I.evs[t], carried, t)
        ch0[t], chaux[t], why[t] = R0, Ra, (w0, wa)
    return ch0, chaux, why

# ---------------- Admissible removal and certification methods ----------------
def gates_at(G, o, sig, kw):
    return G.step(o, sig, **kw)[1]['g']

def no_activation(G, o, sig, sig2, kw):
    """No-opening condition: after the removal no gate exceeds the actual gate (no gate that is actually closed is opened)."""
    g1, g2 = gates_at(G, o, sig, kw), gates_at(G, o, sig2, kw)
    return all(b <= a for a, b in zip(g1, g2))

def event_change(evs2, e):
    t, eid = e['t'], e['id']
    if eid not in evs2:
        return 'does not occur'
    if evs2[eid]['c'] != e['c']:
        return f"component {fr(e['c'])}→{fr(evs2[eid]['c'])}"
    return None

def m1_0(I, e):
    """Same-beat admissible-removal witnesses: [(σ′, change, set of removed components)]; also returns the removals that change e but are not admissible (they open a gate)."""
    t = e['t']
    ok, bad = [], []
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        evs2 = RW.Ev(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
        ch = event_change(evs2, e)
        if ch is None:
            continue
        S = changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[t], sig, t)
        (ok if no_activation(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], sig, I.kw) else bad).append((sig, ch, S))
    return ok, bad

def clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t):
    """Remove sig at beat u and rerun from u to t; the Structural Positions at every beat after u are clamped to their actual values (Trace-Response clamping). Returns (evs2, whether no-opening holds)."""
    def interv(tau, o):
        if tau > u:
            return (I.pre[tau][0], o[1], o[2], o[3])
        return o
    pre2, evs2, g2 = GK.run_with(I.G, I.pre[u], [sig] + I.hist[u + 1:t + 1], t_start=u, interv=interv, **I.kw)
    noact = all(all(b <= a for a, b in zip(I.gates[tau], g2[tau])) for tau in range(u, t + 1))
    return evs2, noact, g2

def m1_aux(I, e, lookback=6):
    t = e['t']
    for u in range(max(0, t - lookback), t):
        for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[u]):
            evs2, noact, _ = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t)
            if noact and not GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                return (u, sig, changed_comps(I.G, I.hist[u], sig, u), event_change(evs2[t], e))
    return None

def gate_summands(G, o, sig, j):
    """Actual summands of the integral vt_j of gate j: ('dom', v_j) → λ_j v_j; ('in', i) → w_ij (s_i = 1); ('I', j) → I_j."""
    w, v, x, y = o
    name, I = sig
    s = G.pat[name]
    out = [(('dom', 'v', j), G.lam[j] * v[j])]
    out += [(('in', i), w[i][j]) for i in range(G.n_in) if s[i]]
    if I[j] != 0:
        out.append((('I', j), F(I[j])))
    return out

def m2_ness(I, e):
    """M2 (NESS): the signal dependence of e runs through gate γ_j (occurrence of PLUS, component of MINUS); some actual sufficient subset contains a positive signal contribution c and becomes insufficient once c is removed.
    Sufficiency is judged with all negative contributions present (N = sum of the negative contributions): ∃ A ⊆ the other positive contributions with Σ A + N < θ ≤ Σ A + N + c."""
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    if kind not in ('PLUS', 'MINUS'):
        return None
    t = e['t']; j = idx[1]
    if not I.gates[t][j]:
        return None
    if kind == 'MINUS' and I.G.Am == 0:
        return None
    sm = gate_summands(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], j)
    N = sum((c for _, c in sm if c < 0), F(0))
    pos = [(tag, c) for tag, c in sm if c > 0]
    th = I.G.theta[j]
    for k, (tag, c) in enumerate(pos):
        if tag[0] == 'dom':
            continue
        others = pos[:k] + pos[k + 1:]
        for r in range(len(others) + 1):
            for A in itertools.combinations(others, r):
                sA = sum((a for _, a in A), F(0))
                if sA + N < th <= sA + N + c:
                    return (tag, c, [a for a, _ in A], N)
    return None

def gate_overdetermined(I, e):
    """The gate γ_j of PLUS/MINUS stays open under every same-beat admissible removal (the gate is overdetermined by in-boundary contributions)."""
    t = e['t']; j = e['id'][1][1]
    if not I.gates[t][j]:
        return False
    for sig in removals(I.G, I.hist[t]):
        if no_activation(I.G, I.pre[t], I.hist[t], sig, I.kw) and not gates_at(I.G, I.pre[t], sig, I.kw)[j]:
            return False
    return True

def coparticipants(I, e):
    """In-boundary conditions that co-participate with the signal in e (each one with a nonzero reading is listed): Trace-Responses already left, auxiliary positions, baseline."""
    G, o = I.G, I.pre[e['t']]
    w, v, x, y = o
    kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
    out = []
    if kind in ('PLUS', 'MINUS', 'CLIP'):
        i, j = idx
        if G.lam[j] * v[j] != 0:
            out.append(f'v{j} = {fr(v[j])} (via gate)')
        sig = I.hist[e['t']]
        trace = [f'w{a}{j} Trace-Response {fr(w[a][j] - G.b[a][j])}' for a in range(G.n_in) if G.pat[sig[0]][a] and abs(w[a][j] - G.b[a][j]) > EPS]
        if trace:
            out.append('Trace-Responses already left, via gate: ' + ', '.join(trace))
        if kind == 'PLUS' and G.dx[i] * x[i] != 0:
            out.append(f'x{i} = {fr(x[i])} (component)')
        if kind == 'MINUS' and G.dy[j] * y[j] != 0:
            out.append(f'y{j} = {fr(y[j])} (component)')
    return out or ['none (auxiliary positions are 0; the synapses read by the gate carry no Trace-Response)']

def classify4(I, lookback=6, tmax=None, old=True, aux=True):
    ch0, chaux, why = chains4(I)
    rows = []
    tmax = I.t1 if tmax is None else tmax
    oldcls = {}
    if old:
        c0, ca, _ = S3.chains(I)
        for r in S3.classify(I, c0, ca, I.pre[0], lookback=lookback, tmax=tmax):
            oldcls[r['e']['id']] = r['cls']
    for t in range(0, tmax + 1):
        for e in I.evs[t].values():
            if e['tgt'] not in I.T or abs(e['c']) <= EPS:
                continue
            key = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
            o0 = ch0[t].get(key, set()); oa = chaux[t].get(key, set())
            ok, bad = m1_0(I, e)
            wit = None
            if ok:
                cls, wit = 'M1₀', ok
            else:
                ma = m1_aux(I, e, lookback) if (aux and oa) else None
                if ma:
                    cls, wit = 'M1_aux', ma
                else:
                    m2 = m2_ness(I, e) if oa else None
                    if m2:
                        cls, wit = 'M2', m2
                    elif oa:
                        if e['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') and gate_overdetermined(I, e):
                            cls = '⊥_OD'
                        elif bad:
                            cls = '⊥_σOpen'
                        else:
                            cls = '⊥_σOver'
                    else:
                        w0 = S3.sigdep0(I, e)
                        wm = None if w0 else S3.sigdep_m(I, e, lookback)
                        if w0 or wm:
                            w_ = (t, w0[0][0]) if w0 else wm
                            cls = '⊥_σOpen' if S3.wfree_reach(I, e, w_) else 'continuation'
                        else:
                            cls = 'no chain'
            p4r = rest_state(I, I.pre[t])
            rows.append(dict(e=e, cls=cls, wit=wit, o0=o0, oa=oa, why=why[t][1].get(key, '—'),
                             p4r=p4r, p4z=zero_aux(I, I.pre[t]), old=oldcls.get(e['id'])))
    return rows

def r_verdict4(rows):
    cl = [r['cls'] for r in rows]
    if 'M1₀' in cl:
        return '1', 'M1₀'
    if 'M1_aux' in cl:
        return '1[EF1′]', 'M1_aux'
    if 'M2' in cl:
        return '1[B_OD]', 'M2'
    if all(c in ('no chain', 'continuation') for c in cl):
        return 0, 'S⁻ (reading N)'
    return '⊥', '/'.join(sorted({c for c in cl if c.startswith('⊥')}))

def count(rows, key='cls'):
    cnt = {}
    for r in rows:
        cnt[r[key]] = cnt.get(r[key], 0) + 1
    return '; '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(cnt.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])))

def show_wit(r):
    c = r['cls']
    w = r['wit']
    if c == 'M1₀':
        sig, ch, S = w[0]
        return f'removal to {sig[0]}+I=({",".join(fr(F(a)) for a in sig[1])}) (removed {", ".join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}): {ch}'
    if c == 'M1_aux':
        u, sig, S, ch = w
        return f'beat {u}: removal to {sig[0]}+I=({",".join(fr(F(a)) for a in sig[1])}) (removed {", ".join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}), rerun with Trace-Response clamping: {ch}'
    if c == 'M2':
        tag, cc, A, N = w
        return f'NESS: signal contribution {tag}={fr(cc)}, the sufficient subset also contains {A or "∅"} (sum of negative contributions {fr(N)})'
    return '—'

# ---------------- 1, 2: SNN instances ----------------
def section_snn(G):
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = GK.Instance('s1 frozen declared history', G, FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    N = GK.Instance('(A+teach 1, B+teach 9/16, rest, rest, rest, A)', G,
                    [('A', (F(1), 0)), ('B', (F(9, 16), 0)), NONE, NONE, NONE, ('A', (0, 0))], 1)
    print('1. Source re-certification of the frozen SNN Instance P (participation-type criterion PS; the effective events acting on T = {w00, w10}, one by one)')
    rows = classify4(P)
    print(f'  {len(rows)} effective events; PS classification: {count(rows)}; strict-subdomain classification: {count(rows, "old")}')
    shown = set()
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']
        tag = (e['id'][0], P.hist[e['t']][0], P.hist[e['t']][1] != (0, 0))
        if e['t'] != 0 and tag in shown:
            continue
        shown.add(tag)
        print(f"    {ename(e)} | c = {fr(e['c'])} | letter {P.hist[e['t']][0]}+I=({','.join(fr(F(a)) for a in P.hist[e['t']][1])}) | "
              f"P4R {int(r['p4r'])} P4Z {int(r['p4z'])} | strict subdomain {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']}")
        print(f"        chain: {r['why']}; origins {sorted(cname(c) for c in r['o0'])}")
        print(f"        source bearing: {show_wit(r)}; co-participation: {'; '.join(coparticipants(P, e))}")
    print('  All 36, one by one (event | strict subdomain → PS | same-beat admissible-removal witness):')
    for r in rows:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | {r['old']} → {r['cls']} | {show_wit(r)}")
    rv = r_verdict4(rows)
    print(f'  r_R(T) = {rv[0]} (basis {rv[1]}; consumes neither P4R, P4Z nor EF1′; M1₀ uses only same-beat removals)')
    n_in_only = 0
    for r in rows:
        e = r['e']; t = e['t']
        for sig in removals(G, P.hist[t]):
            if tuple(sig[1]) != tuple(P.hist[t][1]):
                continue
            if no_activation(G, P.pre[t], P.hist[t], sig, P.kw) and event_change(RW.Ev(G, P.pre[t], sig, t, **P.kw), e):
                n_in_only += 1
                break
    print(f'  Check of the instance with a separately drawn boundary (teaching-current device placed in B, removal domain containing only entry components): of the 36 writes, {n_in_only} are certified by admissible removals that “remove only entries and keep the teaching current”'
          ' (only the removal witnesses are checked; the other declarations of that instance are not redone: once the teaching current is placed in B, its carriers and event individuation must be redeclared)')
    nM1 = sum(1 for r in rows if r['cls'] == 'M1₀')
    print(f'  Among the effective events acting on T, M1₀ certifies {nM1}/{len(rows)}; the 4 at beat 0 (formerly S*₀) and the 32 after them (formerly class S, with field entry co-participating with Trace-Responses already left and auxiliary positions) are all certified by M1₀')
    out = {'P': (P, rows, rv)}
    rowsN = classify4(N)
    rvN = r_verdict4(rowsN)
    print(f'  [Instance N] {N.name}: {len(rowsN)} effective events, PS {count(rowsN)} (strict subdomain {count(rowsN, "old")}); r_R(T) = {rvN[0]}')
    out['N'] = (N, rowsN, rvN)
    o1 = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(4, 3), F(0)], [F(1), F(1), F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)])
    C1 = GK.Instance('C1', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o1)
    rowsC = classify4(C1)
    print(f'  [Contrast C1] declared initial state v0 = 4/3, x0 = x1 = 1; history (rest+teach(0,1), rest, rest); T = {{w00}}:')
    for r in rowsC:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} | c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | strict subdomain {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']} (gate γ0 crosses the threshold from the preset v0 alone: λ·v0 = {fr(G.lam[0] * F(4, 3))} ≥ θ; the teaching current acts only on output 1, and there is no actual chain from a field-entry component to w00)")
    print(f'    r_R({{w00}}) = {r_verdict4(rowsC)[0]} ({r_verdict4(rowsC)[1]}; antecedents REC, ENT, R1, EF1′ and reading N); q = 1; TraceSet = 0')
    C1b = GK.Instance('C1′', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 1), ('w', 1, 1)], o0=o1)
    rowsB = classify4(C1b)
    print(f'  [Contrast C1 with T′ = {{w01, w11}}]')
    for r in rowsB:
        print(f"    {ename(r['e'])} | c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | strict subdomain {r['old']} (P4R, P4Z fail) → PS {r['cls']}; {show_wit(r)}; co-participation: {'; '.join(coparticipants(C1b, r['e']))}")
    print(f'    r_R(T′) = {r_verdict4(rowsB)[0]} (strict subdomain: 1 under RS, otherwise ⊥_RS; PS: teaching current I1 participates through the actual chain INJ1 → γ1 → PLUS, and the event does not occur once it is removed; the preset x0, x1 co-participate in the component and do not constitute a negative determination)')
    print()
    return P, N, out

def section_fld(G, out):
    import decision as DC
    print('2. TraceSet and the Fld verdict path (Instances P, N; model mode, algorithmic level, column A)')
    for nm in ('P', 'N'):
        I, rows, rv = out[nm]
        end = I.pre[len(I.hist)]
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        Iall = GK.Instance('', G, I.hist, I.t0, T=X)
        Tmax = [k for k in X if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Iall.form[k]]
        q = {k: (GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and bool(I.form[k])) for k in I.T}
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        j, jn = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
        trace = 1 if (all(q.values()) and rv[0] == '1') else (0 if not all(q.values()) or rv[0] == 0 else '⊥')
        Q = GK.k_and(trace, j['Part'], j['HDyn'])
        print(f'  [Instance {nm}] T = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in I.T)}}}: q = ' + ', '.join(f'{wname(k)} {int(v)} (window-end z = {fr(GK.zk(G, end, k))})' for k, v in q.items()) +
              f'; r = {rv[0]}; Ident = 1 (POS); HistEv = 1 (REC); TraceSet = {trace}')
        print(f'    Part = {j["Part"]}, HDyn = {j["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: {jn["Part"]}, {jn["HDyn"]}); Q_R(T) = {Q}; T_max = {{{",".join(wname(k) for k in Tmax)}}}')
        if set(Tmax) != set(I.T):
            IT = GK.Instance(I.name, G, I.hist, I.t0, T=Tmax)
            rT = r_verdict4(classify4(IT, old=False))[0]
            DT = DC.Decider(IT)
            jT = DC.judge(IT, DT, True)
            qT = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and IT.form[k] for k in Tmax)
            QT = GK.k_and(1 if (qT and rT == '1') else '⊥', jT['Part'], jT['HDyn'])
            print(f'    on T_max: r = {rT}, Part = {jT["Part"]}, HDyn = {jT["HDyn"]}; Q_R(T_max) = {QT}')
        else:
            QT = Q
        fld = 1 if Q == 1 else (0 if QT == 0 else '⊥')
        print(f'    Fld = {fld}' + (' (algorithmic level; Q_R = 1 on the witness T, read back by Theorem R7; r from M1₀; for the other antecedents see the certificate antecedent table_strict subdomain)' if fld == 1 else
                                   ' (Q_R(T_max) = 0; HDyn tier (a), antecedents MOD-R, R2/R3, REC, ENT, POS, BEAT-R and EF1′; does not consume B_FD)'))
    print()

# ---------------- 3: former ⊥_aux cases and endpoint regression (case by case) ----------------
def gate1(lam, dx, dy, Am=F(0), b=F(1, 4), vreset=F(0)):
    return GC.Gate(1, 1, {'none': (0,), 'A': (1,)}, [lam], [F(1)], [vreset], [dx], [dy], F(1, 8), Am, F(0),
                   [[b]], [[F(0)]], [[F(2)]])

def section_aux():
    print('3. Former ⊥_aux cases and endpoint regression: case-by-case determination (whether the signal participated in the effective trace-leaving through an actual route; whether the verdict changes, and on what basis)')
    H = F(1, 2)
    cases = []
    G = gate1(F(0), F(1), H)
    cases.append(('preset eligibility-trace case', G, ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)]), ('none', (F(1),))))
    for Am in (F(0), F(1, 16)):
        for lam, dx, dy in itertools.product((H, F(1)), repeat=3):
            Gx = gate1(lam, dx, dy, Am)
            cand = []
            if lam == 1: cand.append(('v=1/2', ([[F(1, 4)]], [H], [F(0)], [F(0)])))
            if dx == 1: cand.append(('x=1', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(1)], [F(0)])))
            if dy == 1: cand.append(('y=1', ([[F(1, 4)]], [F(0)], [F(0)], [F(1)])))
            if len(cand) > 1:
                cand.append(('all nonzero', ([[F(1, 4)]], [H if lam == 1 else F(0)], [F(1) if dx == 1 else F(0)], [F(1) if dy == 1 else F(0)])))
            cand.append(('zero auxiliary state', Gx.init()))
            for nm, oo in cand:
                for sig in (('A', (F(1),)), ('none', (F(1),)), ('A', (F(0),))):
                    cases.append((f'A₋={fr(Am)} λ={fr(lam)} d_x={fr(dx)} d_y={fr(dy)} {nm}', Gx, oo, sig))
    tab = {}
    n_case = n_ev = 0
    lines = []
    for nm, Gx, oo, sig in cases:
        I = GK.Instance(nm, Gx, [sig], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=oo)
        rows = classify4(I)
        n_case += 1
        for r in rows:
            n_ev += 1
            k = (r['old'], r['cls'])
            tab[k] = tab.get(k, 0) + 1
            if r['old'] == '⊥_aux' or nm.startswith('preset eligibility-trace case'):
                lines.append(f"    {nm} | {sig[0]}+I={fr(F(sig[1][0]))} | P4R {int(r['p4r'])} P4Z {int(r['p4z'])} | {ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])} | strict subdomain {r['old']} → PS {r['cls']}"
                             f" | chain: {r['why']} | {show_wit(r)} | co-participation: {'; '.join(coparticipants(I, r['e']))}")
    print(f'  {n_case} cases in all (the preset eligibility-trace case; A₋ ∈ {{0, 1/16}} × the 8 combinations of λ, d_x, d_y ∈ {{1/2, 1}} × each nonzero stationary auxiliary state and the zero auxiliary state × letters A+I=1, none+I=1, A+I=0), {n_ev} effective writes.')
    print('  Former ⊥_aux events, one by one (every event the strict subdomain judges ⊥_aux is listed):')
    for ln in lines:
        print(ln)
    print('  Summary (strict subdomain → PS: number of events): ' + '; '.join(f'{a} → {b}: {c}' for (a, b), c in sorted(tab.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0]))))
    print('  Reading: every former ⊥_aux event has an actual chain starting from a field-entry component and a same-beat admissible-removal witness (M1₀), so the signal participated in the effective trace-leaving; preset auxiliary positions (the nonzero ones among x, v, y) are listed only as co-participants'
          ' and do not constitute a negative determination (declared source reading). The former S*₀ positive cases on the zero auxiliary state are likewise certified by M1₀. No case is changed from ⊥_aux to 0 or kept at ⊥.')
    print()

# ---------------- 4: overdetermination cases (case by case) ----------------
def section_od():
    print('4. Overdetermination cases: the signal participates through an actual route, yet no single-item removal changes the write (case by case)')
    H = F(1, 2)
    # Case OD-1: overdetermination between signals (each of the two signals suffices to cross the threshold); x0 is left by the entry at beat 0 (cross-beat auxiliary memory)
    G2 = GC.Gate(2, 1, {'none': (0, 0), 'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'ab': (1, 1)}, [H], [F(1)], [F(0)], [F(1), F(1)], [H, H],
                 F(1, 8), F(0), F(0), [[F(1, 4)], [F(1)]], [[F(0)], [F(0)]], [[F(2)], [F(2)]])
    I1 = GK.Instance('OD-1', G2, [('a', (F(0),)), ('b', (F(1),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)])
    rows = [r for r in classify4(I1) if r['e']['t'] == 1]
    e = rows[0]['e']
    singles = []
    for sig in removals(G2, I1.hist[1]):
        S = changed_comps(G2, I1.hist[1], sig, 1)
        evs2 = RW.Ev(G2, I1.pre[1], sig, 1)
        singles.append(f"remove {', '.join(cname(x) for x in sorted(S))}: {event_change(evs2, e) or 'unchanged'}")
    print(f"  OD-1 (signal-signal overdetermination): beat 1, letter b (w10 = 1 crosses the threshold alone) + I = 1 (crosses the threshold alone), x0 = 1 left by the entry at beat 0; {ename(e)} c = {fr(e['c'])}")
    print(f"    removals one by one: {'; '.join(singles)}")
    print(f"    verdict: {rows[0]['cls']} ({show_wit(rows[0])}) — no single-item removal changes it, while under joint removal it does not occur; the removal domain by definition contains any subset of components, so M1₀ certifies it; proved and closed.")
    # Cases OD-2, OD-3: overdetermination between the signal and a preset auxiliary position (v)
    for tag, Iv in (('OD-2', F(1, 4)), ('OD-3', F(1))):
        Gx = gate1(F(1), F(1), H)
        I = GK.Instance(tag, Gx, [('none', (Iv,))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=([[F(1, 4)]], [F(1)], [F(1)], [F(0)]))
        r = classify4(I)[0]
        print(f"  {tag} (overdetermination of signal and preset membrane potential): λ = 1, preset v = 1 (λv = 1 = θ, crosses the threshold alone), x = 1, letter none+I = {fr(Iv)}; {ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}")
        print(f"    chain: {r['why']}; same-beat removal of I: the gate stays open (λv ≥ θ) and the write is unchanged; no earlier beat")
        print(f"    verdict: {r['cls']}" + (f" ({show_wit(r)}; corresponding antecedent B_OD)" if r['cls'] == 'M2' else ' (NESS fails: the subset {I}, which contains I but not λv, does not suffice to cross the threshold; r is not judged 0)'))
    # Case OD-4: certifiable across beats (the reset value v_reset = θ makes the gate fire on consecutive beats)
    Gx = gate1(F(1), F(1), H, vreset=F(1))
    I = GK.Instance('OD-4', Gx, [('A', (F(1),)), ('none', (F(1, 4),))], 0, T=[('w', 0, 0)])
    r = [x for x in classify4(I) if x['e']['t'] == 1][0]
    print(f"  OD-4 (same-beat overdetermination, certifiable across beats): λ = 1, v_reset = θ = 1; at beat 0 A+I=1 fires (v resets to 1), at beat 1 none+I=1/4; {ename(r['e'])} c = {fr(r['e']['c'])}")
    print(f"    same-beat removal of I@1: the gate stays open (λv = 1); verdict: {r['cls']} ({show_wit(r)}; the route runs through the auxiliary positions v, x and requires EF1′)")
    print('  Reading: overdetermination falls into three classes. Overdetermination between signals is certified by joint removal (M1₀); same-beat overdetermination by in-boundary contributions, where a signal at an earlier beat determines that in-boundary contributor through auxiliary positions, is certified by removal with Trace-Response clamping (M1_aux, requires EF1′); '
          'the rest are certified by NESS (M2, requires B_OD) or recorded as ⊥_OD. ⊥_OD means only that the certification methods of this paper are insufficient; it is not 0.')
    print()

# ---------------- 5: finite verification of Lemmas CH₀, CH and Theorems SUB, STR, and random source classification ----------------
def section_checks():
    print('5. Finite verification')
    rng = random.Random(20261102)
    n_ch = v_ch = n_bad = bad_noorig = 0
    for _ in range(150):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 8)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=X)
        ch0, _, _ = chains4(I)
        for t in range(L):
            for sig in removals(G, h[t]):
                S = changed_comps(G, h[t], sig, t)
                ok = no_activation(G, I.pre[t], h[t], sig, I.kw)
                evs2 = RW.Ev(G, I.pre[t], sig, t, **I.kw)
                for e in I.evs[t].values():
                    if e['tgt'][0] != 'w' or abs(e['c']) <= EPS or event_change(evs2, e) is None:
                        continue
                    o0 = ch0[t].get((e['id'][0], e['id'][1]), set())
                    if ok:
                        n_ch += 1; v_ch += (not (S & o0))
                    else:
                        n_bad += 1; bad_noorig += (not (S & o0))
    print(f'  Lemma CH₀ (same beat: an admissible removal changes an effective write ⇒ the removed components belong to its Ch^D15 origins): 150 random members, admissible and changing {n_ch} times, violations {v_ch}; '
          f'not admissible (opening a gate) yet changing {n_bad} times, of which the removed components are not among the origins {bad_noorig} times (so the no-opening condition cannot be dropped)')
    rng = random.Random(20261103)
    n4 = v4 = v3 = 0
    for _ in range(120):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        X = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=X)
        _, chA, _ = chains4(I)
        _, chO, _ = S3.chains(I)
        for u in range(L - 1):
            for sig in removals(G, h[u]):
                S = changed_comps(G, h[u], sig, u)
                _, _, g2 = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, L - 1)
                for t in range(u + 1, L):
                    if not all(all(b <= a for a, b in zip(I.gates[tau], g2[tau])) for tau in range(u, t + 1)):
                        break
                    evs2, _, _ = clamped_rerun(I, u, sig, t)
                    for e in I.evs[t].values():
                        if e['tgt'][0] != 'w' or abs(e['c']) <= EPS or GK.same_event(evs2, e):
                            continue
                        k = (e['id'][0], e['id'][1])
                        n4 += 1
                        v4 += not (S & chA[t].get(k, set()))
                        v3 += not (S & chO[t].get(k, set()))
    print(f'  Lemma CH (across beats: a removal with Trace-Response clamping and no-opening throughout changes an effective write ⇒ the removed components belong to its cross-beat Ch^D15 origins): 120 random members, changing {n4} times, '
          f'violations under Ch^D15 {v4}; violations under the strict-subdomain chain (LEAK component in place of λ) {v3} (the strict-subdomain chain disagrees with functional support at λ ∈ {{0, 1}} or v = 0)')
    # Theorem SUB: under P4R (or the weaker P4G ∧ P5ₑ) every effective write is certified by M1₀; Theorem STR: M1₀ fails only for RELAX, gate-overdetermined PLUS (s_i = 0), and CLIP none of whose members is certifiable
    rng = random.Random(7)
    tot = {}
    sub_n = sub_v = g_n = g_v = str_v = 0
    old_new = {}
    for _ in range(120):
        G = S3.random_gate(rng)
        L = rng.randint(3, 7)
        h = S3.random_hist(rng, G, L)
        T = [('w', i, j) for i in range(G.n_in) for j in range(G.n_out)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 1, T=T)
        rows = classify4(I, lookback=3, tmax=L - 1)
        for r in rows:
            tot[r['cls']] = tot.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
            k = (r['old'], r['cls']); old_new[k] = old_new.get(k, 0) + 1
            e = r['e']; t = e['t']
            if r['p4r']:
                sub_n += 1; sub_v += (r['cls'] != 'M1₀')
            nl = null_letter(G)
            p4g = not any(gates_at(G, I.pre[t], nl, I.kw))
            p5e = event_change(RW.Ev(G, I.pre[t], nl, t, **I.kw), e) is not None
            if p4g and p5e and h[t] != nl:
                g_n += 1; g_v += (r['cls'] != 'M1₀')
            if r['cls'] != 'M1₀':
                kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
                okk = (kind == 'RELAX') or (kind == 'PLUS' and not G.pat[h[t][0]][idx[0]] and gate_overdetermined(I, e)) or \
                      (kind == 'CLIP')
                str_v += (not okk)
    print(f'  Theorem SUB: effective writes whose pre-beat state satisfies P4R {sub_n}, non-M1₀ {sub_v}; those satisfying P4G ∧ P5ₑ (no gate opens at rest, e changes at rest) {g_n}, non-M1₀ {g_v}')
    print(f'  Theorem STR (lo ≥ 0): the effective writes for which M1₀ fails all belong to RELAX, PLUS with s_i = 0 and an overdetermined gate, or CLIP: violations {str_v}')
    print('  Random source classification (120 random members, T = all synapses; seeds as in the strict subdomain): ' + '; '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(tot.items())))
    print('  Strict subdomain → PS (number of events): ' + '; '.join(f'{a} → {b}: {c}' for (a, b), c in sorted(old_new.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0]))))
    # Overdetermination family: λ = 1, v_reset ∈ {0, 1, 3/2}, random preset auxiliary states (including suprathreshold v); built to produce gate-overdetermined writes, testing M1_aux, M2, ⊥_OD and Theorem STR
    rng = random.Random(20261104)
    tot2, str2, m2chk, auxchk = {}, 0, 0, 0
    n2 = 0
    for _ in range(200):
        n_in, n_out = 2, 1
        pat = {'none': (0, 0), 'a': (1, 0), 'b': (0, 1), 'ab': (1, 1)}
        vr = rng.choice([F(0), F(1), F(3, 2)])
        G = GC.Gate(n_in, n_out, pat, [F(1)], [F(1)], [vr], [rng.choice([F(1, 2), F(1)]) for _ in range(n_in)], [F(1, 2)],
                    F(1, 8), F(rng.randint(0, 1), 16), F(0), [[F(rng.randint(1, 4), 8)] for _ in range(n_in)],
                    [[F(0)] for _ in range(n_in)], [[F(2)] for _ in range(n_in)])
        o0 = ([[G.b[i][0]] for i in range(n_in)], [F(rng.randint(0, 6), 4)], [F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2) for _ in range(n_in)], [F(rng.randint(0, 2), 2)])
        L = rng.randint(2, 5)
        h = [(rng.choice(list(pat)), (F(rng.choice([0, 0, 1, 2, 4]), 4),)) for _ in range(L)]
        I = GK.Instance('', G, h, 0, T=[('w', i, 0) for i in range(n_in)], o0=o0)
        rows = classify4(I, lookback=4, old=False)
        for r in rows:
            n2 += 1
            tot2[r['cls']] = tot2.get(r['cls'], 0) + 1
            e = r['e']; t = e['t']
            if r['cls'] == 'M2':
                tag, c, A, N = r['wit']
                sA = sum((a for (tg, a) in gate_summands(G, I.pre[t], h[t], e['id'][1][1]) if tg in A), F(0))
                m2chk += not (sA + N < G.theta[0] <= sA + N + c and not m1_0(I, e)[0])
            if r['cls'] == 'M1_aux':
                u, sig, S, _ch = r['wit']
                auxchk += not (S & r['oa'])
            if r['cls'] not in ('M1₀',):
                kind, idx = e['id'][0], e['id'][1]
                okk = (kind == 'RELAX') or (kind == 'PLUS' and not G.pat[h[t][0]][idx[0]] and gate_overdetermined(I, e)) or \
                      (kind == 'CLIP' and not any(m1_0(I, m)[0] for m in I.evs[t].values()
                                                  if m['id'][0] in ('PLUS', 'MINUS') and m['id'][1] == idx and abs(m['c']) > EPS))
                str2 += (not okk)
    print(f'  Overdetermination family (200 members: λ = 1, v_reset ∈ {{0, 1, 3/2}}, random preset auxiliary states): {n2} effective writes, classification ' + '; '.join(f'{k} {v}' for k, v in sorted(tot2.items())) +
          f'; M2 witness re-check failures {m2chk}; M1_aux witnesses whose removed components are not among the cross-beat origins {auxchk}; Theorem STR violations {str2}')
    print()

# ---------------- 6: source-fidelity condition of D16 ----------------
def part_mark(M, hist):
    """Participation-type source Mark^A on the F model: clause 0 (an effective change by an out-of-boundary carrier or an out-of-boundary pulse, i.e. the signal itself acts on a Structural Position);
    clause 1: the carrier is in B and the mechanism's occurrence is gated by the letter (it does not run at rest); the event does not occur once this beat's letter is removed to rest, and the removal starts no mechanism that does not actually run (no-opening).
    In F, mechanisms inside B read only the pre-beat values of Structural Positions (D12), so a same-beat removal does not change their components; cross-beat dependence runs only through Structural Positions (Trace-Responses already left) and is not counted."""
    ev_all, _ = M.run(hist)
    mark = {i: [] for i in M.T}
    for t, u, s, evs in ev_all:
        names = {e['name'] for e in evs}
        h2 = list(hist); h2[t] = 'rest'
        ev2 = M.run(h2[:t + 1])[0][t][3]
        n2 = {e['name'] for e in ev2}
        noact = n2 <= names
        for e in evs:
            if e['tgt'] not in M.T or abs(e['c']) <= M.eps:
                continue
            if e['car'] == 'out-of-boundary':
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], 'clause 0'))
            elif u != 'rest' and noact and e['name'] not in n2:
                mark[e['tgt']].append((t, e['name'], 'clause 1'))
    return mark

def section_d16():
    print('6. Source-fidelity condition of D16 (F-layer rules left unchanged; under which encoding conditions Src_F agrees with the participation-type source)')
    H = F(1, 2)
    seed = dict(name='seed', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(0,))
    reset_g = dict(name='reset', tgt='g', car='B', law='lin', reads=('g',), k=F(-1), b=F(0))
    A3 = S3.FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
               [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0)),
                dict(name='amp', tgt='x', car='out-of-boundary', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=H, b=F(0), off=('rest',)), reset_g])
    B3 = S3.FM(('x', 'g'), (0, 0), ('x',), F(0), {'a': [('g', 1)]},
               [seed, dict(name='decay', tgt='x', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=-H, b=F(0), off=('a',)), reset_g])
    h3 = ['rest', 'a', 'rest']
    B5 = S3.FM(('x',), (1,), ('x',), F(0), {},
               [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',)),
                dict(name='e+', tgt='x', car='out-of-boundary', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), beats=(1,)),
                dict(name='i-', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(-1), beats=(1,))])
    B5p = S3.FM(('x',), (0,), ('x',), F(0), {},
                [dict(name='g', tgt='x', car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(1), off=('rest',))])
    h5 = ['w', 'rest']
    for nm, M, h, claim in (('A3 (out-of-boundary carrier amplification + in-boundary shallowing)', A3, h3, 'Chapter 3: r = 1'), ('B3 (signal stops only; in-boundary shallowing)', B3, h3, 'Chapter 3: r = 0'),
                            ('B5 (alphabet-gated in-boundary constant law + out-/in-boundary cancelling pair)', B5, h5, 'Chapter 3: r holds on both sides'), ('B5′ (B5 without the cancelling pair)', B5p, h5, 'Chapter 3: undetermined')):
        m1, m2 = S3.d16_mark(M, h), part_mark(M, h)
        efs = not any(c == 'clause 1' for i in M.T for (_t, _n, c) in m2[i])
        print(f'  {nm}: D16 Src_F = {int(any(m1[i] for i in M.T))} ({m1["x"]}); participation-type Src^A = {int(any(m2[i] for i in M.T))} ({m2["x"]}); EF-σ {efs}; {claim}')
    stats = {}
    for label, seed_, gen in (('F^σ-free (mechanisms inside B without alphabet gating)', 31, 'free'), ('with alphabet-gated write inside B', 32, 'gated')):
        rng = random.Random(seed_)
        snd_v = eq_ef = n_ef = eqsrc = n = iff_v = noef_eq = noef = 0
        for _ in range(600):
            if gen == 'free':
                sites = ('x', 'y', 'z')
                T = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, 3)))
                pulses = {a: [(rng.choice(sites), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 2))] for a in ('a', 'b') if rng.random() < 0.7}
                mechs = []
                for k in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
                    car = rng.choice(['B', 'B', 'out-of-boundary']); law = rng.choice(['const', 'lin'])
                    m = dict(name=f'm{k}', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car=car, law=law,
                             reads=(rng.choice(sites),) if law == 'lin' else (), k=F(rng.randint(-1, 2), 4), b=F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4))
                    if car == 'out-of-boundary' and rng.random() < 0.5:
                        m['off'] = ('rest',)
                    mechs.append(m)
                M = S3.FM(sites, (0, 0, 0), T, F(1, 100), pulses, mechs)
                h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
            else:
                sites = ('x', 'y'); T = ('x',)
                mechs = [dict(name='g', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(rng.randint(1, 3), 4), off=('rest',))]
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    mechs.append(dict(name='m', tgt='x', car=rng.choice(['B', 'out-of-boundary']), law='lin', reads=('y',), k=F(rng.randint(-2, 2), 4), b=F(0)))
                M = S3.FM(sites, (0, 0), T, F(1, 100), {}, mechs)
                h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
            try:
                m1, m2 = S3.d16_mark(M, h), part_mark(M, h)
            except Exception:
                continue
            n += 1
            a = {i: sorted((t, nm) for t, nm in m1[i]) for i in M.T}
            b0 = {i: sorted((t, nm) for t, nm, c in m2[i] if c == 'clause 0') for i in M.T}
            snd_v += (a != b0)                                          # D16's Mark is exactly clause 0 (soundness)
            ef = not any(c == 'clause 1' for i in M.T for (_t, _n, c) in m2[i])
            s1 = any(m1[i] for i in M.T); s2 = any(m2[i] for i in M.T)
            if ef:
                n_ef += 1; eq_ef += (a == {i: sorted((t, nm) for t, nm, _c in m2[i]) for i in M.T})
            else:
                noef += 1; noef_eq += (s1 == s2)
            eqsrc += (s1 == s2)
            iff_v += ((s1 == s2) != ((not s2) or s1))
        stats[label] = (n, snd_v, n_ef, eq_ef, noef, noef_eq, eqsrc, iff_v)
        print(f'  {label}, {n} random cases: D16 Mark identical position by position with participation-type clause 0 (soundness, Theorem D16-SF(1)), violations {snd_v}; '
              f'EF-σ holds in {n_ef} cases, of which Mark identical position by position {eq_ef}; EF-σ fails in {noef} cases, of which Src still takes the same value {noef_eq} (T has other out-of-boundary changes); '
              f'Src same value {eqsrc}/{n}; “Src same value ⇔ (Src^A = 1 ⇒ Src_F = 1)” violations {iff_v}')
    S, _h = GC.load_snn_core()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    P = GK.Instance('', G, [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6 + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    rows = classify4(P, old=False)
    ext_T = sum(1 for t in P.evs for e in P.evs[t].values() if e['car'] == RW.EXT and e['tgt'] in P.T and abs(e['c']) > EPS)
    inj = sum(1 for t in P.evs for e in P.evs[t].values() if e['car'] == RW.EXT)
    print(f'  Frozen SNN Instance P: participation-type certified writes acting on T {sum(1 for r in rows if r["cls"] == "M1₀")}, all with carrier B (PLUS, MINUS); out-of-boundary carrier events {inj} (INJ), '
          f'of which acting on T {ext_T}. Hence EF-σ fails: under any F encoding that assigns carriers faithfully (8.2(3)), D16 gives Src_F(T) = 0 while participation-type r = 1 — '
          'this 0 does not satisfy 8.2(6) and is not read back per 8.4; it is not a misjudgment by D16.')
    print()

# ---------------- 7: frozen SNN parameter grid, 108 groups ----------------
def section_grid(S):
    print('7. q, r and TraceSet on the frozen SNN parameter grid of 108 groups (PS criterion; T = {w00, w10}; declared histories as in the strict subdomain)')
    W0s, APs, Ts, Ns = [F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]
    tab, per = {}, []
    trans = {}
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product(W0s, APs, Ts, Ns):
        S.W0, S.A_PLUS = W0, AP
        G = GC.snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
        I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, len(form) + 8)
        rows = classify4(I)
        rv = r_verdict4(rows)
        oldrv = S3.r_verdict([dict(cls=r['old']) for r in rows])[0]
        end = I.pre[len(hist)]
        q = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and I.form[k] for k in I.T)
        tr = 1 if (q and rv[0] == '1') else (0 if (not q or rv[0] == 0) else '⊥')
        key = (int(q), f'{rv[0]}/{rv[1]}', str(tr))
        tab.setdefault(key, []).append((W0, AP, teach, n))
        trans[(int(q), str(oldrv), str(rv[0]))] = trans.get((int(q), str(oldrv), str(rv[0])), 0) + 1
        per.append(((W0, AP, teach, n), int(q), rv[0], count(rows)))
    S.W0, S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
    for (q, rv, tr), lst in sorted(tab.items()):
        ex = lst[0]
        print(f'  q = {q}, r = {rv}, TraceSet = {tr}: {len(lst)} groups (e.g. W0 = {ex[0]}, A₊ = {ex[1]}, teach {ex[2]}, n = {ex[3]})')
    print('  Strict-subdomain r → PS r (number of groups): ' + '; '.join(f'q = {q}: {a} → {b}: {c}' for (q, a, b), c in sorted(trans.items())))
    print('  Group by group (W0, A₊, teach, n: q; r; classification of the effective events acting on T):')
    for (p, q, r, c) in per:
        print(f'    ({fr(p[0])}, {fr(p[1])}, {fr(p[2])}, {p[3]}): q = {q}; r = {r}; {c}')
    print()
    return per

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    t0 = time.time()
    P, N, out = section_snn(G)
    section_fld(G, out)
    section_aux()
    section_od()
    section_checks()
    section_d16()
    section_grid(S)
    print('End. The above are finite verifications and exact readings; proofs of the general propositions are in Section 5 of the main text.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.15  source\_r5.py  Comparison of the two readings on the separating example, compatibility of D16 with Mark^PS, negative determinations carrying N′ (5.8)

~~~python
"""Source-fidelity condition of D16 (main text 5.8): the verdicts on the separating example under the two route readings compared (N′: 0[N′, EF1′]; Ψ: 1[Ψ]; neither declared: ⊥_N′); check of the compatibility of D16 with the Mark^PS certified in this paper (the fidelity of the complete Mark^A is not checked); negative determinations carry N′ and the other antecedents.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 source_r5.py
"""
import sys, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import source_r3 as S3
import source_r4 as R4
NONE = GK.NONE
EPS = GK.EPS
NEG = 'S⁻ (reading N′; antecedents REC, ENT, R1, EF1′, carried with the conclusion)'

def r_verdict5(rows):
    """As source_r4.r_verdict4, except that a negative determination states the pending antecedents it consumes (N′ and the others); when N′ is undeclared, the negative determination is replaced by ⊥_N′."""
    v, why = R4.r_verdict4(rows)
    if v == 0:
        return '0[N′, EF1′]', NEG
    return v, why

def r_verdict5_noN(rows):
    v, why = R4.r_verdict4(rows)
    if v == 0:
        return '⊥_N′', 'reading N′ undeclared: no certifiable chain, but 0 is not signed'
    return v, why

# ---------------- 1. Re-examining the separating example ----------------
def sep_model():
    copy = dict(name='copy', tgt='y', car='B', law='lin', reads=('x',), k=F(1, 2), b=F(0))
    return S3.FM(('x', 'y'), (0, 0), ('y',), F(0), {'a': [('x', 1)]}, [copy])

def section_sep():
    print('1. Re-examining the separating example (SPR-01): positions x, y, initial state 0, ε = 0, T = {y}; signal a adds 1 to x; the mechanism copy inside B writes x/2 to y at every beat (timing and law fixed, independent of the letter); history (a, rest)')
    M = sep_model()
    h = ['a', 'rest']
    ev, end = M.run(h)
    for t, u, s, evs in ev:
        print(f'  beat {t} (letter {u}), pre-beat x = {s["x"]}, y = {s["y"]}; events: ' +
              '; '.join(f'{e["name"]} ({e["car"]}, acting on {e["tgt"]}, component {GK.fr(e["c"])})' for e in evs))
    print(f'  window end x = {end["x"]}, y = {end["y"]}')
    ev0, end0 = M.run(['rest', 'rest'])
    c1 = [e['c'] for e in ev0[1][3] if e['name'] == 'copy'][0]
    print(f'  signal at beat 0 removed (history (rest, rest)): window end x = {end0["x"]}, y = {end0["y"]}; copy component at beat 1 {GK.fr(c1)} (actual 1/2)')
    d16 = S3.d16_mark(M, h)['y']
    ps = R4.part_mark(M, h)['y']
    print(f'  D16 Mark(y) = {d16}; Mark^PS(y) certified in this paper = {ps} (neither clause 0 nor same-beat letter gating is triggered)')
    # The two route readings
    print('  Effective event acting on T: copy@1 (carrier B, reads x, k = 1/2 ≠ 0). Its source analysis:')
    print('    Actual events: signal a@0 → out-of-boundary pulse on x (effective, x: 0 → 1, leaving a Trace-Response) → copy@1 reads the pre-beat x (nonzero functional support) → 1/2 on y; removing a@0 changes the copy@1 component from 1/2 to 0.')
    print('    Reading N′ (pending verification: a route ends at the Structural Position where it leaves a change, and succeeding actions that read the Trace-Responses already left are continuation): the route of a ends at x; copy@1 has no chain that bypasses Structural Positions and is continuation; '
          'under R1, REC, ENT, EF1′, r({y}) = 0, and the verdict carries N′: ' + r_verdict5([dict(cls='no chain')])[0])
    print('    Reading Ψ (unadjudicated: a route may continue along Trace-Responses already left, and removal comparison certifies that a prior signal is a source of a succeeding change): the chain a@0 → x → copy@1 actually occurs and the removal changes copy@1, '
          'so under the certification format of A-src, r({y}) = 1, and the verdict carries Ψ: 1[Ψ]')
    print('    Neither reading declared: criterion PS of this paper does not certify (no same-beat chain; x is a Structural Position and may not be used for M1_aux), and a negative determination lacks N′: r({y}) = ' + r_verdict5_noN([dict(cls='no chain')])[0])
    print('  Reading: this example shows that “true F, reading only pre-beat Structural Positions” cannot by itself yield EF-σ or Mark_F = Mark^A; D16 and Mark^PS are compatible in this example (both empty), '
          'and whether the complete Mark^A contains copy@1 depends on the open route-termination question (main text U2).')
    print()
    return d16, ps

# ---------------- 2. Compatibility of D16 with Mark^PS ----------------
def section_compat():
    print('2. Compatibility of D16 with the Mark^PS certified in this paper (Theorem D16-SF(3): only compatibility is claimed)')
    for label, seed_, gen in (('F^σ-free (mechanisms inside B without alphabet gating)', 31, 'free'), ('with alphabet-gated write inside B', 32, 'gated')):
        rng = random.Random(seed_)
        n = sub_v = eq = trace_read = 0
        for _ in range(600):
            if gen == 'free':
                sites = ('x', 'y', 'z')
                T = tuple(rng.sample(sites, rng.randint(1, 3)))
                pulses = {a: [(rng.choice(sites), F(rng.randint(0, 3), 2))] for a in ('a', 'b') if rng.random() < 0.7}
                mechs = []
                for k in range(rng.randint(1, 4)):
                    car = rng.choice(['B', 'B', 'out-of-boundary']); law = rng.choice(['const', 'lin'])
                    m = dict(name=f'm{k}', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car=car, law=law,
                             reads=(rng.choice(sites),) if law == 'lin' else (), k=F(rng.randint(-1, 2), 4), b=F(rng.randint(0, 2), 4))
                    if car == 'out-of-boundary' and rng.random() < 0.5:
                        m['off'] = ('rest',)
                    mechs.append(m)
                M = S3.FM(sites, (0, 0, 0), T, F(1, 100), pulses, mechs)
                h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a', 'b']) for _ in range(rng.randint(2, 5))]
            else:
                sites = ('x', 'y'); T = ('x',)
                mechs = [dict(name='g', tgt=rng.choice(sites), car='B', law='const', reads=(), k=F(0), b=F(rng.randint(1, 3), 4), off=('rest',))]
                if rng.random() < 0.5:
                    mechs.append(dict(name='m', tgt='x', car=rng.choice(['B', 'out-of-boundary']), law='lin', reads=('y',), k=F(rng.randint(-2, 2), 4), b=F(0)))
                M = S3.FM(sites, (0, 0), T, F(1, 100), {}, mechs)
                h = [rng.choice(['rest', 'a']) for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 4))]
            try:
                m1, m2 = S3.d16_mark(M, h), R4.part_mark(M, h)
            except Exception:
                continue
            n += 1
            a = {i: set((t, nm) for t, nm in m1[i]) for i in M.T}
            b = {i: set((t, nm) for t, nm, _c in m2[i]) for i in M.T}
            sub_v += any(not a[i] <= b[i] for i in M.T)
            eq += (a == b)
            # Events where “a mechanism inside B reads a Structural Position once acted on by the signal and makes an effective write on T”: here the complete Mark^A depends on the unadjudicated route-termination question
            ev, _ = M.run(h)
            touched = set()
            hit = False
            for t, u, s, evs in ev:
                for e in evs:
                    if e['car'] == 'B' and e['tgt'] in M.T and abs(e['c']) > M.eps and set(e['reads']) & touched:
                        hit = True
                for e in evs:
                    if abs(e['c']) > M.eps and (e['car'] == 'out-of-boundary' or e['tgt'] in touched):
                        touched.add(e['tgt'])
            trace_read += hit
~~~
