        print(f'  {label}, {n} random cases: Mark_F ⊆ Mark^PS violations {sub_v}; Mark_F = Mark^PS in {eq} cases; '
              f'of these, {trace_read} contain “a mechanism inside B reads a Structural Position once acted on by the signal and makes an effective write on T” (on these cases, whether the complete Mark^A equals Mark^PS depends on main text U2 and is not determined by this program)')
    print('  Reading: finite verification of soundness (D16-SF(1)) and compatibility ((3)); the fidelity of the complete Mark^A takes EF-σ as an independent antecedent ((2)) and does not follow from these equality counts.')
    print()

# ---------------- 3. Negative determinations carry N′ ----------------
def section_neg(S):
    print('3. Reprint of the negative determinations (negative determinations carry N′ and the other antecedents; positive determinations are unaffected)')
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    o1 = ([[G.b[i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)], [F(4, 3), F(0)], [F(1), F(1), F(0), F(0)], [F(0), F(0)])
    C1 = GK.Instance('C1', G, [('none', (0, F(1))), NONE, NONE], 1, T=[('w', 0, 0)], o0=o1)
    rows = R4.classify4(C1, old=False)
    print(f'  Contrast C1, T = {{w00}}: r = {r_verdict5(rows)[0]} ({r_verdict5(rows)[1]}); N′ undeclared: {r_verdict5_noN(rows)[0]}')
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    P = GK.Instance('P', G, FORM + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), 26)
    print(f'  Instance P: r = {r_verdict5(R4.classify4(P, old=False))[0]} (M1₀, N′ not involved)')
    neg = []
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)], [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        S.W0, S.A_PLUS = W0, AP
        Gp = GC.snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
        I = GK.Instance('', Gp, form + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0(), len(form) + 8)
        v = r_verdict5(R4.classify4(I, old=False))[0]
        if v not in ('1',):
            neg.append((f'({GK.fr(W0)}, {GK.fr(AP)}, {GK.fr(teach)}, {n})', v))
    S.W0, S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
    print(f'  {len(neg)} of the 108 grid groups have r ≠ 1: ' + '; '.join(f'{a} r = {b}' for a, b in neg) +
          ' (these groups have q = 0, so TraceSet = 0 does not depend on r; the 0 of r carries N′ and EF1′)')
    print()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    section_sep()
    section_compat()
    section_neg(S)
    print('End. For the general propositions see main text 5.8.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.16  grid\_tmax4.py  Part, HDyn, Q\_R, T\_max and Fld on the 108 grid groups (7.5(3))

~~~python
"""q, r, TraceSet, Part, HDyn (listed T = {w00, w10}), Q_R, T_max and Fld for the 108 groups of the frozen SNN parameter grid (main text 7.5(3)).
For groups with T_max ⊋ T, a witness with Q_R = 1 is first sought among the one- and two-element subsets of T_max, then T_max is adjudicated under a time limit; on timeout “not completed” is registered, Fld is pending T_max and is not read as 0.
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 grid_tmax4.py (long-running; the time limits depend on the machine; progress is written to standard error)
"""
import sys, time, itertools, signal
from fractions import Fraction as F
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
import source_r4 as R4
NONE = GK.NONE
EPS = GK.EPS
SUB_S = 150
BUDGET_S = 60

class Timeout(Exception):
    pass

def _alarm(signum, frame):
    raise Timeout()

def main():
    S, h = GC.load_snn_core()
    print(f'snn_core.py SHA-256 = {h} (matches frozen value)')
    signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _alarm)
    rows = []
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        S.W0, S.A_PLUS = W0, AP
        G = GC.snn_gate(S)
        form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
        hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + GC.WIN0()
        I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, len(form) + 8)
        end = I.pre[len(hist)]
        q = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and I.form[k] for k in I.T)
        rv = R4.r_verdict4(R4.classify4(I, old=False))[0]
        tr = 1 if (q and rv == '1') else (0 if (not q or rv == 0) else '⊥')
        lab = f'({GK.fr(W0)}, {GK.fr(AP)}, {GK.fr(teach)}, {n})'
        print(f'  … {lab}', file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
        if tr != 1:
            rows.append((lab, q, rv, tr, '—', '—', '—', 0, '—', 'TraceSet = 0 ⇒ Q_R(T) = 0 (listed T only; Fld is not judged from this)'))
            continue
        D = DC.Decider(I)
        j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
        tier = sorted({str(r['tier']) for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1 and r['v'] != 1}) or ['none (a = 0)']
        Q = GK.k_and(tr, j1['Part'], j1['HDyn'])
        if Q == 1:
            rows.append((lab, q, rv, tr, j1['Part'], f'{j1["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: {j0["HDyn"]})', j0['HDyn'], Q, '—',
                         'Fld = 1 (algorithmic level; Q_R = 1 on the witness T)'))
            continue
        X = [('w', i, jj) for i in range(G.n_in) for jj in range(G.n_out)]
        Iall = GK.Instance('', G, hist, I.t0, T=X)
        Tmax = [k for k in X if GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Iall.form[k]]
        tm = '{' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in Tmax) + '}'
        hd = f'{j1["HDyn"]} (B_FD not declared: {j0["HDyn"]}; tiers of the atoms with a = 1 and η ≠ 1: {", ".join(tier)})'
        if set(Tmax) == set(I.T):
            nW = sum(1 for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T)
            if not any(r['a'] == 1 for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T):
                src0 = f'all {nW} events in W acting on T are ineffective (a = 0, component ≤ ε), so the 0 of HDyn comes from Act, not from the three tiers of η; antecedents MOD-R, RD, REC, ENT, BEAT-R; does not consume B_FD'
            elif j0['HDyn'] == 0:
                src0 = 'the 0 of HDyn comes from tier (a) of η; antecedents MOD-R, R2/R3, REC, ENT, POS, BEAT-R; does not consume B_FD'
            else:
                src0 = 'the 0 of HDyn uses tier (b) of η; antecedents additionally DOM[𝒟₁], B_FD; ⊥_Over when B_FD is not declared'
            why = (f'Fld = 0 (T_max = T, Q_R(T_max) = 0; {src0}; EF1′ also required)') if Q == 0 else f'Fld = ⊥ (Q_R = {Q})'
            rows.append((lab, q, rv, tr, j1['Part'], hd, j0['HDyn'], Q, tm, why))
            continue
        # first seek a witness with Q_R = 1 among the one- and two-element subsets of T_max ([3] 4.7: the existence of a witness suffices, Fld = 1); time limit SUB_S seconds
        wit, nsub, sub_h, sub_done = None, 0, set(), True
        signal.alarm(SUB_S)
        try:
            for rr in (1, 2):
                for Ts in itertools.combinations(Tmax, rr):
                    Is = GK.Instance('', G, hist, I.t0, T=list(Ts))
                    qs = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and Is.form[k] for k in Ts)
                    rs = R4.r_verdict4(R4.classify4(Is, old=False))[0]
                    Ds = DC.Decider(Is)
                    js, js0 = DC.judge(Is, Ds, True), DC.judge(Is, Ds, False)
                    nsub += 1
                    sub_h.add(f'{js["HDyn"]}/{js0["HDyn"]}')
                    if qs and rs == '1' and GK.k_and(1, js['Part'], js['HDyn']) == 1:
                        wit = Ts
                        break
                if wit:
                    break
            signal.alarm(0)
        except Timeout:
            sub_done = False
        if wit:
            rows.append((lab, q, rv, tr, j1['Part'], hd, j0['HDyn'], Q, tm,
                         'Fld = 1 (algorithmic level; Q_R = 1 on the witness T = {' + ','.join(f'w{k[1]}{k[2]}' for k in wit) + '})'))
            continue
        nall = len(Tmax) + len(Tmax) * (len(Tmax) - 1) // 2
        subnote = (f'of the {nall} one- and two-element subsets of T_max, {nsub} adjudicated' + ('' if sub_done else f' (within the {SUB_S} s budget)') +
                   f', no witness with Q_R = 1 (HDyn of the adjudicated subsets, B_FD declared / not declared: {", ".join(sorted(sub_h))}); ')
        t0 = time.time()
        signal.alarm(BUDGET_S)
        try:
            IT = GK.Instance('', G, hist, I.t0, T=Tmax)
            rT = R4.r_verdict4(R4.classify4(IT, old=False))[0]
            qT = all(GK.zk(G, end, k) > EPS and IT.form[k] for k in Tmax)
            DT = DC.Decider(IT)
            jT1, jT0 = DC.judge(IT, DT, True), DC.judge(IT, DT, False)
            signal.alarm(0)
            trT = 1 if (qT and rT == '1') else '⊥'
            QT = GK.k_and(trT, jT1['Part'], jT1['HDyn'])
            QT0 = GK.k_and(trT, jT0['Part'], jT0['HDyn'])
            if QT == 1:
                why = f'Fld = 1 (algorithmic level; Q_R = 1 on the witness T_max; time {time.time() - t0:.0f} s)'
            elif QT == 0:
                why = (f'Fld = 0 (Q_R(T_max) = 0: HDyn(T_max) = {jT1["HDyn"]}, {jT0["HDyn"]} when B_FD is not declared; ' +
                       ('does not consume B_FD' if QT0 == 0 else 'consumes B_FD, ⊥_Over when not declared') + f'; EF1′ also required; time {time.time() - t0:.0f} s)')
            else:
                why = f'Fld = ⊥ (Q_R(T_max) = {QT}; time {time.time() - t0:.0f} s)'
        except Timeout:
            why = f'Fld pending T_max: {subnote}exact adjudication on T_max not completed within the {BUDGET_S} s budget, not read as 0'
        rows.append((lab, q, rv, tr, j1['Part'], hd, j0['HDyn'], Q, tm, why))
    S.W0, S.A_PLUS = F(1, 4), F(1, 8)
    print('Frozen SNN parameter grid, 108 groups: q, r (criterion PS), TraceSet, Part, HDyn (listed T = {w00, w10}), Q_R(T), T_max and Fld')
    cnt = {}
    for (lab, q, rv, tr, P, hd, h0, Q, tm, why) in rows:
        print(f'  {lab}: q = {int(q)}, r = {rv}, TraceSet = {tr}, Part = {P}, HDyn = {hd}, Q_R(T) = {Q}; T_max = {tm}; {why}')
        k = why.split(' (')[0].split(':')[0]
        cnt[k] = cnt.get(k, 0) + 1
    print('Summary: ' + '; '.join(f'{k}: {v} groups' for k, v in sorted(cnt.items())))
    print('Reading: Q_R(T) = 0 holds only for the listed T = {w00, w10}; a 0 for Fld is registered only when Q_R = 0 on T_max (Theorem R7(2), [core] Theorem TM, Proposition M-r); '
          'for groups not completed, Fld is pending T_max and is not read as 0; a group with TraceSet = 0 only shows that the listed T does not satisfy TraceSet.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.17  lp03\_rescan.py  Declared histories and operational readings of the original scan of the frozen SNN (library)

~~~python
"""Declared histories and operational readings of the original frozen-SNN scan (imported by Appendix A.18).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic.
"""
import sys, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
from collections import Counter
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
EPS = GK.EPS
T = [(0, 0), (1, 0)]
NONE = GK.NONE
WIN0 = GC.WIN0()

def original_readings(S, teach, n_pair, gap, win):
    """Word-for-word equivalent in meaning to snn_scan.readings (computed with snn_core)."""
    form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * gap) * n_pair
    ctrl = ([('A', (0, 0))] + [NONE] * gap) * n_pair
    st_f, _ = S.run(form); st_r, _ = S.run([NONE] * 8, st=st_f)
    zf = [S.z(st_f)[i][j] for (i, j) in T]; zc = [S.z(S.run(ctrl)[0])[i][j] for (i, j) in T]
    Fm = all(v > EPS for v in zf) and all(v <= EPS for v in zc)
    erased = dict(S.init(), v=st_r['v'], x=st_r['x'], y=st_r['y'])
    st_w, lw = S.run(win, st=st_r); st_a, la = S.run(win, st=erased)
    sp = lambda log: sum(ev['s_out'][0] for ev in log)
    pos = lambda log: sum(ev['comp'][(i, j)][0] for ev in log for (i, j) in T)
    dz = lambda log: [sum(ev['comp'][(i, j)][3] for ev in log) for (i, j) in T]
    P = sp(lw) > sp(la); H = pos(lw) > pos(la)
    _, lc = S.run(win, st=st_r, bap=False); _, lc0 = S.run(win, st=erased, bap=False)
    C = dz(lc) == dz(lc0)
    R = all(S.z(st_w)[i][j] > EPS for (i, j) in T)
    rd = dict(F=Fm, P=P, H=H, C=C, R=R)
    bad = [k for k in 'FPHCR' if not rd[k]]
    verdict = 'holds' if not bad else 'fails:' + ''.join(bad)
    col1 = dict(sp_w=sp(lw), sp_a=sp(la), pos_w=pos(lw), pos_a=pos(la), zc=zc, zf=zf)
    return verdict, col1

def new_eval(S, teach, n_pair, gap, win):
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * gap) * n_pair
    hist = form + [NONE] * 8 + win
    I = GK.Instance('', G, hist, len(form) + 8)
    ev = GK.evaluate(I)
    th = GK.third_path(I, True)
    q = tuple(int(r and f) for (r, f, _) in ev['q'].values())
    ret = tuple(r for (r, f, _) in ev['q'].values())
    ncov = sum(1 for r in ev['rows'] if r['a'] == 1 and r['eta'] == GK.COV)
    return dict(Part=ev['Part'], HDyn=ev['HDyn'], Part_c=ev['Part_cons'], HDyn_c=ev['HDyn_cons'], q=q, ret=ret,
                n_over=ev['n_over'], ncov=ncov, third=(th['Part'], th['HDyn']), wit=ev['Part_wit'])

def code(ne):
    return (f"Part={ne['Part']} HDyn={ne['HDyn']} q={''.join(map(str, ne['q']))}"
            f" | conservative Part={ne['Part_c']} HDyn={ne['HDyn_c']}")

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    base = {k: getattr(S, k) for k in ['LEAK', 'THETA', 'DEC', 'A_PLUS', 'A_MINUS', 'W0', 'W_MAX']}
    def setp(**kw):
        for k, val in base.items():
            setattr(S, k, kw.get(k, val))
    print('0. Check of the R1(a′) reproduction data (s1; erasure side: weights reset to W0, v, x, y carried over from the end-of-rest state, plasticity on)')
    setp()
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    FORM = [('A', (F(1), 0)), NONE, NONE] * 6
    st_r = S.run([NONE] * 8, st=S.run(FORM)[0])[0]
    o = (S.init()['w'], st_r['v'], st_r['x'], st_r['y'])
    print(f'  window start v0 = {o[1][0]}, y0 = {o[3][0]} (reference values: v₀ = 0, y₀ = 37449/33554432)')
    k = 0
    for t, sig in enumerate(WIN0):
        o2, rec = G.step(o, sig)
        if G.pat[sig[0]] != (0, 0, 0, 0):
            k += 1
            if k == 1:
                print(f'  after the first A beat w00 = {o2[0][0][0]} (< 1/4: {o2[0][0][0] < F(1, 4)}; reference value 268398007/1073741824)')
            if k == 3:
                r = G.lam[0] ** 3
                fixed = 2 * S.W0 * (1 - r ** 3) / (1 - r)
                print(f'  membrane potential after the third input integration {rec["vt"][0]}; fixed-weight formula value {fixed}; difference {rec["vt"][0] - fixed} (reference values 429419959/536870912, 6553/8192, −37449/536870912)')
        o = o2
    print('  Reading: the erasure side is the setting “reset all 8 weights”, not an exclusive LOC that changes only T; moreover, the residual y means the weights have already been rewritten by depression before the first firing. '
          'Hence the erasure-side readings serve only as contrast readings in column 1, not as certification atoms for Part, HDyn (column 2 uses KO, legitimate LOC and the dependence graph instead).')
    print('1. Parameter grid, 108 groups (declared history: formation (A+teach, rest×2)×n, rest 8 beats, W is (A,rest,rest,B,rest,rest)×3)')
    by_orig = {}
    lines = []
    tot_over = tot_cov = 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        setp(W0=W0, A_PLUS=AP)
        v, c1 = original_readings(S, teach, n, 2, WIN0)
        ne = new_eval(S, teach, n, 2, WIN0)
        tot_over += ne['n_over'] > 0; tot_cov += ne['ncov'] > 0
        by_orig.setdefault(v, Counter())[code(ne)] += 1
        lines.append(f'  W0={W0} A+={AP} teach={teach} n={n}: original {v}; col. 1 firing {c1["sp_w"]}/{c1["sp_a"]}, '
                     f'positive-component sum {float(c1["pos_w"]):.4f}/{float(c1["pos_a"]):.4f}, contrast formation z={float(c1["zc"][0]):.4f}; '
                     f'col. 2 Part={ne["Part"]} HDyn={ne["HDyn"]} (conservative {ne["Part_c"]}/{ne["HDyn_c"]}; overdetermined T events {ne["n_over"]}, ⊥_Cov {ne["ncov"]}); '
                     f'col. 3 Ret={"".join(map(str, ne["ret"]))} q={"".join(map(str, ne["q"]))}; Chapter 3 Part/HDyn={ne["third"]}')
    setp()
    print('  Distribution of the original readings and their re-evaluation in the three columns of this paper (principal reading; the “conservative” column records overdetermined points as ⊥_Over):')
    for v in sorted(by_orig):
        print(f'    originally “{v}” {sum(by_orig[v].values())} groups →', '; '.join(f'{k}: {c}' for k, c in sorted(by_orig[v].items())))
    print(f'  groups with overdetermined T events {tot_over}; groups with ⊥_Cov {tot_cov}')
    print('  Records by group:')
    for ln in lines:
        print(ln)
    print('2. Random input family, 200 windows (W0 = 1/4, A_PLUS = 1/8, teach 1, n = 6; seed 20261031, windows drawn as in the original scan)')
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    by_orig2 = {}
    lines2 = []
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (0, 0)) for _ in range(18)]
        v, c1 = original_readings(S, F(1), 6, 2, win)
        ne = new_eval(S, F(1), 6, 2, win)
        by_orig2.setdefault(v, Counter())[code(ne)] += 1
        if v != 'holds':
            nA = sum(1 for s in win if s[0] == 'A')
            lines2.append(f'  window {kk + 1} (A {nA} times in the window): original {v}; col. 1 firing {c1["sp_w"]}/{c1["sp_a"]}, positive-component sum '
                          f'{float(c1["pos_w"]):.4f}/{float(c1["pos_a"]):.4f}; col. 2 Part={ne["Part"]} HDyn={ne["HDyn"]}'
                          f' (conservative {ne["Part_c"]}/{ne["HDyn_c"]}; overdetermined {ne["n_over"]}, ⊥_Cov {ne["ncov"]}; Part witness {ne["wit"][0] if ne["wit"] else "none"}); '
                          f'col. 3 q={"".join(map(str, ne["q"]))}; Chapter 3 {ne["third"]}')
    for v in sorted(by_orig2):
        print(f'    originally “{v}” {sum(by_orig2[v].values())} windows →', '; '.join(f'{k}: {c}' for k, c in sorted(by_orig2[v].items())))
    print('  Records of the failing windows, one by one:')
    for ln in lines2:
        print(ln)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

A.18  lp03\_exact.py  Exact 𝒟₁ re-evaluation of the 108 groups and 200 random windows of the original scan (E-g5, E-g6 of Section 6)

~~~python
"""Re-evaluation of the original frozen-SNN scan (108-group grid, 200 random windows) by exact adjudication on 𝒟₁ (main text Section 6, E-g5, E-g6).
Standard library only, exact rational arithmetic. Run: python3 lp03_exact.py (about one hour)
"""
import sys, random, itertools
from fractions import Fraction as F
from collections import Counter
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import gate_class as GC
import gate_criteria as GK
import decision as DC
import lp03_rescan as L3
NONE = GK.NONE

def ev(S, teach, n, win):
    G = GC.snn_gate(S)
    form = ([('A', (teach, 0))] + [NONE] * 2) * n
    I = GK.Instance('', G, form + [NONE] * 8 + win, len(form) + 8)
    D = DC.Decider(I)
    j1, j0 = DC.judge(I, D, True), DC.judge(I, D, False)
    tiers = Counter(r['tier'] for r in j1['rows'] if r['e']['tgt'] in I.T and r['a'] == 1)
    q = ''.join(str(int(r and f)) for (r, f, _) in GK.evaluate(I)['q'].values()) if False else None
    return j1, j0, tiers, all(D.complete_u.values()), D.verify_fail

def main():
    S, _ = GC.load_snn_core()
    base = {k: getattr(S, k) for k in ['LEAK', 'THETA', 'DEC', 'A_PLUS', 'A_MINUS', 'W0', 'W_MAX']}
    def setp(**kw):
        for k, val in base.items():
            setattr(S, k, kw.get(k, val))
    print('1. Parameter grid, 108 groups (exact adjudication on 𝒟₁; the “B_FD” column declares the functional-dependence correspondence antecedent, the “no B_FD” column does not)')
    cnt = {}
    allc, vf = True, 0
    for W0, AP, teach, n in itertools.product([F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(3, 8), F(1, 2)], [F(1, 16), F(1, 8), F(1, 4)],
                                              [F(1, 2), F(1), F(2)], [1, 3, 6]):
        setp(W0=W0, A_PLUS=AP)
        v, _ = L3.original_readings(S, teach, n, 2, GC.WIN0())
        j1, j0, tiers, comp, f = ev(S, teach, n, GC.WIN0())
        allc &= comp; vf += f
        key = (v, f'Part={j1["Part"]} HDyn={j1["HDyn"]} | no B_FD: Part={j0["Part"]} HDyn={j0["HDyn"]}')
        cnt[key] = cnt.get(key, 0) + 1
    setp()
    for (v, k), c in sorted(cnt.items()):
        print(f'    originally “{v}” → {k}: {c} groups')
    print(f'  all exhausted: {allc}; witness re-run failures: {vf}')
    print('2. Random input family, 200 windows (W0 = 1/4, A₊ = 1/8, teach 1, n = 6; seed 20261031)')
    rng = random.Random(20261031)
    cnt = {}
    allc, vf = True, 0
    for kk in range(200):
        win = [(rng.choice(['A', 'B', 'none', 'none']), (0, 0)) for _ in range(18)]
        v, _ = L3.original_readings(S, F(1), 6, 2, win)
        j1, j0, tiers, comp, f = ev(S, F(1), 6, win)
        allc &= comp; vf += f
        key = (v, f'Part={j1["Part"]} HDyn={j1["HDyn"]} | no B_FD: Part={j0["Part"]} HDyn={j0["HDyn"]}')
        cnt[key] = cnt.get(key, 0) + 1
    for (v, k), c in sorted(cnt.items()):
        print(f'    originally “{v}” → {k}: {c} windows')
    print(f'  all exhausted: {allc}; witness re-run failures: {vf}')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
~~~

&nbsp;&nbsp;N1: potentiation component a = A₊x′₀ = 67146313/536870912 ≈ 0.125070 \> ε: True. For an event-wise F mechanism the component c(v)·\[\|c(v)\| \> ε\] equals a identically on \[w*, hi\]; the polynomial c takes the value a at infinitely many points, so c ≡ a; at v ∈ \[b, w*) then \|c\| = a \> ε and a is booked, whereas the program has no occurrence there—a contradiction.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;N2: jump of the net increment at w\*: J = 33591881/536870912 ≈ 0.062569754; J/ε = 839797025/134217728 ≈ 6.256975, in εℤ: False  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;analytically: J = A₊x′₀ − A₋ (firing adds 1 to y′, so the depression component gains an extra −A₋) = 1/8·1.000558 − 1/16 = 33591881/536870912  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;The linear rescaling s = c·z, ε′ = c·ε leaves J/ε unchanged; hence the conclusion holds for every encoding in which depth is proportional to the Trace-Response.

&nbsp;&nbsp;subdomain tests for this instance: Ω\_F (single-branch, no clipping) True; EF4′ False; NS\_act False; identical trajectories False  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Conclusion: the counterexample satisfies Ω\_F—the only condition on which the equality “E\_F and the host agree beat by beat within 𝒟₁” rests—yet the trajectories differ, so the equality fails. (The counterexample itself is not in EF4′: RELAX00 at beat 1 depends on the setting at beat 0 and does not read the available positions of beat 1; the beat-by-beat agreement equality does not presuppose EF4′.)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Regression 2 (θ = 3 keeps the gating from ever firing; history (rest, A), W = beat 1): host beat 1 z = 3/400 (unavailable), F s = 3/200 (available); Chapter 3 on the F run Part = 1; this paper (host) Part = 1; subdomain tests: Ω\_F True, EF4′ False, NS\_act False  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Reading: Regression 2 is not in EF4′ (SYN00 at beat 1 depends on the setting at beat 0 and does not read the available positions of beat 1), so the subdomain Ω\_F ∩ EF4′ already excludes it; it shows that once the trajectories diverge, “available positions” means different things on the two sides, and the verdict comparison loses its object.

&nbsp;&nbsp;Summary (strict subdomain → PS: number of events): S\*₀ → M1₀: 24; ⊥\_aux → M1₀: 70  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Reading: every former ⊥\_aux event has an actual chain starting from a field-entry component and a same-beat admissible-removal witness (M1₀), so the signal participated in the effective trace-leaving; preset auxiliary positions (the nonzero ones among x, v, y) are listed only as co-participants and do not constitute a negative determination (declared source reading). The former S\*₀ positive cases on the zero auxiliary state are likewise certified by M1₀. No case is changed from ⊥\_aux to 0 or kept at ⊥.  
4\. Overdetermination cases: the signal participates through an actual route, yet no single-item removal changes the write (case by case)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;OD-1 (signal-signal overdetermination): beat 1, letter b (w10 = 1 crosses the threshold alone) + I = 1 (crosses the threshold alone), x0 = 1 left by the entry at beat 0; PLUS00@1 c = 1/8  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;removals one by one: remove teaching current I0@1, entry 1@1: does not occur; remove entry 1@1: unchanged; remove teaching current I0@1: unchanged  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;verdict: M1₀ (removal to none+I=(0) (removed teaching current I0@1, entry 1@1): does not occur) — no single-item removal changes it, while under joint removal it does not occur; the removal domain by definition contains any subset of components, so M1₀ certifies it; proved and closed.  
&nbsp;&nbsp;OD-2 (overdetermination of signal and preset membrane potential): λ = 1, preset v = 1 (λv = 1 = θ, crosses the threshold alone), x = 1, letter none+I = 1/4; PLUS00@0 c = 1/8  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chain: firing γ0 reaches the synapse by backpropagation (occurrence; inputs to γ0: INJ0 (1/4)); same-beat removal of I: the gate stays open (λv ≥ θ) and the write is unchanged; no earlier beat  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;verdict: ⊥\_OD (NESS fails: the subset {I}, which contains I but not λv, does not suffice to cross the threshold; r is not judged 0)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;OD-3 (overdetermination of signal and preset membrane potential): λ = 1, preset v = 1 (λv = 1 = θ, crosses the threshold alone), x = 1, letter none+I = 1; PLUS00@0 c = 1/8  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chain: firing γ0 reaches the synapse by backpropagation (occurrence; inputs to γ0: INJ0 (1)); same-beat removal of I: the gate stays open (λv ≥ θ) and the write is unchanged; no earlier beat  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;verdict: M2 (NESS: signal contribution ('I', 0)=1, the sufficient subset also contains ∅ (sum of negative contributions 0); corresponding antecedent B\_OD)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;OD-4 (same-beat overdetermination, certifiable across beats): λ = 1, v\_reset = θ = 1; at beat 0 A+I=1 fires (v resets to 1), at beat 1 none+I=1/4; PLUS00@1 c = 1/8  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;same-beat removal of I@1: the gate stays open (λv = 1); verdict: M1\_aux (beat 0: removal to none+I=(0) (removed teaching current I0@0, entry 0@0), rerun with Trace-Response clamping: does not occur; the route runs through the auxiliary positions v, x and requires EF1′)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;Reading: overdetermination falls into three classes. Overdetermination between signals is certified by joint removal (M1₀); same-beat overdetermination by in-boundary contributions, where a signal at an earlier beat determines that in-boundary contributor through auxiliary positions, is certified by removal with Trace-Response clamping (M1\_aux, requires EF1′); the rest are certified by NESS (M2, requires B\_OD) or recorded as ⊥\_OD. ⊥\_OD means only that the certification methods of this paper are insufficient; it is not 0.

End. The above are finite verifications and exact readings; proofs of the general propositions are in Section 5 of the main text.
